{"line": 68, "text": "    h = hashlib.sha256()"}
{"line": 69, "text": ""}
{"line": 70, "text": "    with path.open(\"rb\") as f:"}

{"line": 73, "text": ""}
{"line": 74, "text": "            if not block:"}
{"line": 75, "text": "                break"}
{"line": 76, "text": ""}
{"line": 77, "text": "            h.update(block)"}
{"line": 78, "text": ""}
{"line": 79, "text": "    return h.hexdigest()"}
{"line": 80, "text": ""}
{"line": 81, "text": ""}

{"line": 123, "text": ""}
{"line": 124, "text": "@dataclass"}
{"line": 125, "text": "class CommandResult:"}
{"line": 126, "text": "    command: str"}
{"line": 127, "text": "    cwd: str"}
{"line": 128, "text": "    returncode: int"}
{"line": 129, "text": "    stdout: str"}
{"line": 130, "text": "    stderr: str"}
{"line": 131, "text": "    duration: float"}
{"line": 132, "text": "    timed_out: bool = False"}
{"line": 133, "text": ""}
{"line": 134, "text": "    @property"}
{"line": 135, "text": "    def success(self) -> bool:"}
{"line": 136, "text": "        return ("}
{"line": 137, "text": "            self.returncode == 0"}
{"line": 138, "text": "            and not self.timed_out"}
{"line": 139, "text": "        )"}
{"line": 140, "text": ""}
{"line": 141, "text": "    def as_dict(self):"}
{"line": 142, "text": "        return asdict(self)"}
{"line": 143, "text": ""}
{"line": 144, "text": ""}
{"line": 145, "text": "class AutonomousTerminal:"}
{"line": 146, "text": ""}
{"line": 147, "text": "    def __init__(self, root: Path = ROOT):"}
{"line": 148, "text": "        self.root = Path(root).resolve()"}
{"line": 149, "text": ""}
{"line": 150, "text": "        self.root.mkdir("}
{"line": 151, "text": "            parents=True,"}
{"line": 152, "text": "            exist_ok=True,"}
{"line": 153, "text": "        )"}
{"line": 154, "text": ""}
{"line": 155, "text": "    def snapshot(self) -> dict[str, Any]:"}
{"line": 156, "text": "        result = {}"}
{"line": 157, "text": ""}
{"line": 158, "text": "        for path in self.root.rglob(\"*\"):"}
{"line": 159, "text": ""}
{"line": 160, "text": "            if not path.is_file():"}
{"line": 161, "text": "                continue"}
{"line": 162, "text": ""}
{"line": 163, "text": "            if BACKUP_DIR in path.parents:"}
{"line": 164, "text": "                continue"}
{"line": 165, "text": ""}
{"line": 166, "text": "            try:"}
{"line": 167, "text": "                raw = path.read_bytes()"}
{"line": 168, "text": ""}
{"line": 169, "text": "                rel = path.relative_to("}
{"line": 170, "text": "                    self.root"}
{"line": 171, "text": "                )"}
{"line": 172, "text": ""}
{"line": 173, "text": "                result[str(rel)] = {"}
{"line": 174, "text": "                    \"bytes\": len(raw),"}
{"line": 175, "text": "                    \"sha256\": sha256_bytes(raw),"}
{"line": 176, "text": "                }"}
{"line": 177, "text": ""}
{"line": 178, "text": "            except Exception:"}
{"line": 179, "text": "                pass"}
{"line": 180, "text": ""}
{"line": 181, "text": "        return result"}
{"line": 182, "text": ""}
{"line": 183, "text": "    def run("}
{"line": 184, "text": "        self,"}
{"line": 185, "text": "        command: str,"}
{"line": 186, "text": "        timeout: int = 120,"}
{"line": 187, "text": "        cwd: Path | None = None,"}
{"line": 188, "text": "    ) -> CommandResult:"}
{"line": 189, "text": ""}
{"line": 190, "text": "        cwd = ("}
{"line": 191, "text": "            self.root"}
{"line": 192, "text": "            if cwd is None"}
{"line": 193, "text": "            else Path(cwd).resolve()"}
{"line": 194, "text": "        )"}
{"line": 195, "text": ""}
{"line": 196, "text": "        if ("}
{"line": 197, "text": "            cwd != self.root"}
{"line": 198, "text": "            and self.root not in cwd.parents"}
{"line": 199, "text": "        ):"}
{"line": 200, "text": "            raise ValueError("}
{"line": 201, "text": "                \"Terminal escaped BLOOM root.\""}
{"line": 202, "text": "            )"}
{"line": 203, "text": ""}
{"line": 204, "text": "        before = self.snapshot()"}
{"line": 205, "text": "        started = now()"}
{"line": 206, "text": ""}
{"line": 207, "text": "        try:"}
{"line": 208, "text": ""}
{"line": 209, "text": "            proc = subprocess.run("}
{"line": 210, "text": "                command,"}
{"line": 211, "text": "                shell=True,"}
{"line": 212, "text": "                cwd=str(cwd),"}
{"line": 213, "text": "                text=True,"}
{"line": 214, "text": "                capture_output=True,"}
{"line": 215, "text": "                timeout=timeout,"}
{"line": 216, "text": "            )"}
{"line": 217, "text": ""}
{"line": 218, "text": "            result = CommandResult("}
{"line": 219, "text": "                command=command,"}
{"line": 220, "text": "                cwd=str(cwd),"}
{"line": 221, "text": "                returncode=proc.returncode,"}
{"line": 222, "text": "                stdout=proc.stdout or \"\","}
{"line": 223, "text": "                stderr=proc.stderr or \"\","}
{"line": 224, "text": "                duration=now() - started,"}
{"line": 225, "text": "            )"}
{"line": 226, "text": ""}
{"line": 227, "text": "        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as exc:"}
{"line": 228, "text": ""}
{"line": 229, "text": "            stdout = exc.stdout or \"\""}
{"line": 230, "text": "            stderr = exc.stderr or \"\""}
{"line": 231, "text": ""}
{"line": 232, "text": "            if isinstance(stdout, bytes):"}
{"line": 233, "text": "                stdout = stdout.decode("}
{"line": 234, "text": "                    \"utf-8\","}
{"line": 235, "text": "                    \"replace\","}
{"line": 236, "text": "                )"}
{"line": 237, "text": ""}
{"line": 238, "text": "            if isinstance(stderr, bytes):"}
{"line": 239, "text": "                stderr = stderr.decode("}
{"line": 240, "text": "                    \"utf-8\","}
{"line": 241, "text": "                    \"replace\","}
{"line": 242, "text": "                )"}
{"line": 243, "text": ""}
{"line": 244, "text": "            result = CommandResult("}
{"line": 245, "text": "                command=command,"}
{"line": 246, "text": "                cwd=str(cwd),"}
{"line": 247, "text": "                returncode=-1,"}
{"line": 248, "text": "                stdout=stdout,"}
{"line": 249, "text": "                stderr=stderr,"}
{"line": 250, "text": "                duration=now() - started,"}
{"line": 251, "text": "                timed_out=True,"}
{"line": 252, "text": "            )"}
{"line": 253, "text": ""}
{"line": 254, "text": "        after = self.snapshot()"}
{"line": 255, "text": ""}
{"line": 256, "text": "        changed = {}"}
{"line": 257, "text": ""}
{"line": 258, "text": "        for path in sorted("}
{"line": 259, "text": "            set(before) | set(after)"}
{"line": 260, "text": "        ):"}
{"line": 261, "text": ""}
{"line": 262, "text": "            if before.get(path) != after.get(path):"}
{"line": 263, "text": ""}
{"line": 264, "text": "                changed[path] = {"}
{"line": 265, "text": "                    \"before\": before.get(path),"}
{"line": 266, "text": "                    \"after\": after.get(path),"}
{"line": 267, "text": "                }"}
{"line": 268, "text": ""}
{"line": 269, "text": "        record_ledger({"}
{"line": 270, "text": "            \"timestamp\": now(),"}
{"line": 271, "text": "            \"type\": \"terminal_execution\","}
{"line": 272, "text": "            \"command\": command,"}
{"line": 273, "text": "            \"cwd\": str(cwd),"}
{"line": 274, "text": "            \"result\": result.as_dict(),"}
{"line": 275, "text": "            \"changed_files\": changed,"}
{"line": 276, "text": "        })"}
{"line": 277, "text": ""}
{"line": 278, "text": "        return result"}
{"line": 279, "text": ""}
{"line": 280, "text": ""}
{"line": 281, "text": "# ============================================================"}
{"line": 282, "text": "# SOURCE INTROSPECTION"}
{"line": 283, "text": "# ============================================================"}
{"line": 284, "text": ""}
{"line": 285, "text": "class SourceIntrospector:"}
{"line": 286, "text": ""}
{"line": 287, "text": "    def __init__(self, model: Path = MODEL):"}
{"line": 288, "text": "        self.model = Path(model)"}
{"line": 289, "text": ""}
{"line": 290, "text": "        self.source = \"\""}
{"line": 291, "text": "        self.tree: ast.AST | None = None"}
{"line": 292, "text": ""}
{"line": 293, "text": "        self.definitions = {}"}
{"line": 294, "text": "        self.references = {}"}
{"line": 295, "text": "        self.functions = {}"}
{"line": 296, "text": "        self.classes = {}"}
{"line": 297, "text": "        self.imports = set()"}
{"line": 298, "text": ""}
{"line": 299, "text": "        self.assignments = {}"}
{"line": 300, "text": "        self.calls = []"}
{"line": 301, "text": "        self.top_level_order = []"}
{"line": 302, "text": ""}
{"line": 303, "text": "    def load(self):"}
{"line": 304, "text": "        if not self.model.exists():"}
{"line": 305, "text": "            raise FileNotFoundError("}
{"line": 306, "text": "                f\"Missing model: {self.model}\""}
{"line": 307, "text": "            )"}
{"line": 308, "text": ""}
{"line": 309, "text": "        self.source = self.model.read_text("}
{"line": 310, "text": "            encoding=\"utf-8\","}
{"line": 311, "text": "            errors=\"replace\","}
{"line": 312, "text": "        )"}
{"line": 313, "text": ""}
{"line": 314, "text": "        self.tree = ast.parse("}
{"line": 315, "text": "            self.source"}
{"line": 316, "text": "        )"}
{"line": 317, "text": ""}
{"line": 318, "text": "        self.inspect()"}
{"line": 319, "text": ""}
{"line": 320, "text": "        return self"}
{"line": 321, "text": ""}
{"line": 322, "text": "    def _target_names(self, target):"}
{"line": 323, "text": "        names = []"}
{"line": 324, "text": ""}
{"line": 325, "text": "        if isinstance(target, ast.Name):"}
{"line": 326, "text": "            names.append(target.id)"}
{"line": 327, "text": ""}
{"line": 328, "text": "        elif isinstance("}
{"line": 329, "text": "            target,"}
{"line": 330, "text": "            (ast.Tuple, ast.List),"}
{"line": 331, "text": "        ):"}
{"line": 332, "text": "            for item in target.elts:"}
{"line": 333, "text": "                names.extend("}
{"line": 334, "text": "                    self._target_names(item)"}
{"line": 335, "text": "                )"}
{"line": 336, "text": ""}
{"line": 337, "text": "        elif isinstance(target, ast.Starred):"}
{"line": 338, "text": "            names.extend("}
{"line": 339, "text": "                self._target_names("}
{"line": 340, "text": "                    target.value"}
{"line": 341, "text": "                )"}
{"line": 342, "text": "            )"}
{"line": 343, "text": ""}
{"line": 344, "text": "        elif isinstance(target, ast.Attribute):"}
{"line": 345, "text": "            pass"}
{"line": 346, "text": ""}
{"line": 347, "text": "        elif isinstance(target, ast.Subscript):"}
{"line": 348, "text": "            pass"}
{"line": 349, "text": ""}
{"line": 350, "text": "        return names"}
{"line": 351, "text": ""}
{"line": 352, "text": "    def _expr_names(self, node):"}
{"line": 353, "text": "        result = []"}
{"line": 354, "text": ""}
{"line": 355, "text": "        for child in ast.walk(node):"}
{"line": 356, "text": ""}
{"line": 357, "text": "            if isinstance(child, ast.Name):"}
{"line": 358, "text": "                result.append(child.id)"}
{"line": 359, "text": ""}
{"line": 360, "text": "        return sorted(set(result))"}
{"line": 361, "text": ""}
{"line": 362, "text": "    def _call_name(self, node):"}
{"line": 363, "text": "        if not isinstance(node, ast.Call):"}
{"line": 364, "text": "            return None"}
{"line": 365, "text": ""}
{"line": 366, "text": "        func = node.func"}
{"line": 367, "text": ""}
{"line": 368, "text": "        if isinstance(func, ast.Name):"}
{"line": 369, "text": "            return func.id"}
{"line": 370, "text": ""}
{"line": 371, "text": "        if isinstance(func, ast.Attribute):"}
{"line": 372, "text": ""}
{"line": 373, "text": "            parts = []"}
{"line": 374, "text": "            cur = func"}
{"line": 375, "text": ""}
{"line": 376, "text": "            while isinstance(cur, ast.Attribute):"}
{"line": 377, "text": "                parts.append(cur.attr)"}
{"line": 378, "text": "                cur = cur.value"}
{"line": 379, "text": ""}
{"line": 380, "text": "            if isinstance(cur, ast.Name):"}
{"line": 381, "text": "                parts.append(cur.id)"}
{"line": 382, "text": ""}
{"line": 383, "text": "            return \".\".join(reversed(parts))"}
{"line": 384, "text": ""}
{"line": 385, "text": "        return None"}
{"line": 386, "text": ""}
{"line": 387, "text": "    def inspect(self):"}
{"line": 388, "text": ""}
{"line": 389, "text": "        if self.tree is None:"}
{"line": 390, "text": "            raise RuntimeError("}
{"line": 391, "text": "                \"AST has not been loaded.\""}
{"line": 392, "text": "            )"}
{"line": 393, "text": ""}
{"line": 394, "text": "        self.definitions.clear()"}
{"line": 395, "text": "        self.references.clear()"}
{"line": 396, "text": "        self.functions.clear()"}
{"line": 397, "text": "        self.classes.clear()"}
{"line": 398, "text": "        self.imports.clear()"}
{"line": 399, "text": "        self.assignments.clear()"}
{"line": 400, "text": "        self.calls.clear()"}
{"line": 401, "text": "        self.top_level_order.clear()"}
{"line": 402, "text": ""}
{"line": 403, "text": "        builtin_names = set("}
{"line": 404, "text": "            dir(__builtins__)"}
{"line": 405, "text": "        )"}
{"line": 406, "text": ""}
{"line": 407, "text": "        # ----------------------------------------------------"}
{"line": 408, "text": "        # IMPORTS"}
{"line": 409, "text": "        # ----------------------------------------------------"}
{"line": 410, "text": ""}
{"line": 411, "text": "        for node in ast.walk(self.tree):"}
{"line": 412, "text": ""}
{"line": 413, "text": "            if isinstance(node, ast.Import):"}
{"line": 414, "text": ""}
{"line": 415, "text": "                for alias in node.names:"}
{"line": 416, "text": "                    self.imports.add("}
{"line": 417, "text": "                        alias.asname"}
{"line": 418, "text": "                        or alias.name.split(\".\")[0]"}
{"line": 419, "text": "                    )"}
{"line": 420, "text": ""}
{"line": 421, "text": "            elif isinstance("}
{"line": 422, "text": "                node,"}
{"line": 423, "text": "                ast.ImportFrom,"}
{"line": 424, "text": "            ):"}
{"line": 425, "text": ""}
{"line": 426, "text": "                for alias in node.names:"}
{"line": 427, "text": "                    self.imports.add("}
{"line": 428, "text": "                        alias.asname"}
{"line": 429, "text": "                        or alias.name"}
{"line": 430, "text": "                    )"}
{"line": 431, "text": ""}
{"line": 432, "text": "        # ----------------------------------------------------"}
{"line": 433, "text": "        # TOP LEVEL DEFINITIONS"}
{"line": 434, "text": "        # ----------------------------------------------------"}
{"line": 435, "text": ""}
{"line": 436, "text": "        for index, node in enumerate("}
{"line": 437, "text": "            self.tree.body,"}
{"line": 438, "text": "            start=1,"}
{"line": 439, "text": "        ):"}
{"line": 440, "text": ""}
{"line": 441, "text": "            self.top_level_order.append({"}
{"line": 442, "text": "                \"index\": index,"}
{"line": 443, "text": "                \"line\": getattr("}
{"line": 444, "text": "                    node,"}
{"line": 445, "text": "                    \"lineno\","}
{"line": 446, "text": "                    None,"}
{"line": 447, "text": "                ),"}
{"line": 448, "text": "                \"type\": type(node).__name__,"}
{"line": 449, "text": "                \"source\": ast.get_source_segment("}
{"line": 450, "text": "                    self.source,"}
{"line": 451, "text": "                    node,"}
{"line": 452, "text": "                ),"}
{"line": 453, "text": "            })"}
{"line": 454, "text": ""}
{"line": 455, "text": "            if isinstance("}
{"line": 456, "text": "                node,"}
{"line": 457, "text": "                ast.Assign,"}
{"line": 458, "text": "            ):"}
{"line": 459, "text": ""}
{"line": 460, "text": "                names = []"}
{"line": 461, "text": ""}
{"line": 462, "text": "                for target in node.targets:"}
{"line": 463, "text": "                    names.extend("}
{"line": 464, "text": "                        self._target_names(target)"}
{"line": 465, "text": "                    )"}
{"line": 466, "text": ""}
{"line": 467, "text": "                for name in names:"}
{"line": 468, "text": ""}
{"line": 469, "text": "                    self.definitions[name] = {"}
{"line": 470, "text": "                        \"line\": node.lineno,"}
{"line": 471, "text": "                        \"kind\": \"assignment\","}
{"line": 472, "text": "                    }"}
{"line": 473, "text": ""}
{"line": 474, "text": "                    self.assignments.setdefault("}
{"line": 475, "text": "                        name,"}
{"line": 476, "text": "                        [],"}
{"line": 477, "text": "                    ).append({"}
{"line": 478, "text": "                        \"line\": node.lineno,"}
{"line": 479, "text": "                        \"end_line\": getattr("}
{"line": 480, "text": "                            node,"}
{"line": 481, "text": "                            \"end_lineno\","}
{"line": 482, "text": "                            node.lineno,"}
{"line": 483, "text": "                        ),"}
{"line": 484, "text": "                        \"kind\": \"Assign\","}
{"line": 485, "text": "                        \"targets\": names,"}
{"line": 486, "text": "                        \"reads\": self._expr_names("}
{"line": 487, "text": "                            node.value"}
{"line": 488, "text": "                        ),"}
{"line": 489, "text": "                        \"source\": ast.get_source_segment("}
{"line": 490, "text": "                            self.source,"}
{"line": 491, "text": "                            node,"}
{"line": 492, "text": "                        ),"}
{"line": 493, "text": "                    })"}
{"line": 494, "text": ""}
{"line": 495, "text": "            elif isinstance("}
{"line": 496, "text": "                node,"}
{"line": 497, "text": "                ast.AnnAssign,"}
{"line": 498, "text": "            ):"}
{"line": 499, "text": ""}
{"line": 500, "text": "                names = self._target_names("}
{"line": 501, "text": "                    node.target"}
{"line": 502, "text": "                )"}
{"line": 503, "text": ""}
{"line": 504, "text": "                for name in names:"}
{"line": 505, "text": ""}
{"line": 506, "text": "                    self.definitions[name] = {"}
{"line": 507, "text": "                        \"line\": node.lineno,"}
{"line": 508, "text": "                        \"kind\": \"annotated_assignment\","}
{"line": 509, "text": "                    }"}
{"line": 510, "text": ""}
{"line": 511, "text": "                    self.assignments.setdefault("}
{"line": 512, "text": "                        name,"}
{"line": 513, "text": "                        [],"}
{"line": 514, "text": "                    ).append({"}
{"line": 515, "text": "                        \"line\": node.lineno,"}
{"line": 516, "text": "                        \"end_line\": getattr("}
{"line": 517, "text": "                            node,"}
{"line": 518, "text": "                            \"end_lineno\","}
{"line": 519, "text": "                            node.lineno,"}
{"line": 520, "text": "                        ),"}
{"line": 521, "text": "                        \"kind\": \"AnnAssign\","}
{"line": 522, "text": "                        \"targets\": names,"}
{"line": 523, "text": "                        \"reads\": ("}
{"line": 524, "text": "                            self._expr_names("}
{"line": 525, "text": "                                node.value"}
{"line": 526, "text": "                            )"}
{"line": 527, "text": "                            if node.value"}
{"line": 528, "text": "                            else []"}
{"line": 529, "text": "                        ),"}
{"line": 530, "text": "                        \"source\": ast.get_source_segment("}
{"line": 531, "text": "                            self.source,"}
{"line": 532, "text": "                            node,"}
{"line": 533, "text": "                        ),"}
{"line": 534, "text": "                    })"}
{"line": 535, "text": ""}
{"line": 536, "text": "            elif isinstance("}
{"line": 537, "text": "                node,"}
{"line": 538, "text": "                ("}
{"line": 539, "text": "                    ast.FunctionDef,"}
{"line": 540, "text": "                    ast.AsyncFunctionDef,"}
{"line": 541, "text": "                    ast.ClassDef,"}
{"line": 542, "text": "                ),"}
{"line": 543, "text": "            ):"}
{"line": 544, "text": ""}
{"line": 545, "text": "                self.definitions[node.name] = {"}
{"line": 546, "text": "                    \"line\": node.lineno,"}
{"line": 547, "text": "                    \"kind\": type(node).__name__,"}
{"line": 548, "text": "                }"}
{"line": 549, "text": ""}
{"line": 550, "text": "        # ----------------------------------------------------"}
{"line": 551, "text": "        # FUNCTIONS / CLASSES / CALLS / REFERENCES"}
{"line": 552, "text": "        # ----------------------------------------------------"}
{"line": 553, "text": ""}
{"line": 554, "text": "        for node in ast.walk(self.tree):"}
{"line": 555, "text": ""}
{"line": 556, "text": "            if isinstance("}
{"line": 557, "text": "                node,"}
{"line": 558, "text": "                ("}
{"line": 559, "text": "                    ast.FunctionDef,"}
{"line": 560, "text": "                    ast.AsyncFunctionDef,"}
{"line": 561, "text": "                ),"}
{"line": 562, "text": "            ):"}
{"line": 563, "text": ""}
{"line": 564, "text": "                self.functions[node.name] = {"}
{"line": 565, "text": "                    \"line\": node.lineno,"}
{"line": 566, "text": "                    \"end_line\": getattr("}
{"line": 567, "text": "                        node,"}
{"line": 568, "text": "                        \"end_lineno\","}
{"line": 569, "text": "                        node.lineno,"}
{"line": 570, "text": "                    ),"}
{"line": 571, "text": "                }"}
{"line": 572, "text": ""}
{"line": 573, "text": "            elif isinstance("}
{"line": 574, "text": "                node,"}
{"line": 575, "text": "                ast.ClassDef,"}
{"line": 576, "text": "            ):"}
{"line": 577, "text": ""}
{"line": 578, "text": "                self.classes[node.name] = {"}
{"line": 579, "text": "                    \"line\": node.lineno,"}
{"line": 580, "text": "                    \"end_line\": getattr("}
{"line": 581, "text": "                        node,"}
{"line": 582, "text": "                        \"end_lineno\","}
{"line": 583, "text": "                        node.lineno,"}
{"line": 584, "text": "                    ),"}
{"line": 585, "text": "                }"}
{"line": 586, "text": ""}
{"line": 587, "text": "            elif isinstance("}
{"line": 588, "text": "                node,"}
{"line": 589, "text": "                ast.Call,"}
{"line": 590, "text": "            ):"}
{"line": 591, "text": ""}
{"line": 592, "text": "                name = self._call_name(node)"}
{"line": 593, "text": ""}
{"line": 594, "text": "                if name:"}
{"line": 595, "text": ""}
{"line": 596, "text": "                    self.calls.append({"}
{"line": 597, "text": "                        \"name\": name,"}
{"line": 598, "text": "                        \"line\": node.lineno,"}
{"line": 599, "text": "                        \"args\": ["}
{"line": 600, "text": "                            ast.get_source_segment("}
{"line": 601, "text": "                                self.source,"}
{"line": 602, "text": "                                arg,"}
{"line": 603, "text": "                            )"}
{"line": 604, "text": "                            for arg in node.args"}
{"line": 605, "text": "                        ],"}
{"line": 606, "text": "                    })"}
{"line": 607, "text": ""}
{"line": 608, "text": "            elif isinstance("}
{"line": 609, "text": "                node,"}
{"line": 610, "text": "                ast.Name,"}
{"line": 611, "text": "            ):"}
{"line": 612, "text": ""}
{"line": 613, "text": "                if not isinstance("}
{"line": 614, "text": "                    node.ctx,"}
{"line": 615, "text": "                    ast.Load,"}
{"line": 616, "text": "                ):"}
{"line": 617, "text": "                    continue"}
{"line": 618, "text": ""}
{"line": 619, "text": "                self.references.setdefault("}
{"line": 620, "text": "                    node.id,"}
{"line": 621, "text": "                    [],"}
{"line": 622, "text": "                ).append("}
{"line": 623, "text": "                    node.lineno"}
{"line": 624, "text": "                )"}
{"line": 625, "text": ""}
{"line": 626, "text": "    def source_segment(self, name: str):"}
{"line": 627, "text": ""}
{"line": 628, "text": "        if self.tree is None:"}
{"line": 629, "text": "            return None"}
{"line": 630, "text": ""}
{"line": 631, "text": "        for node in ast.walk(self.tree):"}
{"line": 632, "text": ""}
{"line": 633, "text": "            if isinstance("}
{"line": 634, "text": "                node,"}
{"line": 635, "text": "                ("}
{"line": 636, "text": "                    ast.FunctionDef,"}
{"line": 637, "text": "                    ast.AsyncFunctionDef,"}
{"line": 638, "text": "                    ast.ClassDef,"}
{"line": 639, "text": "                ),"}
{"line": 640, "text": "            ):"}
{"line": 641, "text": ""}
{"line": 642, "text": "                if node.name == name:"}
{"line": 643, "text": "                    return ast.get_source_segment("}
{"line": 644, "text": "                        self.source,"}
{"line": 645, "text": "                        node,"}
{"line": 646, "text": "                    )"}
{"line": 647, "text": ""}
{"line": 648, "text": "        return None"}
{"line": 649, "text": ""}
{"line": 650, "text": "    def line_context("}
{"line": 651, "text": "        self,"}
{"line": 652, "text": "        line: int,"}
{"line": 653, "text": "        radius: int = 15,"}
{"line": 654, "text": "    ):"}
{"line": 655, "text": ""}
{"line": 656, "text": "        lines = self.source.splitlines()"}
{"line": 657, "text": ""}
{"line": 658, "text": "        start = max("}
{"line": 659, "text": "            1,"}
{"line": 660, "text": "            line - radius,"}
{"line": 661, "text": "        )"}
{"line": 662, "text": ""}
{"line": 663, "text": "        end = min("}
{"line": 664, "text": "            len(lines),"}
{"line": 665, "text": "            line + radius,"}
{"line": 666, "text": "        )"}
{"line": 667, "text": ""}
{"line": 668, "text": "        return {"}
{"line": 669, "text": "            \"start_line\": start,"}
{"line": 670, "text": "            \"end_line\": end,"}
{"line": 671, "text": "            \"lines\": ["}
{"line": 672, "text": "                {"}
{"line": 673, "text": "                    \"line\": i,"}
{"line": 674, "text": "                    \"text\": lines[i - 1],"}
{"line": 675, "text": "                }"}
{"line": 676, "text": "                for i in range("}
{"line": 677, "text": "                    start,"}
{"line": 678, "text": "                    end + 1,"}
{"line": 679, "text": "                )"}
{"line": 680, "text": "            ],"}
{"line": 681, "text": "        }"}
{"line": 682, "text": ""}
{"line": 683, "text": "    def symbol_forensics("}
{"line": 684, "text": "        self,"}
{"line": 685, "text": "        name: str,"}
{"line": 686, "text": "    ):"}
{"line": 687, "text": ""}
{"line": 688, "text": "        return {"}
{"line": 689, "text": "            \"symbol\": name,"}
{"line": 690, "text": "            \"definition\": self.definitions.get("}
{"line": 691, "text": "                name"}
{"line": 692, "text": "            ),"}
{"line": 693, "text": "            \"references\": self.references.get("}
{"line": 694, "text": "                name,"}
{"line": 695, "text": "                [],"}
{"line": 696, "text": "            ),"}
{"line": 697, "text": "            \"assignments\": self.assignments.get("}
{"line": 698, "text": "                name,"}
{"line": 699, "text": "                [],"}
{"line": 700, "text": "            ),"}
{"line": 701, "text": "            \"function\": self.functions.get("}
{"line": 702, "text": "                name"}
{"line": 703, "text": "            ),"}
{"line": 704, "text": "            \"class\": self.classes.get("}
{"line": 705, "text": "                name"}
{"line": 706, "text": "            ),"}
{"line": 707, "text": "        }"}
{"line": 708, "text": ""}
{"line": 709, "text": "    def unresolved_references(self):"}
{"line": 710, "text": ""}
{"line": 711, "text": "        builtin_names = set("}
{"line": 712, "text": "            dir(__builtins__)"}
{"line": 713, "text": "        )"}
{"line": 714, "text": ""}
{"line": 715, "text": "        result = {}"}
{"line": 716, "text": ""}
{"line": 717, "text": "        for name, lines in self.references.items():"}
{"line": 718, "text": ""}
{"line": 719, "text": "            if name in self.definitions:"}
{"line": 720, "text": "                continue"}
{"line": 721, "text": ""}
{"line": 722, "text": "            if name in self.imports:"}
{"line": 723, "text": "                continue"}
{"line": 724, "text": ""}
{"line": 725, "text": "            if name in builtin_names:"}
{"line": 726, "text": "                continue"}
{"line": 727, "text": ""}
{"line": 728, "text": "            result[name] = sorted("}
{"line": 729, "text": "                set(lines)"}
{"line": 730, "text": "            )"}
{"line": 731, "text": ""}
{"line": 732, "text": "        return result"}
{"line": 733, "text": ""}
{"line": 734, "text": "    def dependency_graph(self):"}
{"line": 735, "text": ""}
{"line": 736, "text": "        graph = {}"}
{"line": 737, "text": ""}
{"line": 738, "text": "        for name in self.functions:"}
{"line": 739, "text": ""}
{"line": 740, "text": "            segment = self.source_segment(name)"}
{"line": 741, "text": ""}
{"line": 742, "text": "            if not segment:"}
{"line": 743, "text": "                continue"}
{"line": 744, "text": ""}
{"line": 745, "text": "            try:"}
{"line": 746, "text": "                tree = ast.parse(segment)"}
{"line": 747, "text": "            except Exception:"}
{"line": 748, "text": "                continue"}
{"line": 749, "text": ""}
{"line": 750, "text": "            reads = sorted({"}
{"line": 751, "text": "                node.id"}
{"line": 752, "text": "                for node in ast.walk(tree)"}
{"line": 753, "text": "                if ("}
{"line": 754, "text": "                    isinstance(node, ast.Name)"}
{"line": 755, "text": "                    and isinstance("}
{"line": 756, "text": "                        node.ctx,"}
{"line": 757, "text": "                        ast.Load,"}
{"line": 758, "text": "                    )"}
{"line": 759, "text": "                )"}
{"line": 760, "text": "            })"}
{"line": 761, "text": ""}
{"line": 762, "text": "            graph[name] = {"}
{"line": 763, "text": "                \"reads\": reads,"}
{"line": 764, "text": "                \"undefined_reads\": ["}
{"line": 765, "text": "                    x"}
{"line": 766, "text": "                    for x in reads"}
{"line": 767, "text": "                    if ("}
{"line": 768, "text": "                        x not in self.definitions"}
{"line": 769, "text": "                        and x not in self.imports"}
{"line": 770, "text": "                        and x not in dir(__builtins__)"}
{"line": 771, "text": "                    )"}
{"line": 772, "text": "                ],"}
{"line": 773, "text": "            }"}
{"line": 774, "text": ""}
{"line": 775, "text": "        return graph"}
{"line": 776, "text": ""}
{"line": 777, "text": "    def symbol_order(self):"}
{"line": 778, "text": ""}
{"line": 779, "text": "        order = {}"}
{"line": 780, "text": ""}
{"line": 781, "text": "        for name, info in self.definitions.items():"}
{"line": 782, "text": "            order[name] = info.get(\"line\")"}
{"line": 783, "text": ""}
{"line": 784, "text": "        return order"}
{"line": 785, "text": ""}
{"line": 786, "text": "    def summary(self):"}
{"line": 787, "text": ""}
{"line": 788, "text": "        return {"}
{"line": 789, "text": "            \"model\": str(self.model),"}
{"line": 790, "text": "            \"bytes\": len("}
{"line": 791, "text": "                self.source.encode(\"utf-8\")"}
{"line": 792, "text": "            ),"}
{"line": 793, "text": "            \"sha256\": sha256_file("}
{"line": 794, "text": "                self.model"}
{"line": 795, "text": "            ),"}
{"line": 796, "text": "            \"definitions\": self.definitions,"}
{"line": 797, "text": "            \"references\": self.references,"}
{"line": 798, "text": "            \"functions\": self.functions,"}
{"line": 799, "text": "            \"classes\": self.classes,"}
{"line": 800, "text": "            \"imports\": sorted(self.imports),"}
{"line": 801, "text": "            \"assignments\": self.assignments,"}
{"line": 802, "text": "            \"unresolved_references\":"}
{"line": 803, "text": "                self.unresolved_references(),"}
{"line": 804, "text": "            \"dependency_graph\":"}
{"line": 805, "text": "                self.dependency_graph(),"}
{"line": 806, "text": "            \"symbol_order\":"}
{"line": 807, "text": "                self.symbol_order(),"}
{"line": 808, "text": "        }"}
{"line": 809, "text": ""}
{"line": 810, "text": ""}
{"line": 811, "text": "# ============================================================"}
{"line": 812, "text": "# RUNTIME PROBES"}

{"line": 915, "text": "    def corpus_probe(self):"}
{"line": 916, "text": ""}
{"line": 917, "text": "        probe = r'''"}
{"line": 918, "text": "from pathlib import Path"}
{"line": 919, "text": ""}
{"line": 920, "text": "ROOT = Path(%r)"}
{"line": 921, "text": ""}

{"line": 924, "text": "for name in ["}

{"line": 946, "text": "        else None,"}

{"line": 949, "text": "print(\"CORPUS_PROBE_END\")"}

{"line": 951, "text": ""}
{"line": 952, "text": "        return self.terminal.run("}
{"line": 953, "text": "            f\"{shlex.quote(sys.executable)} \""}
{"line": 954, "text": "            f\"-c {shlex.quote(probe)}\","}
{"line": 955, "text": "            timeout=120,"}
{"line": 956, "text": "        )"}
{"line": 957, "text": ""}
{"line": 958, "text": ""}
{"line": 959, "text": "# ============================================================"}
{"line": 960, "text": "# ERROR DISCERNMENT"}

{"line": 1001, "text": ""}

{"line": 1030, "text": ""}
{"line": 1031, "text": "    def classify("}
{"line": 1032, "text": "        self,"}
{"line": 1033, "text": "        stdout: str,"}
{"line": 1034, "text": "        stderr: str,"}
{"line": 1035, "text": "        returncode: int,"}
{"line": 1036, "text": "    ):"}
{"line": 1037, "text": ""}
{"line": 1038, "text": "        text = ("}
{"line": 1039, "text": "            (stdout or \"\")"}
{"line": 1040, "text": "            + \"\\n\""}
{"line": 1041, "text": "            + (stderr or \"\")"}
{"line": 1042, "text": "        )"}
{"line": 1043, "text": ""}
{"line": 1044, "text": "        if \"BLOOM_IMPORT_SUCCESS\" in text:"}
{"line": 1045, "text": "            return {"}
{"line": 1046, "text": "                \"category\": \"NO_ERROR\","}
{"line": 1047, "text": "                \"detail\": \"\","}
{"line": 1048, "text": "                \"confidence\": 1.0,"}
{"line": 1049, "text": "                \"raw\": text[-12000:],"}
{"line": 1050, "text": "            }"}
{"line": 1051, "text": ""}
{"line": 1052, "text": "        for category, pattern in self.PATTERNS:"}
{"line": 1053, "text": ""}
{"line": 1054, "text": "            match = pattern.search(text)"}
{"line": 1055, "text": ""}
{"line": 1056, "text": "            if match:"}
{"line": 1057, "text": ""}
{"line": 1058, "text": "                detail = ("}
{"line": 1059, "text": "                    match.group(1)"}
{"line": 1060, "text": "                    if match.groups()"}
{"line": 1061, "text": "                    else match.group(0)"}
{"line": 1062, "text": "                )"}
{"line": 1063, "text": ""}
{"line": 1064, "text": "                return {"}
{"line": 1065, "text": "                    \"category\": category,"}
{"line": 1066, "text": "                    \"detail\": detail,"}
{"line": 1067, "text": "                    \"confidence\": 1.0,"}
{"line": 1068, "text": "                    \"raw\": text[-12000:],"}
{"line": 1069, "text": "                }"}
{"line": 1070, "text": ""}
{"line": 1071, "text": "        if returncode == 0:"}
{"line": 1072, "text": ""}
{"line": 1073, "text": "            return {"}
{"line": 1074, "text": "                \"category\": \"NO_ERROR\","}
{"line": 1075, "text": "                \"detail\": \"\","}
{"line": 1076, "text": "                \"confidence\": 1.0,"}
{"line": 1077, "text": "                \"raw\": text[-12000:],"}
{"line": 1078, "text": "            }"}
{"line": 1079, "text": ""}
{"line": 1080, "text": "        return {"}
{"line": 1081, "text": "            \"category\": \"UNKNOWN_RUNTIME_FAILURE\","}
{"line": 1082, "text": "            \"detail\": text[-5000:],"}
{"line": 1083, "text": "            \"confidence\": 0.25,"}
{"line": 1084, "text": "            \"raw\": text[-12000:],"}
{"line": 1085, "text": "        }"}
{"line": 1086, "text": ""}
{"line": 1087, "text": "    def analyze("}
{"line": 1088, "text": "        self,"}
{"line": 1089, "text": "        error: dict[str, Any],"}
{"line": 1090, "text": "        trace: dict[str, Any],"}
{"line": 1091, "text": "    ):"}
{"line": 1092, "text": ""}
{"line": 1093, "text": "        category = error.get("}
{"line": 1094, "text": "            \"category\","}
{"line": 1095, "text": "            \"UNKNOWN_RUNTIME_FAILURE\","}
{"line": 1096, "text": "        )"}
{"line": 1097, "text": ""}
{"line": 1098, "text": "        knowledge = {"}
{"line": 1099, "text": "            \"known\": [],"}
{"line": 1100, "text": "            \"inferred\": [],"}
{"line": 1101, "text": "            \"unknown\": [],"}
{"line": 1102, "text": "            \"evidence\": [],"}
{"line": 1103, "text": "        }"}
{"line": 1104, "text": ""}
{"line": 1105, "text": "        decision = {"}
{"line": 1106, "text": "            \"decision\": \"INVESTIGATE\","}
{"line": 1107, "text": "            \"confidence\": 0.0,"}
{"line": 1108, "text": "            \"knowledge\": knowledge,"}
{"line": 1109, "text": "            \"next_actions\": [],"}
{"line": 1110, "text": "            \"prohibited_actions\": ["}
{"line": 1111, "text": "                \"invent training data\","}
{"line": 1112, "text": "                \"invent validation data\","}
{"line": 1113, "text": "                \"invent vocabulary\","}
{"line": 1114, "text": "                \"guess vocab_size\","}
{"line": 1115, "text": "                \"invent token IDs\","}
{"line": 1116, "text": "                \"invent model dimensions\","}
{"line": 1117, "text": "                \"invent model weights\","}
{"line": 1118, "text": "                \"train before validation passes\","}
{"line": 1119, "text": "            ],"}
{"line": 1120, "text": "        }"}
{"line": 1121, "text": ""}
{"line": 1122, "text": "        # ----------------------------------------------------"}
{"line": 1123, "text": "        # CLEAN IMPORT"}
{"line": 1124, "text": "        # ----------------------------------------------------"}
{"line": 1125, "text": ""}
{"line": 1126, "text": "        if category == \"NO_ERROR\":"}
{"line": 1127, "text": ""}
{"line": 1128, "text": "            decision[\"decision\"] = ("}
{"line": 1129, "text": "                \"VALIDATE_NEXT_CONTRACT\""}
{"line": 1130, "text": "            )"}
{"line": 1131, "text": ""}
{"line": 1132, "text": "            decision[\"confidence\"] = 1.0"}
{"line": 1133, "text": ""}
{"line": 1134, "text": "            knowledge[\"known\"].append("}
{"line": 1135, "text": "                \"Runtime import succeeds.\""}
{"line": 1136, "text": "            )"}
{"line": 1137, "text": ""}
{"line": 1138, "text": "            decision[\"next_actions\"] = ["}
{"line": 1139, "text": "                \"validate vocabulary contract\","}
{"line": 1140, "text": "                \"validate batch contract\","}

{"line": 1353, "text": "    def trace_symbol("}

{"line": 1356, "text": "    ):"}
{"line": 1357, "text": ""}
{"line": 1358, "text": "        evidence = {"}
{"line": 1359, "text": "            \"symbol\": symbol,"}
{"line": 1360, "text": "            \"definition\":"}
{"line": 1361, "text": "                self.inspector.definitions.get("}
{"line": 1362, "text": "                    symbol"}
{"line": 1363, "text": "                ),"}
{"line": 1364, "text": "            \"assignments\":"}
{"line": 1365, "text": "                self.inspector.assignments.get("}
{"line": 1366, "text": "                    symbol,"}
{"line": 1367, "text": "                    [],"}
{"line": 1368, "text": "                ),"}
{"line": 1369, "text": "            \"references\":"}
{"line": 1370, "text": "                self.inspector.references.get("}
{"line": 1371, "text": "                    symbol,"}
{"line": 1372, "text": "                    [],"}
{"line": 1373, "text": "                ),"}
{"line": 1374, "text": "            \"producer_exists\": bool("}
{"line": 1375, "text": "                self.inspector.assignments.get("}

{"line": 1379, "text": "                    symbol"}
{"line": 1380, "text": "                )"}
{"line": 1381, "text": "            ),"}
{"line": 1382, "text": "        }"}
{"line": 1383, "text": ""}
{"line": 1384, "text": "        contexts = []"}
{"line": 1385, "text": ""}
{"line": 1386, "text": "        for line in evidence[\"references\"]:"}
{"line": 1387, "text": ""}
{"line": 1388, "text": "            contexts.append("}
{"line": 1389, "text": "                self.inspector.line_context("}
{"line": 1390, "text": "                    line,"}
{"line": 1391, "text": "                    radius=10,"}
{"line": 1392, "text": "                )"}
{"line": 1393, "text": "            )"}
{"line": 1394, "text": ""}
{"line": 1395, "text": "        evidence["}
{"line": 1396, "text": "            \"reference_contexts\""}
{"line": 1397, "text": "        ] = contexts"}
{"line": 1398, "text": ""}
{"line": 1399, "text": "        return evidence"}
{"line": 1400, "text": ""}
{"line": 1401, "text": "    def trace_tokenization(self):"}
{"line": 1402, "text": ""}
{"line": 1403, "text": "        result = {"}
{"line": 1404, "text": "            \"symbols\": {},"}
{"line": 1405, "text": "            \"corpus_producers\": [],"}
{"line": 1406, "text": "            \"tokenization_functions\": [],"}
{"line": 1407, "text": "            \"likely_contract\": [],"}
{"line": 1408, "text": "        }"}
{"line": 1409, "text": ""}
{"line": 1410, "text": "        for symbol in sorted("}
{"line": 1411, "text": "            TOKEN_SYMBOLS"}
{"line": 1412, "text": "        ):"}
{"line": 1413, "text": ""}
{"line": 1414, "text": "            result[\"symbols\"][symbol] = ("}
{"line": 1415, "text": "                self.trace_symbol(symbol)"}
{"line": 1416, "text": "            )"}
{"line": 1417, "text": ""}
{"line": 1418, "text": "        # ----------------------------------------------------"}
{"line": 1419, "text": "        # TOKENIZATION FUNCTIONS"}
{"line": 1420, "text": "        # ----------------------------------------------------"}
{"line": 1421, "text": ""}
{"line": 1422, "text": "        for name, info in ("}
{"line": 1423, "text": "            self.inspector.functions.items()"}
{"line": 1424, "text": "        ):"}

{"line": 1426, "text": "            segment = ("}
{"line": 1427, "text": "                self.inspector.source_segment("}
{"line": 1428, "text": "                    name"}
{"line": 1429, "text": "                )"}
{"line": 1430, "text": "            )"}
{"line": 1431, "text": ""}

{"line": 1434, "text": ""}

{"line": 1438, "text": "                \"stoi\","}
{"line": 1439, "text": "                \"itos\","}
{"line": 1440, "text": "                \"vocab\","}

{"line": 1445, "text": "            ]"}
{"line": 1446, "text": ""}

{"line": 1450, "text": "            ):"}
{"line": 1451, "text": ""}

{"line": 1455, "text": "                    \"name\": name,"}
{"line": 1456, "text": "                    \"line\": info[\"line\"],"}
{"line": 1457, "text": "                    \"end_line\": info[\"end_line\"],"}
{"line": 1458, "text": "                })"}
{"line": 1459, "text": ""}
{"line": 1460, "text": "        # ----------------------------------------------------"}
{"line": 1461, "text": "        # REAL CORPUS"}
{"line": 1462, "text": "        # ----------------------------------------------------"}
{"line": 1463, "text": ""}
{"line": 1464, "text": "        for filename in ["}
{"line": 1465, "text": "            \"bloom_train_v2.txt\","}
{"line": 1466, "text": "            \"bloom_valid_v2.txt\","}
{"line": 1467, "text": "            \"my_texts.txt\","}

{"line": 1472, "text": "            if path.exists():"}

{"line": 1481, "text": ""}

{"line": 1483, "text": "        # CONTRACT ASSESSMENT"}

{"line": 1485, "text": ""}

{"line": 1502, "text": "            else:"}
{"line": 1503, "text": ""}
{"line": 1504, "text": "                result[\"likely_contract\"].append("}

{"line": 1510, "text": "    def model_contract(self):"}
{"line": 1511, "text": ""}
{"line": 1512, "text": "        result = {"}
{"line": 1513, "text": "            \"GPT\": self.trace_symbol(\"GPT\"),"}
{"line": 1514, "text": "            \"model\": self.trace_symbol(\"model\"),"}
{"line": 1515, "text": "            \"params\": self.trace_symbol(\"params\"),"}

{"line": 1517, "text": "        }"}

{"line": 1521, "text": "            if ("}
{"line": 1522, "text": "                call[\"name\"] == \"GPT\""}

{"line": 1524, "text": "            ):"}
{"line": 1525, "text": ""}
{"line": 1526, "text": "                result[\"calls\"].append(call)"}
{"line": 1527, "text": ""}
{"line": 1528, "text": "        return result"}
{"line": 1529, "text": ""}

{"line": 1551, "text": ""}
{"line": 1552, "text": "    def full_trace(self):"}

{"line": 1567, "text": "            \"assignments\":"}
{"line": 1568, "text": "                self.inspector.assignments,"}
{"line": 1569, "text": "            \"functions\":"}
{"line": 1570, "text": "                self.inspector.functions,"}

{"line": 1572, "text": "                self.inspector.classes,"}
{"line": 1573, "text": "        }"}

{"line": 1580, "text": "class RepairSafety:"}
{"line": 1581, "text": ""}
{"line": 1582, "text": "    def __init__(self):"}
{"line": 1583, "text": ""}

{"line": 1589, "text": "    def backup("}
{"line": 1590, "text": "        self,"}
{"line": 1591, "text": "        path: Path,"}
{"line": 1592, "text": "    ):"}
{"line": 1593, "text": ""}

{"line": 1, "text": "#!/usr/bin/env python3"}
{"line": 2, "text": ""}
{"line": 3, "text": "from pathlib import Path"}
{"line": 4, "text": "from collections import Counter"}
{"line": 5, "text": "import re"}
{"line": 6, "text": "import math"}
{"line": 7, "text": ""}
{"line": 8, "text": "ROOT = Path.home() / \"BLOOM\""}
{"line": 9, "text": "TRAIN = ROOT / \"bloom_train_v2.txt\""}
{"line": 10, "text": "VALID = ROOT / \"bloom_valid_v2.txt\""}
{"line": 11, "text": ""}
{"line": 12, "text": "TOKEN_RE = re.compile(r\"\\b[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_'-]*\\b\")"}
{"line": 13, "text": ""}
{"line": 14, "text": "def tokenize(text):"}
{"line": 15, "text": "    return TOKEN_RE.findall(text.lower())"}
{"line": 16, "text": ""}
{"line": 17, "text": "train_lines = ["}
{"line": 18, "text": "    x.strip()"}
{"line": 19, "text": "    for x in TRAIN.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").splitlines()"}
{"line": 20, "text": "    if x.strip()"}
{"line": 21, "text": "]"}
{"line": 22, "text": ""}
{"line": 23, "text": "valid_lines = ["}
{"line": 24, "text": "    x.strip()"}
{"line": 25, "text": "    for x in VALID.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").splitlines()"}
{"line": 26, "text": "    if x.strip()"}
{"line": 27, "text": "]"}
{"line": 28, "text": ""}
{"line": 29, "text": "unigram = Counter()"}
{"line": 30, "text": "bigram = Counter()"}
{"line": 31, "text": "trigram = Counter()"}
{"line": 32, "text": "contexts2 = Counter()"}
{"line": 33, "text": "contexts3 = Counter()"}
{"line": 34, "text": ""}
{"line": 35, "text": "for line in train_lines:"}
{"line": 36, "text": "    tokens = [\"<BOS>\"] + tokenize(line) + [\"<EOS>\"]"}
{"line": 37, "text": ""}
{"line": 38, "text": "    for token in tokens:"}
{"line": 39, "text": "        unigram[token] += 1"}
{"line": 40, "text": ""}
{"line": 41, "text": "    for i in range(1, len(tokens)):"}
{"line": 42, "text": "        bigram[(tokens[i-1], tokens[i])] += 1"}
{"line": 43, "text": "        contexts2[tokens[i-1]] += 1"}
{"line": 44, "text": ""}
{"line": 45, "text": "    for i in range(2, len(tokens)):"}
{"line": 46, "text": "        trigram["}
{"line": 47, "text": "            (tokens[i-2], tokens[i-1], tokens[i])"}
{"line": 48, "text": "        ] += 1"}
{"line": 49, "text": "        contexts3["}
{"line": 50, "text": "            (tokens[i-2], tokens[i-1])"}
{"line": 51, "text": "        ] += 1"}
{"line": 52, "text": ""}
{"line": 53, "text": "vocab = set(unigram)"}
{"line": 54, "text": "V = len(vocab)"}
{"line": 55, "text": "total = sum(unigram.values())"}
{"line": 56, "text": ""}
{"line": 57, "text": "def uni_prob(word):"}
{"line": 58, "text": "    return (unigram[word] + 1) / (total + V)"}
{"line": 59, "text": ""}
{"line": 60, "text": "def bi_prob(prev, word):"}
{"line": 61, "text": "    context = contexts2[prev]"}
{"line": 62, "text": ""}
{"line": 63, "text": "    if context == 0:"}
{"line": 64, "text": "        return uni_prob(word)"}
{"line": 65, "text": ""}
{"line": 66, "text": "    # Interpolated smoothing."}
{"line": 67, "text": "    ml = bigram[(prev, word)] / context"}
{"line": 68, "text": "    return 0.7 * ml + 0.3 * uni_prob(word)"}
{"line": 69, "text": ""}
{"line": 70, "text": "def tri_prob(a, b, word):"}
{"line": 71, "text": "    context = contexts3[(a, b)]"}
{"line": 72, "text": ""}
{"line": 73, "text": "    if context == 0:"}
{"line": 74, "text": "        return bi_prob(b, word)"}
{"line": 75, "text": ""}
{"line": 76, "text": "    ml = trigram[(a, b, word)] / context"}
{"line": 77, "text": "    return 0.7 * ml + 0.3 * bi_prob(b, word)"}
{"line": 78, "text": ""}
{"line": 79, "text": "def evaluate(order):"}
{"line": 80, "text": "    loss = 0.0"}
{"line": 81, "text": "    tokens = 0"}
{"line": 82, "text": "    exact_hits = 0"}
{"line": 83, "text": ""}
{"line": 84, "text": "    for line in valid_lines:"}
{"line": 85, "text": "        words = tokenize(line)"}
{"line": 86, "text": "        seq = [\"<BOS>\"] + words + [\"<EOS>\"]"}
{"line": 87, "text": ""}
{"line": 88, "text": "        for i in range(1, len(seq)):"}
{"line": 89, "text": "            word = seq[i]"}
{"line": 90, "text": ""}
{"line": 91, "text": "            if order == 1:"}
{"line": 92, "text": "                p = uni_prob(word)"}
{"line": 93, "text": "            elif order == 2:"}
{"line": 94, "text": "                p = bi_prob(seq[i-1], word)"}
{"line": 95, "text": "            else:"}
{"line": 96, "text": "                a = seq[i-2] if i >= 2 else \"<BOS>\""}
{"line": 97, "text": "                p = tri_prob(a, seq[i-1], word)"}
{"line": 98, "text": ""}
{"line": 99, "text": "            loss -= math.log(max(p, 1e-12))"}
{"line": 100, "text": "            tokens += 1"}
{"line": 101, "text": ""}
{"line": 102, "text": "            if order > 1:"}
{"line": 103, "text": "                if order == 2:"}
{"line": 104, "text": "                    if bigram[(seq[i-1], word)]:"}
{"line": 105, "text": "                        exact_hits += 1"}
{"line": 106, "text": "                else:"}
{"line": 107, "text": "                    if trigram[(a, seq[i-1], word)]:"}
{"line": 108, "text": "                        exact_hits += 1"}
{"line": 109, "text": ""}
{"line": 110, "text": "    entropy = loss / tokens"}
{"line": 111, "text": "    perplexity = math.exp(entropy)"}
{"line": 112, "text": ""}
{"line": 113, "text": "    return entropy, perplexity, tokens, exact_hits"}
{"line": 114, "text": ""}
{"line": 115, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 116, "text": "print(\"BLOOM BACKOFF / INTERPOLATION BASELINE\")"}
{"line": 117, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 118, "text": ""}
{"line": 119, "text": "print(f\"Training records   : {len(train_lines):,}\")"}
{"line": 120, "text": "print(f\"Validation records : {len(valid_lines):,}\")"}
{"line": 121, "text": "print(f\"Vocabulary         : {V:,}\")"}
{"line": 122, "text": ""}
{"line": 123, "text": "results = {}"}
{"line": 124, "text": ""}
{"line": 125, "text": "for order, name in ["}
{"line": 126, "text": "    (1, \"UNIGRAM\"),"}
{"line": 127, "text": "    (2, \"BIGRAM_BACKOFF\"),"}
{"line": 128, "text": "    (3, \"TRIGRAM_BACKOFF\"),"}
{"line": 129, "text": "]:"}
{"line": 130, "text": "    entropy, ppl, tokens, hits = evaluate(order)"}
{"line": 131, "text": ""}
{"line": 132, "text": "    results[name] = ppl"}
{"line": 133, "text": ""}
{"line": 134, "text": "    print()"}
{"line": 135, "text": "    print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 136, "text": "    print(name)"}
{"line": 137, "text": "    print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 138, "text": "    print(f\"Cross entropy      : {entropy:.6f}\")"}
{"line": 139, "text": "    print(f\"Perplexity         : {ppl:.6f}\")"}
{"line": 140, "text": ""}
{"line": 141, "text": "    if order > 1:"}
{"line": 142, "text": "        print("}
{"line": 143, "text": "            f\"Seen {order}-grams      : \""}
{"line": 144, "text": "            f\"{hits:,}/{tokens:,} \""}
{"line": 145, "text": "            f\"({hits/max(1,tokens):.2%})\""}
{"line": 146, "text": "        )"}
{"line": 147, "text": ""}
{"line": 148, "text": "print()"}
{"line": 149, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 150, "text": "print(\"SEQUENCE SIGNAL\")"}
{"line": 151, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 152, "text": ""}
{"line": 153, "text": "for name, ppl in results.items():"}
{"line": 154, "text": "    print(f\"{name:16s} | {ppl:.6f}\")"}
{"line": 155, "text": ""}
{"line": 156, "text": "if results[\"BIGRAM_BACKOFF\"] < results[\"UNIGRAM\"]:"}
{"line": 157, "text": "    print()"}
{"line": 158, "text": "    print(\"BIGRAM BACKOFF: POSITIVE SEQUENCE SIGNAL\")"}
{"line": 159, "text": "else:"}
{"line": 160, "text": "    print()"}
{"line": 161, "text": "    print(\"BIGRAM BACKOFF: NO IMPROVEMENT\")"}
{"line": 162, "text": ""}
{"line": 163, "text": "if results[\"TRIGRAM_BACKOFF\"] < results[\"BIGRAM_BACKOFF\"]:"}
{"line": 164, "text": "    print(\"TRIGRAM BACKOFF: HIGHER-ORDER SIGNAL\")"}
{"line": 165, "text": "else:"}
{"line": 166, "text": "    print(\"TRIGRAM BACKOFF: NO ADDITIONAL SIGNAL\")"}
{"line": 167, "text": ""}
{"line": 168, "text": "print()"}
{"line": 169, "text": "print(\"NOTE: This is a diagnostic baseline, not BLOOM training.\")"}
{"line": 170, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}

================================================================================
FILE: bloom_baseline.py
================================================================================
{"line": 1, "text": "#!/usr/bin/env python3"}
{"line": 2, "text": ""}
{"line": 3, "text": "from pathlib import Path"}
{"line": 4, "text": "from collections import Counter"}
{"line": 5, "text": "import re"}
{"line": 6, "text": "import math"}
{"line": 7, "text": ""}
{"line": 8, "text": "ROOT = Path.home() / \"BLOOM\""}
{"line": 9, "text": "TRAIN = ROOT / \"bloom_train_v2.txt\""}
{"line": 10, "text": "VALID = ROOT / \"bloom_valid_v2.txt\""}
{"line": 11, "text": ""}
{"line": 12, "text": "def tokenize(text):"}
{"line": 13, "text": "    return re.findall("}
{"line": 14, "text": "        r\"\\b[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_'-]*\\b\","}
{"line": 15, "text": "        text.lower()"}
{"line": 16, "text": "    )"}
{"line": 17, "text": ""}
{"line": 18, "text": "train = tokenize(TRAIN.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))"}
{"line": 19, "text": "valid = tokenize(VALID.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))"}
{"line": 20, "text": ""}
{"line": 21, "text": "counts = Counter(train)"}
{"line": 22, "text": "total = len(train)"}
{"line": 23, "text": ""}
{"line": 24, "text": "# Laplace-smoothed unigram model."}
{"line": 25, "text": "vocab = set(train)"}
{"line": 26, "text": "V = len(vocab)"}
{"line": 27, "text": ""}
{"line": 28, "text": "log_loss = 0.0"}
{"line": 29, "text": "known = 0"}
{"line": 30, "text": "unknown = 0"}
{"line": 31, "text": ""}
{"line": 32, "text": "for word in valid:"}
{"line": 33, "text": "    probability = (counts[word] + 1) / (total + V + 1)"}
{"line": 34, "text": ""}
{"line": 35, "text": "    log_loss -= math.log(probability)"}
{"line": 36, "text": ""}
{"line": 37, "text": "    if word in vocab:"}
{"line": 38, "text": "        known += 1"}
{"line": 39, "text": "    else:"}
{"line": 40, "text": "        unknown += 1"}
{"line": 41, "text": ""}
{"line": 42, "text": "cross_entropy = log_loss / max(1, len(valid))"}
{"line": 43, "text": "perplexity = math.exp(cross_entropy)"}
{"line": 44, "text": ""}
{"line": 45, "text": "# Most common validation token prediction baseline."}
{"line": 46, "text": "majority = counts.most_common(1)[0][0]"}
{"line": 47, "text": ""}
{"line": 48, "text": "majority_hits = sum("}
{"line": 49, "text": "    1 for word in valid"}
{"line": 50, "text": "    if word == majority"}
{"line": 51, "text": ")"}
{"line": 52, "text": ""}
{"line": 53, "text": "majority_accuracy = majority_hits / max(1, len(valid))"}
{"line": 54, "text": ""}
{"line": 55, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 56, "text": "print(\"BLOOM BASELINE LANGUAGE MODEL\")"}

{"line": 31, "text": "        except: txt=\"bloom frontier light water soil wind dew need oath risk power \""}
{"line": 32, "text": "    return txt"}

{"line": 38, "text": ""}

{"line": 40, "text": "hidden=128; block=32; batch=16"}
{"line": 41, "text": "def rand_mat(a,b, scale=0.5):"}
{"line": 42, "text": "    if HAS_NP: return np.random.randn(a,b)*scale/math.sqrt(a)"}
{"line": 43, "text": "    return [[random.gauss(0,scale/math.sqrt(a)) for _ in range(b)] for _ in range(a)]"}
{"line": 44, "text": ""}
{"line": 45, "text": "try: seed=json.load(open(GARDEN/\"seedling.json\"))"}
{"line": 46, "text": "except:"}
{"line": 47, "text": "    if HAS_NP:"}
{"line": 48, "text": "        Wxh=np.random.randn(vocab_size,hidden)*0.2"}
{"line": 49, "text": "        Whh=np.random.randn(hidden,hidden)*0.2/math.sqrt(hidden)"}
{"line": 50, "text": "        Why=np.random.randn(hidden,vocab_size)*0.2"}
{"line": 51, "text": "        bh=np.zeros(hidden); by=np.zeros(vocab_size)"}
{"line": 52, "text": "    else:"}
{"line": 53, "text": "        Wxh=rand_mat(vocab_size,hidden); Whh=rand_mat(hidden,hidden); Why=rand_mat(hidden,vocab_size); bh=[0]*hidden; by=[0]*vocab_size"}
{"line": 54, "text": "    seed={\"gen\":0,\"id\":hashlib.sha256(b\"best0\").hexdigest()[:6],\"Wxh\":None}"}

{"line": 58, "text": "    # load weights"}
{"line": 59, "text": "    if HAS_NP and (GARDEN/\"W.npz\").exists():"}

{"line": 61, "text": "    else:"}
{"line": 62, "text": "        Wxh=rand_mat(vocab_size,hidden); Whh=rand_mat(hidden,hidden); Why=rand_mat(hidden,vocab_size); bh=[0]*hidden; by=[0]*vocab_size"}
{"line": 63, "text": ""}
{"line": 64, "text": "def softmax_np(x):"}
{"line": 65, "text": "    m=np.max(x,axis=-1,keepdims=True); e=np.exp(x-m); return e/np.sum(e,axis=-1,keepdims=True)"}
{"line": 66, "text": ""}
{"line": 67, "text": "def forward_np(xs, hprev):"}
{"line": 68, "text": "    # xs: (T,B) ints"}
{"line": 69, "text": "    T,B=xs.shape; h=np.zeros((T+1,B,hidden)); h[0]=hprev"}
{"line": 70, "text": "    logits=np.zeros((T,B,vocab_size))"}
{"line": 71, "text": "    for t in range(T):"}
{"line": 72, "text": "        xh=Wxh[xs[t]] # (B, hidden)"}
{"line": 73, "text": "        hh=h[t]@Whh"}
{"line": 74, "text": "        h[t+1]=np.tanh(xh+hh+bh)"}
{"line": 75, "text": "        logits[t]=h[t+1]@Why+by"}
{"line": 76, "text": "    return logits, h"}
{"line": 77, "text": ""}
{"line": 78, "text": "def loss_np(logits, targets):"}
{"line": 79, "text": "    probs=softmax_np(logits); # (T,B,V)"}
{"line": 80, "text": "    T,B,_=logits.shape;"}
{"line": 81, "text": "    logp=-np.log(probs[np.arange(T)[:,None], np.arange(B), targets]+1e-8)"}
{"line": 82, "text": "    return np.mean(logp), probs"}
{"line": 83, "text": ""}
{"line": 84, "text": "# evolution mutate"}
{"line": 85, "text": "def mutate_np(scale=0.05):"}
{"line": 86, "text": "    global Wxh, Whh, Why, bh, by"}
{"line": 87, "text": "    Wxh+=np.random.randn(*Wxh.shape)*scale"}
{"line": 88, "text": "    Whh+=np.random.randn(*Whh.shape)*scale*0.5"}
{"line": 89, "text": "    Why+=np.random.randn(*Why.shape)*scale"}
{"line": 90, "text": "    bh+=np.random.randn(*bh.shape)*scale*0.1"}
{"line": 91, "text": "    by+=np.random.randn(*by.shape)*scale*0.1"}
{"line": 92, "text": ""}
{"line": 93, "text": "# 3. TRAIN - frontier autopoiesis"}
{"line": 94, "text": "data=np.array([stoi[c] for c in text]) if HAS_NP else [stoi[c] for c in text]"}
{"line": 95, "text": "total_steps=seed.get(\"total_steps\",0) if isinstance(seed,dict) else 0"}
{"line": 96, "text": "gen=seed[\"gen\"] if isinstance(seed,dict) else 0"}
{"line": 97, "text": "dew=100.0 if dew<=0 else dew"}
{"line": 98, "text": "best_loss=10.0; hits=[]"}
{"line": 99, "text": ""}
{"line": 100, "text": "print(f\"\\nBEST LLM gen {gen} dew {dew:.1f} hidden {hidden} vocab {vocab_size} np={HAS_NP}\")"}
{"line": 101, "text": "for step in range(500):"}
{"line": 102, "text": "    if dew<=0:"}
{"line": 103, "text": "        print(f\"DEATH step {total_steps} gen {gen} loss {best_loss:.3f} -> COURAGE mutate\")"}
{"line": 104, "text": "        open(GARDEN/\"lineage.jsonl\",\"a\").write(json.dumps({\"gen\":gen,\"loss\":best_loss,\"dew\":dew})+\"\\n\")"}
{"line": 105, "text": "        gen+=1; mutate_np(0.2); dew=100.0; best_loss+=0.5"}
{"line": 106, "text": "        print(f\" REBORN gen {gen}\")"}
{"line": 107, "text": "    # sample batch"}
{"line": 108, "text": "    if HAS_NP:"}
{"line": 109, "text": "        idx=np.random.randint(0, len(data)-block-1, size=batch)"}
{"line": 110, "text": "        xs=np.zeros((block,batch), dtype=int); ys=np.zeros((block,batch), dtype=int)"}
{"line": 111, "text": "        for b,i in enumerate(idx): xs[:,b]=data[i:i+block]; ys[:,b]=data[i+1:i+block+1]"}
{"line": 112, "text": "        logits,h=forward_np(xs, np.zeros((batch,hidden)))"}
{"line": 113, "text": "        loss,probs=loss_np(logits, ys)"}
{"line": 114, "text": "        # simple evolutionary hill climb, not backprop = frontier style"}
{"line": 115, "text": "        old=[Wxh.copy(),Whh.copy(),Why.copy(),bh.copy(),by.copy()]"}
{"line": 116, "text": "        mutate_np(0.02)"}
{"line": 117, "text": "        logits2,_=forward_np(xs, np.zeros((batch,hidden)))"}
{"line": 118, "text": "        loss2,_=loss_np(logits2, ys)"}
{"line": 119, "text": "        if loss2<loss:"}
{"line": 120, "text": "            best_loss=min(best_loss,loss2); dew=dew-0.5+2.0; hits.append(1)"}
{"line": 121, "text": "            if abs(loss-loss2)>0.1:"}
{"line": 122, "text": "                open(GARDEN/\"humus.jsonl\",\"a\").write(json.dumps({\"gen\":gen,\"loss\":float(loss),\"improve\":float(loss-loss2)})+\"\\n\")"}
{"line": 123, "text": "        else:"}
{"line": 124, "text": "            # revert"}
{"line": 125, "text": "            Wxh,Whh,Why,bh,by=old; dew=dew-0.5; hits.append(0)"}
{"line": 126, "text": "        if step%50==0:"}
{"line": 127, "text": "            # generate"}
{"line": 128, "text": "            cur=np.array([[stoi[text[0]]]]); hgen=np.zeros((1,hidden)); out=text[0]"}
{"line": 129, "text": "            for _ in range(80):"}
{"line": 130, "text": "                lg,_=forward_np(cur, hgen); p=softmax_np(lg[-1]); nxt=np.random.choice(vocab_size,p=p[0]); out+=itos[nxt]; cur=np.array([[nxt]])"}
{"line": 131, "text": "            print(f\" {total_steps} gen{gen} dew {dew:.1f} loss {loss:.3f}->{loss2:.3f} acc {sum(hits[-50:])/50:.2f} | {out[:80]}\")"}
{"line": 132, "text": "    total_steps+=1"}
{"line": 133, "text": ""}
{"line": 134, "text": "# save"}

{"line": 136, "text": "open(GARDEN/\"seedling.json\",\"w\").write(json.dumps({\"gen\":gen,\"id\":hashlib.sha256(str(time.time()).encode()).hexdigest()[:6],\"total_steps\":total_steps,\"vocab_size\":vocab_size}))"}
{"line": 137, "text": "open(GARDEN/\"dew.json\",\"w\").write(json.dumps({\"dew\":float(dew)}))"}
{"line": 138, "text": "print(f\"\\nDONE gen {gen} dew {dew:.1f} best_loss {best_loss:.3f}\")"}

{"line": 1, "text": "import requests, numpy as np"}
{"line": 2, "text": "from pathlib import Path"}
{"line": 3, "text": ""}
{"line": 4, "text": "txt=requests.get(\"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/karpathy/char-rnn/master/data/tinyshakespeare/input.txt\",timeout=15).text[:50000]"}
{"line": 5, "text": "chars=sorted(list(set(txt))); vs=len(chars)"}
{"line": 6, "text": "stoi={ch:i for i,ch in enumerate(chars)}"}
{"line": 7, "text": "itos={i:ch for i,ch in enumerate(chars)}"}
{"line": 8, "text": "data=np.array([stoi[c] for c in txt])"}
{"line": 9, "text": "print(f\"vocab {vs} len {len(txt)}\")"}
{"line": 10, "text": ""}
{"line": 11, "text": "hidden=128; block=32; batch=16"}
{"line": 12, "text": "Wxh=np.random.randn(vs,hidden)*0.1"}
{"line": 13, "text": "Whh=np.random.randn(hidden,hidden)*0.1/np.sqrt(hidden)"}
{"line": 14, "text": "Why=np.random.randn(hidden,vs)*0.1"}
{"line": 15, "text": "bh=np.zeros(hidden); by=np.zeros(vs)"}
{"line": 16, "text": "mWxh=np.zeros_like(Wxh); mWhh=np.zeros_like(Whh); mWhy=np.zeros_like(Why); mbh=np.zeros_like(bh); mby=np.zeros_like(by)"}
{"line": 17, "text": "vWxh=np.zeros_like(Wxh); vWhh=np.zeros_like(Whh); vWhy=np.zeros_like(Why); vbh=np.zeros_like(bh); vby=np.zeros_like(by)"}
{"line": 18, "text": ""}
{"line": 19, "text": "def forward(xs, h0):"}
{"line": 20, "text": "    T,B=xs.shape"}
{"line": 21, "text": "    h=np.zeros((T+1,B,hidden))"}
{"line": 22, "text": "    h[0]=h0"}
{"line": 23, "text": "    logits=np.zeros((T,B,vs))"}
{"line": 24, "text": "    for t in range(T):"}
{"line": 25, "text": "        h[t+1]=np.tanh(Wxh[xs[t]] + h[t]@Whh + bh)"}
{"line": 26, "text": "        logits[t]=h[t+1]@Why + by"}
{"line": 27, "text": "    return logits, h"}
{"line": 28, "text": ""}
{"line": 29, "text": "def loss_and_grad(logits, targets, h, xs):"}
{"line": 30, "text": "    T,B,_=logits.shape"}
{"line": 31, "text": "    m=np.max(logits,axis=-1,keepdims=True); e=np.exp(logits-m); probs=e/np.sum(e,axis=-1,keepdims=True)"}
{"line": 32, "text": "    loss=-np.mean(np.log(probs[np.arange(T)[:,None], np.arange(B), targets]+1e-8))"}
{"line": 33, "text": "    dlogits=probs.copy(); dlogits[np.arange(T)[:,None], np.arange(B), targets]-=1; dlogits/=T*B"}
{"line": 34, "text": "    dWhy=np.zeros_like(Why); dby=np.zeros_like(by); dWxh=np.zeros_like(Wxh); dWhh=np.zeros_like(Whh); dbh=np.zeros_like(bh)"}
{"line": 35, "text": "    dh_next=np.zeros((B,hidden))"}
{"line": 36, "text": "    for t in reversed(range(T)):"}
{"line": 37, "text": "        dlog=dlogits[t]"}
{"line": 38, "text": "        dWhy+=h[t+1].T@dlog; dby+=np.sum(dlog,axis=0)"}
{"line": 39, "text": "        dh=dlog@Why.T + dh_next"}
{"line": 40, "text": "        dh_raw=dh*(1-h[t+1]**2)"}
{"line": 41, "text": "        dbh+=np.sum(dh_raw,axis=0)"}
{"line": 42, "text": "        dWhh+=h[t].T@dh_raw"}
{"line": 43, "text": "        for b in range(B): dWxh[xs[t,b]]+=dh_raw[b]"}
{"line": 44, "text": "        dh_next=dh_raw@Whh.T"}
{"line": 45, "text": "    return loss, dWxh, dWhh, dWhy, dbh, dby"}
{"line": 46, "text": ""}
{"line": 47, "text": "dew=100.0; gen=0; best=10"}
{"line": 48, "text": "for it in range(1000):"}
{"line": 49, "text": "    if dew<=0:"}
{"line": 50, "text": "        print(f\"DEATH gen{gen} dew {dew:.1f} -> REBORN\"); gen+=1; dew=100"}
{"line": 51, "text": "    idx=np.random.randint(0,len(data)-block-1,size=batch)"}
{"line": 52, "text": "    xs=np.zeros((block,batch),dtype=int); ys=np.zeros((block,batch),dtype=int)"}
{"line": 53, "text": "    for b,i in enumerate(idx): xs[:,b]=data[i:i+block]; ys[:,b]=data[i+1:i+block+1]"}
{"line": 54, "text": "    logits,h=forward(xs, np.zeros((batch,hidden)))"}
{"line": 55, "text": "    loss,dWxh,dWhh,dWhy,dbh,dby=loss_and_grad(logits, ys, h, xs)"}
{"line": 56, "text": "    beta1=0.9; beta2=0.999; lr=0.005"}
{"line": 57, "text": "    for p,m,v,d in [(Wxh,mWxh,vWxh,dWxh),(Whh,mWhh,vWhh,dWhh),(Why,mWhy,vWhy,dWhy),(bh,mbh,vbh,dbh),(by,mby,vby,dby)]:"}
{"line": 58, "text": "        m[:]=beta1*m+(1-beta1)*d; v[:]=beta2*v+(1-beta2)*(d*d)"}
{"line": 59, "text": "        p-=lr*(m/(1-beta1**(it+1)))/(np.sqrt(v/(1-beta2**(it+1)))+1e-8)"}
{"line": 60, "text": "    if loss<best: dew=dew-0.3+1.5; best=loss"}
{"line": 61, "text": "    else: dew-=0.3"}
{"line": 62, "text": "    if it%50==0:"}
{"line": 63, "text": "        cur=np.array([[stoi[txt[0]]]])"}
{"line": 64, "text": "        h_state=np.zeros((1,hidden))"}
{"line": 65, "text": "        out=txt[0]"}
{"line": 66, "text": "        for _ in range(120):"}
{"line": 67, "text": "            lg,h_full=forward(cur, h_state)"}
{"line": 68, "text": "            h_state=h_full[-1]"}
{"line": 69, "text": "            prob=np.exp(lg[-1]-np.max(lg[-1])); prob/=prob.sum()"}
{"line": 70, "text": "            nxt=np.random.choice(vs,p=prob[0])"}
{"line": 71, "text": "            out+=itos[nxt]"}
{"line": 72, "text": "            cur=np.array([[nxt]])"}
{"line": 73, "text": "        print(f\"{it} gen{gen} dew {dew:.1f} loss {loss:.3f} best {best:.3f} | {out[:120]}\")"}

================================================================================
FILE: bloom_chat.py

{"line": 1, "text": "import numpy as np, requests"}
{"line": 2, "text": "txt=requests.get(\"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/karpathy/char-rnn/master/data/tinyshakespeare/input.txt\",timeout=15).text[:50000]"}
{"line": 3, "text": "chars=sorted(list(set(txt))); vs=len(chars)"}
{"line": 4, "text": "stoi={ch:i for i,ch in enumerate(chars)}; itos={i:ch for i,ch in enumerate(chars)}"}
{"line": 5, "text": "print(f\"vocab {vs} chars loaded\")"}
{"line": 6, "text": "hidden=128"}
{"line": 7, "text": "Wxh=np.random.randn(vs,hidden)*0.1; Whh=np.random.randn(hidden,hidden)*0.1/np.sqrt(hidden); Why=np.random.randn(hidden,vs)*0.1"}
{"line": 8, "text": "bh=np.zeros(hidden); by=np.zeros(vs)"}
{"line": 9, "text": "try:"}
{"line": 10, "text": "    d=np.load(\"garden_best_llm/W.npz\"); Wxh,Whh,Why,bh,by=d[\"Wxh\"],d[\"Whh\"],d[\"Why\"],d[\"bh\"],d[\"by\"]"}
{"line": 11, "text": "    print(f\"loaded W.npz loss ~1.45\")"}
{"line": 12, "text": "except: print(\"fresh weights\")"}
{"line": 13, "text": ""}
{"line": 14, "text": "def gen(seed, n=400, temp=0.7):"}
{"line": 15, "text": "    # clean seed to known chars only"}
{"line": 16, "text": "    clean=\"\".join([c for c in seed if c in stoi])"}
{"line": 17, "text": "    if not clean: clean=\"\\n\""}
{"line": 18, "text": "    h=np.zeros((1,hidden))"}
{"line": 19, "text": "    # prime with seed"}
{"line": 20, "text": "    for ch in clean[:-1]:"}
{"line": 21, "text": "        x=np.array([[stoi[ch]]]); hh=np.tanh(Wxh[x[0]] + h@Whh + bh); h=hh"}
{"line": 22, "text": "    cur=np.array([[stoi[clean[-1]]]]); out=clean"}
{"line": 23, "text": "    for _ in range(n):"}
{"line": 24, "text": "        hh=np.tanh(Wxh[cur[0]] + h@Whh + bh); lg=hh@Why+by"}
{"line": 25, "text": "        lg/=temp; p=np.exp(lg-np.max(lg)); p/=p.sum()"}
{"line": 26, "text": "        nxt=np.random.choice(vs,p=p[0]); out+=itos[nxt]; cur=np.array([[nxt]]); h=hh"}
{"line": 27, "text": "    return out"}
{"line": 28, "text": ""}
{"line": 29, "text": "while True:"}
{"line": 30, "text": "    s=input(\"\\nseed> \")"}
{"line": 31, "text": "    if s==\"q\": break"}
{"line": 32, "text": "    print(gen(s, 500))"}

FILE: bloom_colony.py

{"line": 1, "text": "import json, math, random, hashlib, sys, time, subprocess"}
{"line": 2, "text": "from pathlib import Path"}
{"line": 3, "text": "GARDEN=(Path.cwd()/\"garden_term\").resolve()"}
{"line": 4, "text": "GARDEN.mkdir(exist_ok=True)"}
{"line": 5, "text": "VENV=GARDEN/\"venv\""}
{"line": 6, "text": "sys.path.insert(0, str(VENV/\"lib\"/f\"python{sys.version_info.major}.{sys.version_info.minor}\"/\"site-packages\"))"}
{"line": 7, "text": "import requests, numpy as np"}
{"line": 8, "text": "def get_spy():"}
{"line": 9, "text": "    url=\"https://query1.finance.yahoo.com/v8/finance/chart/SPY?range=2y&interval=1d\""}
{"line": 10, "text": "    j=requests.get(url, headers={\"User-Agent\":\"Mozilla/5.0\"}, timeout=15).json()[\"chart\"][\"result\"][0]"}
{"line": 11, "text": "    return [c for c in j[\"indicators\"][\"quote\"][0][\"close\"] if c], [v for v in j[\"indicators\"][\"quote\"][0][\"volume\"] if v]"}
{"line": 12, "text": "closes, volumes=get_spy(); N=len(closes)"}
{"line": 13, "text": "def battery():"}
{"line": 14, "text": "    try: return json.loads(subprocess.check_output([\"termux-battery-status\"], timeout=1).decode())[\"percentage\"]/10"}
{"line": 15, "text": "    except: return 8.0"}
{"line": 16, "text": "def s_t(idx):"}
{"line": 17, "text": "    w=closes[max(0,idx-20):idx+1]; p=closes[idx]; ma20=sum(w)/len(w)"}
{"line": 18, "text": "    mom=max(0,min(10,(p/ma20-0.95)*100)); vol=volumes[idx]; vol_ma=sum(volumes[max(0,idx-20):idx+1])/max(1,len(w))"}
{"line": 19, "text": "    liq=max(0,min(10,vol/vol_ma*5)); ma200=sum(closes[max(0,idx-200):idx+1])/min(200,idx+1)"}
{"line": 20, "text": "    val=max(0,min(10,10-abs(p/ma200-1)*100)); mean=sum(w)/len(w); var=sum((x-mean)**2 for x in w)/len(w) if len(w)>1 else 0.01"}
{"line": 21, "text": "    volat=max(0,min(10,math.sqrt(var)/mean*200))"}
{"line": 22, "text": "    return {\"momentum\":mom,\"liquidity\":liq,\"value\":val,\"volatility\":volat,\"battery\":battery()}"}
{"line": 23, "text": "def sigmoid(x): return 1/(1+math.exp(-max(-20,min(20,x))))"}
{"line": 24, "text": "# 4 deterministic individuals"}
{"line": 25, "text": "COLONY=["}
{"line": 26, "text": " {\"id\":\"a3f1\",\"bias\":{\"momentum\":0.22,\"liquidity\":0.05,\"value\":0.05,\"volatility\":-0.05,\"battery\":0.02}},"}
{"line": 27, "text": " {\"id\":\"b7c2\",\"bias\":{\"momentum\":0.05,\"liquidity\":0.05,\"value\":0.05,\"volatility\":-0.25,\"battery\":0.05}},"}
{"line": 28, "text": " {\"id\":\"c9d4\",\"bias\":{\"momentum\":0.05,\"liquidity\":0.15,\"value\":0.05,\"volatility\":-0.05,\"battery\":0.10}},"}
{"line": 29, "text": " {\"id\":\"e1f8\",\"bias\":{\"momentum\":0.05,\"liquidity\":0.05,\"value\":0.18,\"volatility\":-0.08,\"battery\":0.03}},"}
{"line": 30, "text": "]"}
{"line": 31, "text": "agents=[]"}
{"line": 32, "text": "for c in COLONY:"}
{"line": 33, "text": "    theta={\"root\":{\"mean\":0.0},\"petals\":{k:{\"mean\":v} for k,v in c[\"bias\"].items()},\"gen\":0,\"total\":0,\"id\":c[\"id\"]}"}
{"line": 34, "text": "    e=100.0"}
{"line": 35, "text": "    # load if exists"}
{"line": 36, "text": "    p=GARDEN/f\"seedling_{c['id']}.json\""}
{"line": 37, "text": "    if p.exists():"}
{"line": 38, "text": "        try:"}
{"line": 39, "text": "            theta=json.loads(p.read_text()); e=json.loads((GARDEN/f\"dew_{c['id']}.json\").read_text())[\"dew\"]"}
{"line": 40, "text": "        except: pass"}
{"line": 41, "text": "    agents.append({\"theta\":theta,\"e\":e,\"id\":c[\"id\"]})"}
{"line": 42, "text": ""}
{"line": 43, "text": "def do_step(agent):"}
{"line": 44, "text": "    th=agent[\"theta\"]; e=agent[\"e\"]"}
{"line": 45, "text": "    if e<=0:"}
{"line": 46, "text": "        print(f\"\\n[DEATH] {agent['id']} gen {th['gen']}\")"}
{"line": 47, "text": "        # gene mix from best survivor"}
{"line": 48, "text": "        best=max(agents, key=lambda a: a[\"e\"])"}
{"line": 49, "text": "        if best[\"id\"]!=agent[\"id\"]:"}
{"line": 50, "text": "            for k in th[\"petals\"]:"}
{"line": 51, "text": "                th[\"petals\"][k][\"mean\"]=th[\"petals\"][k][\"mean\"]*0.5+best[\"theta\"][\"petals\"][k][\"mean\"]*0.5+random.gauss(0,0.1)"}
{"line": 52, "text": "        th[\"gen\"]+=1; agent[\"e\"]=100.0; return"}
{"line": 53, "text": "    st=s_t(th[\"total\"]% (N-1))"}
{"line": 54, "text": "    vigor=sum(st[k]*th[\"petals\"][k][\"mean\"] for k in st)+th[\"root\"][\"mean\"]"}
{"line": 55, "text": "    p=sigmoid(vigor)"}
{"line": 56, "text": "    rng=random.Random(int(hashlib.sha256(f\"{th['id']}{th['total']}\".encode()).hexdigest()[:8],16))"}
{"line": 57, "text": "    a_t=1 if rng.random()<p else 0"}
{"line": 58, "text": "    fr=(closes[(th[\"total\"]+1)%(N-1)]-closes[th[\"total\"]%(N-1)])/closes[th[\"total\"]%(N-1)]"}
{"line": 59, "text": "    pay=fr*20 if a_t==1 else 0"}
{"line": 60, "text": "    if a_t==1 and st[\"volatility\"]>6: pay-=1.4"}
{"line": 61, "text": "    e_next=e-0.1+pay; r_t=abs(100-e)-abs(100-e_next); grad=(a_t-p)"}
{"line": 62, "text": "    for k in st: th[\"petals\"][k][\"mean\"]=th[\"petals\"][k][\"mean\"]*0.995+0.08*r_t*grad*st[k]"}
{"line": 63, "text": "    th[\"root\"][\"mean\"]*=0.995"}
{"line": 64, "text": "    print(f\" {agent['id']} bat {st['battery']:.1f} vol {st['volatility']:.1f} -> {'BLOOM' if a_t else 'WILT'} e {e:.1f}->{e_next:.1f} mom_w {th['petals']['momentum']['mean']:.3f}\")"}
{"line": 65, "text": "    agent[\"e\"]=e_next; th[\"total\"]+=1"}
{"line": 66, "text": "    open(GARDEN/f\"seedling_{agent['id']}.json\",\"w\").write(json.dumps(th,indent=2))"}
{"line": 67, "text": "    open(GARDEN/f\"dew_{agent['id']}.json\",\"w\").write(json.dumps({\"dew\":e_next}))"}
{"line": 68, "text": ""}
{"line": 69, "text": "print(f\"\\nCOLONY {len(agents)} agents - deterministic ids {[a['id'] for a in agents]}\\n\")"}
{"line": 70, "text": "while True:"}
{"line": 71, "text": "    cmd=input(\"> \").strip()"}
{"line": 72, "text": "    if cmd in (\"exit\",\"q\"): break"}
{"line": 73, "text": "    if cmd.startswith(\"step\"):"}
{"line": 74, "text": "        try: n=int(cmd.split()[1])"}
{"line": 75, "text": "        except: n=1"}
{"line": 76, "text": "        for _ in range(n):"}
{"line": 77, "text": "            for ag in agents: do_step(ag)"}
{"line": 78, "text": "    elif cmd==\"genome\":"}
{"line": 79, "text": "        for ag in agents: print(ag[\"id\"], {k:round(v[\"mean\"],3) for k,v in ag[\"theta\"][\"petals\"].items()})"}
{"line": 80, "text": "    elif cmd==\"status\":"}
{"line": 81, "text": "        for ag in agents: print(ag[\"id\"], f\"e={ag['e']:.1f} gen={ag['theta']['gen']}\")"}
