{"line": 1, "text": "#!/usr/bin/env python3"}
{"line": 2, "text": ""}
{"line": 3, "text": "import hashlib"}
{"line": 4, "text": "from pathlib import Path"}
{"line": 5, "text": ""}
{"line": 6, "text": "ROOT = Path.home() / \"BLOOM\""}
{"line": 7, "text": "OUTPUT = ROOT / \"training_corpus.txt\""}
{"line": 8, "text": "MIN_CHARS = 1000"}
{"line": 9, "text": ""}
{"line": 10, "text": "EXCLUDE_DIRS = {"}
{"line": 11, "text": "    \".git\","}
{"line": 12, "text": "    \"__pycache__\","}
{"line": 13, "text": "    \".venv\","}
{"line": 14, "text": "    \"venv\","}
{"line": 15, "text": "    \"node_modules\","}
{"line": 16, "text": "}"}
{"line": 17, "text": ""}
{"line": 18, "text": "EXCLUDE_FILES = {"}
{"line": 19, "text": "    OUTPUT.name,"}
{"line": 20, "text": "    \"bloom_corpus.py\","}
{"line": 21, "text": "    \"training_corpus.txt\","}
{"line": 22, "text": "}"}
{"line": 23, "text": ""}
{"line": 24, "text": "EXTENSIONS = {"}
{"line": 25, "text": "    \".txt\","}
{"line": 26, "text": "    \".md\","}
{"line": 27, "text": "    \".rst\","}
{"line": 28, "text": "    \".text\","}
{"line": 29, "text": "    \".log\","}
{"line": 30, "text": "    \".csv\","}
{"line": 31, "text": "    \".json\","}
{"line": 32, "text": "    \".jsonl\","}
{"line": 33, "text": "    \".py\","}
{"line": 34, "text": "    \".sh\","}
{"line": 35, "text": "    \".yaml\","}
{"line": 36, "text": "    \".yml\","}
{"line": 37, "text": "}"}
{"line": 38, "text": ""}
{"line": 39, "text": "def sha256(data):"}
{"line": 40, "text": "    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()"}
{"line": 41, "text": ""}
{"line": 42, "text": "def discover():"}
{"line": 43, "text": "    found = []"}
{"line": 44, "text": ""}
{"line": 45, "text": "    for p in ROOT.rglob(\"*\"):"}
{"line": 46, "text": "        if not p.is_file():"}
{"line": 47, "text": "            continue"}
{"line": 48, "text": ""}
{"line": 49, "text": "        if p.name in EXCLUDE_FILES:"}
{"line": 50, "text": "            continue"}
{"line": 51, "text": ""}
{"line": 52, "text": "        if any(part in EXCLUDE_DIRS for part in p.parts):"}
{"line": 53, "text": "            continue"}
{"line": 54, "text": ""}
{"line": 55, "text": "        if p.suffix.lower() not in EXTENSIONS:"}
{"line": 56, "text": "            continue"}
{"line": 57, "text": ""}
{"line": 58, "text": "        try:"}
{"line": 59, "text": "            data = p.read_bytes()"}
{"line": 60, "text": "            text = data.decode(\"utf-8\", errors=\"strict\")"}
{"line": 61, "text": "        except Exception:"}
{"line": 62, "text": "            continue"}
{"line": 63, "text": ""}
{"line": 64, "text": "        if not text.strip():"}
{"line": 65, "text": "            continue"}
{"line": 66, "text": ""}
{"line": 67, "text": "        found.append((p, data, text))"}
{"line": 68, "text": ""}
{"line": 69, "text": "    return found"}
{"line": 70, "text": ""}
{"line": 71, "text": "def main():"}
{"line": 72, "text": "    print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 73, "text": "    print(\"BLOOM CORPUS ENGINE\")"}
{"line": 74, "text": "    print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 75, "text": ""}
{"line": 76, "text": "    sources = discover()"}
{"line": 77, "text": ""}
{"line": 78, "text": "    seen = set()"}
{"line": 79, "text": "    accepted = []"}
{"line": 80, "text": "    duplicates = 0"}
{"line": 81, "text": ""}
{"line": 82, "text": "    for path, data, text in sources:"}
{"line": 83, "text": "        digest = sha256(data)"}
{"line": 84, "text": ""}
{"line": 85, "text": "        if digest in seen:"}
{"line": 86, "text": "            duplicates += 1"}
{"line": 87, "text": "            continue"}
{"line": 88, "text": ""}
{"line": 89, "text": "        seen.add(digest)"}
{"line": 90, "text": "        accepted.append((path, data, text))"}
{"line": 91, "text": ""}
{"line": 92, "text": "        print("}
{"line": 93, "text": "            f\"{str(path.relative_to(ROOT)):<45} \""}
{"line": 94, "text": "            f\"bytes={len(data):<8} \""}
{"line": 95, "text": "            f\"chars={len(text):<8} \""}
{"line": 96, "text": "            f\"words={len(text.split()):<8} \""}
{"line": 97, "text": "            f\"sha256={digest[:16]}\""}
{"line": 98, "text": "        )"}
{"line": 99, "text": ""}
{"line": 100, "text": "    total_bytes = sum(len(x[1]) for x in accepted)"}
{"line": 101, "text": "    total_chars = sum(len(x[2]) for x in accepted)"}
{"line": 102, "text": "    total_words = sum(len(x[2].split()) for x in accepted)"}
{"line": 103, "text": ""}
{"line": 104, "text": "    print()"}
{"line": 105, "text": "    print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 106, "text": "    print(\"CORPUS ACCOUNTING\")"}
{"line": 107, "text": "    print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 108, "text": "    print(f\"Unique sources : {len(accepted)}\")"}
{"line": 109, "text": "    print(f\"Duplicate files: {duplicates}\")"}
{"line": 110, "text": "    print(f\"Bytes          : {total_bytes}\")"}
{"line": 111, "text": "    print(f\"Characters     : {total_chars}\")"}
{"line": 112, "text": "    print(f\"Words          : {total_words}\")"}
{"line": 113, "text": ""}
{"line": 114, "text": "    if total_chars < MIN_CHARS:"}
{"line": 115, "text": "        print()"}
{"line": 116, "text": "        print(\"[STATUS] INSUFFICIENT_REAL_CORPUS\")"}
{"line": 117, "text": "        print(f\"[REQUIRED] >= {MIN_CHARS:,} characters\")"}
{"line": 118, "text": "        print(f\"[ACTUAL]   {total_chars:,} characters\")"}
{"line": 119, "text": "        print()"}
{"line": 120, "text": "        print(\"No training corpus will be produced.\")"}
{"line": 121, "text": "        return 1"}
{"line": 122, "text": ""}
{"line": 123, "text": "    with OUTPUT.open(\"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as out:"}
{"line": 124, "text": "        for path, _, text in accepted:"}
{"line": 125, "text": "            out.write(f\"\\n\\n===== SOURCE: {path.relative_to(ROOT)} =====\\n\\n\")"}
{"line": 126, "text": "            out.write(text.rstrip())"}
{"line": 127, "text": "            out.write(\"\\n\")"}
{"line": 128, "text": ""}
{"line": 129, "text": "    print()"}
{"line": 130, "text": "    print(\"[STATUS] CORPUS_READY\")"}
{"line": 131, "text": "    print(f\"[OUTPUT] {OUTPUT}\")"}
{"line": 132, "text": "    print(f\"[CHARS]  {total_chars:,}\")"}
{"line": 133, "text": "    print(f\"[WORDS]  {total_words:,}\")"}
{"line": 134, "text": ""}
{"line": 135, "text": "    return 0"}
{"line": 136, "text": ""}
{"line": 137, "text": "if __name__ == \"__main__\":"}
{"line": 138, "text": "    raise SystemExit(main())"}

================================================================================
FILE: bloom_corpus_clean.py

{"line": 1, "text": "#!/usr/bin/env python3"}
{"line": 2, "text": ""}
{"line": 3, "text": "from pathlib import Path"}
{"line": 4, "text": "import re"}
{"line": 5, "text": "import hashlib"}
{"line": 6, "text": ""}
{"line": 7, "text": "ROOT = Path.home() / \"BLOOM\""}
{"line": 8, "text": "RAW = ROOT / \"training_corpus.txt\""}
{"line": 9, "text": "CLEAN = ROOT / \"training_corpus_clean.txt\""}
{"line": 10, "text": ""}
{"line": 11, "text": "raw = RAW.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")"}
{"line": 12, "text": ""}
{"line": 13, "text": "blocks = re.split("}
{"line": 14, "text": "    r\"\\n\\n===== SOURCE: (.*?) =====\\n\\n\","}
{"line": 15, "text": "    raw,"}
{"line": 16, "text": ")"}
{"line": 17, "text": ""}
{"line": 18, "text": "records = []"}
{"line": 19, "text": ""}
{"line": 20, "text": "for i in range(1, len(blocks), 2):"}
{"line": 21, "text": "    source = blocks[i]"}
{"line": 22, "text": "    content = blocks[i + 1].strip()"}
{"line": 23, "text": ""}
{"line": 24, "text": "    if not content:"}
{"line": 25, "text": "        continue"}
{"line": 26, "text": ""}
{"line": 27, "text": "    records.append((source, content))"}
{"line": 28, "text": ""}
{"line": 29, "text": "def normalize(text):"}
{"line": 30, "text": "    text = text.replace(\"\\x00\", \" \")"}
{"line": 31, "text": "    text = re.sub(r\"[ \\t]+\", \" \", text)"}
{"line": 32, "text": "    text = re.sub(r\"\\n{3,}\", \"\\n\\n\", text)"}
{"line": 33, "text": "    return text.strip()"}
{"line": 34, "text": ""}
{"line": 35, "text": "seen = set()"}
{"line": 36, "text": "accepted = []"}
{"line": 37, "text": ""}
{"line": 38, "text": "for source, content in records:"}
{"line": 39, "text": "    content = normalize(content)"}
{"line": 40, "text": ""}
{"line": 41, "text": "    digest = hashlib.sha256(content.encode(\"utf-8\")).hexdigest()"}
{"line": 42, "text": ""}
{"line": 43, "text": "    if digest in seen:"}
{"line": 44, "text": "        continue"}
{"line": 45, "text": ""}
{"line": 46, "text": "    seen.add(digest)"}
{"line": 47, "text": "    accepted.append((source, content))"}
{"line": 48, "text": ""}
{"line": 49, "text": "with CLEAN.open(\"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:"}
{"line": 50, "text": "    for source, content in accepted:"}
{"line": 51, "text": "        f.write(f\"===== SOURCE: {source} =====\\n\")"}
{"line": 52, "text": "        f.write(content)"}
{"line": 53, "text": "        f.write(\"\\n\\n\")"}
{"line": 54, "text": ""}
{"line": 55, "text": "text = CLEAN.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")"}
{"line": 56, "text": ""}
{"line": 57, "text": "words = re.findall("}
{"line": 58, "text": "    r\"\\b[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_'-]*\\b\","}
{"line": 59, "text": "    text"}
{"line": 60, "text": ")"}
{"line": 61, "text": ""}
{"line": 62, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 63, "text": "print(\"BLOOM CLEAN CORPUS\")"}
{"line": 64, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 65, "text": "print(f\"Raw sources       : {len(records):,}\")"}
{"line": 66, "text": "print(f\"Unique sources    : {len(accepted):,}\")"}
{"line": 67, "text": "print(f\"Characters        : {len(text):,}\")"}
{"line": 68, "text": "print(f\"Words             : {len(words):,}\")"}
{"line": 69, "text": "print(f\"Bytes             : {CLEAN.stat().st_size:,}\")"}
{"line": 70, "text": "print(f\"SHA256            : {hashlib.sha256(text.encode()).hexdigest()}\")"}
{"line": 71, "text": ""}
{"line": 72, "text": "print()"}
{"line": 73, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 74, "text": "print(\"STATUS\")"}
{"line": 75, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 76, "text": ""}
{"line": 77, "text": "if len(words) >= 10000:"}
{"line": 78, "text": "    print(\"CORPUS_STATUS: STRONG\")"}
{"line": 79, "text": "elif len(words) >= 1000:"}
{"line": 80, "text": "    print(\"CORPUS_STATUS: USABLE\")"}
{"line": 81, "text": "else:"}
{"line": 82, "text": "    print(\"CORPUS_STATUS: SMALL\")"}
{"line": 83, "text": ""}
{"line": 84, "text": "print(f\"OUTPUT: {CLEAN}\")"}
{"line": 85, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}

{"line": 2, "text": ""}
{"line": 3, "text": "from pathlib import Path"}
{"line": 4, "text": "from collections import Counter"}
{"line": 5, "text": "import re"}
{"line": 6, "text": "import math"}
{"line": 7, "text": ""}
{"line": 8, "text": "ROOT = Path.home() / \"BLOOM\""}
{"line": 9, "text": "CORPUS = ROOT / \"training_corpus_clean.txt\""}
{"line": 10, "text": ""}
{"line": 11, "text": "text = CORPUS.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")"}
{"line": 12, "text": ""}
{"line": 13, "text": "blocks = re.split(r\"===== SOURCE: (.*?) =====\\n\", text)"}
{"line": 14, "text": ""}
{"line": 15, "text": "records = []"}
{"line": 16, "text": "for i in range(1, len(blocks), 2):"}
{"line": 17, "text": "    source = blocks[i]"}
{"line": 18, "text": "    content = blocks[i + 1].strip()"}
{"line": 19, "text": "    records.append((source, content))"}
{"line": 20, "text": ""}
{"line": 21, "text": "def tokenize(s):"}
{"line": 22, "text": "    return re.findall(r\"\\b[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_'-]*\\b\", s.lower())"}
{"line": 23, "text": ""}
{"line": 24, "text": "code_ext = {\".py\", \".sh\", \".json\", \".jsonl\"}"}
{"line": 25, "text": "prose_ext = {\".txt\", \".log\"}"}
{"line": 26, "text": ""}
{"line": 27, "text": "total_words = 0"}
{"line": 28, "text": "code_words = 0"}
{"line": 29, "text": "prose_words = 0"}
{"line": 30, "text": "source_stats = []"}
{"line": 31, "text": ""}
{"line": 32, "text": "for source, content in records:"}
{"line": 33, "text": "    w = tokenize(content)"}
{"line": 34, "text": "    n = len(w)"}
{"line": 35, "text": "    total_words += n"}
{"line": 36, "text": ""}
{"line": 37, "text": "    ext = Path(source).suffix.lower()"}
{"line": 38, "text": ""}
{"line": 39, "text": "    if ext in code_ext:"}
{"line": 40, "text": "        code_words += n"}
{"line": 41, "text": "        kind = \"CODE/DATA\""}
{"line": 42, "text": "    else:"}
{"line": 43, "text": "        prose_words += n"}
{"line": 44, "text": "        kind = \"TEXT/LOG\""}
{"line": 45, "text": ""}
{"line": 46, "text": "    source_stats.append((source, n, kind))"}
{"line": 47, "text": ""}
{"line": 48, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 49, "text": "print(\"BLOOM CORPUS INTEGRITY AUDIT\")"}
{"line": 50, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 51, "text": ""}
{"line": 52, "text": "print(f\"Total words       : {total_words:,}\")"}
{"line": 53, "text": "print(f\"Code/data words   : {code_words:,}\")"}
{"line": 54, "text": "print(f\"Text/log words    : {prose_words:,}\")"}
{"line": 55, "text": "print(f\"Code/data share   : {code_words/total_words:.2%}\")"}
{"line": 56, "text": "print(f\"Text/log share    : {prose_words/total_words:.2%}\")"}
{"line": 57, "text": ""}
{"line": 58, "text": "print()"}
{"line": 59, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 60, "text": "print(\"LARGEST SOURCES\")"}
{"line": 61, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 62, "text": ""}
{"line": 63, "text": "for source, n, kind in sorted(source_stats, key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)[:20]:"}
{"line": 64, "text": "    print(f\"{n:8,d} | {kind:10s} | {source}\")"}
{"line": 65, "text": ""}
{"line": 66, "text": "print()"}
{"line": 67, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 68, "text": "print(\"SEMANTIC SIGNAL\")"}
{"line": 69, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 70, "text": ""}
{"line": 71, "text": "# Technical/semantic terms expected to carry meaning beyond syntax."}
{"line": 72, "text": "semantic_terms = {"}
{"line": 73, "text": "    \"reason\",\"because\",\"therefore\",\"however\",\"although\","}
{"line": 74, "text": "    \"goal\",\"purpose\",\"cause\",\"effect\",\"decision\",\"evidence\","}
{"line": 75, "text": "    \"learn\",\"learning\",\"knowledge\",\"memory\",\"context\","}
{"line": 76, "text": "    \"future\",\"risk\",\"market\",\"capital\",\"power\",\"honor\","}
{"line": 77, "text": "    \"truth\",\"false\",\"real\",\"novel\",\"pattern\",\"rule\","}
{"line": 78, "text": "    \"prediction\",\"result\",\"outcome\",\"strategy\",\"reasoning\","}
{"line": 79, "text": "    \"state\",\"change\",\"behavior\",\"system\",\"model\""}
{"line": 80, "text": "}"}
{"line": 81, "text": ""}
{"line": 82, "text": "counts = Counter(tokenize(text))"}
{"line": 83, "text": ""}
{"line": 84, "text": "semantic_hits = sum(counts[x] for x in semantic_terms)"}
{"line": 85, "text": ""}
{"line": 86, "text": "print(f\"Semantic anchor hits : {semantic_hits:,}\")"}
{"line": 87, "text": "print(f\"Anchor density       : {semantic_hits/total_words:.4%}\")"}
{"line": 88, "text": ""}
{"line": 89, "text": "print()"}
{"line": 90, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 91, "text": "print(\"REPETITION PRESSURE\")"}
{"line": 92, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 93, "text": ""}
{"line": 94, "text": "top = counts.most_common(20)"}
{"line": 95, "text": "top20_words = sum(c for _, c in top)"}
{"line": 96, "text": ""}
{"line": 97, "text": "print(f\"Top 20 token volume  : {top20_words:,}\")"}
{"line": 98, "text": "print(f\"Top 20 share         : {top20_words/total_words:.2%}\")"}
{"line": 99, "text": ""}
{"line": 100, "text": "entropy = 0.0"}
{"line": 101, "text": "for c in counts.values():"}
{"line": 102, "text": "    p = c / total_words"}
{"line": 103, "text": "    entropy -= p * math.log2(p)"}
{"line": 104, "text": ""}
{"line": 105, "text": "print(f\"Lexical entropy      : {entropy:.6f}\")"}
{"line": 106, "text": ""}
{"line": 107, "text": "print()"}
{"line": 108, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 109, "text": "print(\"VERDICT\")"}
{"line": 110, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 111, "text": ""}
{"line": 112, "text": "if code_words / total_words > 0.70:"}
{"line": 113, "text": "    print(\"STATUS: CODE_HEAVY\")"}
{"line": 114, "text": "    print(\"WARNING: Corpus is dominated by code/data.\")"}
{"line": 115, "text": "elif semantic_hits / total_words < 0.01:"}
{"line": 116, "text": "    print(\"STATUS: LOW_SEMANTIC_DENSITY\")"}
{"line": 117, "text": "else:"}
{"line": 118, "text": "    print(\"STATUS: SEMANTICALLY_USEFUL\")"}
{"line": 119, "text": ""}
{"line": 120, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}

FILE: bloom_corpus_profile.py

{"line": 1, "text": "#!/usr/bin/env python3"}
{"line": 2, "text": ""}
{"line": 3, "text": "from pathlib import Path"}
{"line": 4, "text": "from collections import Counter"}
{"line": 5, "text": "import re"}
{"line": 6, "text": "import math"}
{"line": 7, "text": ""}
{"line": 8, "text": "ROOT = Path.home() / \"BLOOM\""}
{"line": 9, "text": "CORPUS = ROOT / \"training_corpus.txt\""}
{"line": 10, "text": ""}
{"line": 11, "text": "text = CORPUS.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")"}
{"line": 12, "text": ""}
{"line": 13, "text": "sources = re.findall(r\"===== SOURCE: (.*?) =====\", text)"}
{"line": 14, "text": ""}
{"line": 15, "text": "body_parts = re.split(r\"===== SOURCE: .*? =====\", text)"}
{"line": 16, "text": "body = \"\\n\".join(body_parts)"}
{"line": 17, "text": ""}
{"line": 18, "text": "words = re.findall(r\"\\b[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_'-]*\\b\", body)"}
{"line": 19, "text": "lower = [w.lower() for w in words]"}
{"line": 20, "text": ""}
{"line": 21, "text": "counts = Counter(lower)"}
{"line": 22, "text": ""}
{"line": 23, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 24, "text": "print(\"BLOOM CORPUS PROFILE\")"}
{"line": 25, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 26, "text": ""}
{"line": 27, "text": "print(f\"Characters       : {len(text):,}\")"}
{"line": 28, "text": "print(f\"Words            : {len(words):,}\")"}
{"line": 29, "text": "print(f\"Sources          : {len(sources):,}\")"}
{"line": 30, "text": "print(f\"Unique words     : {len(counts):,}\")"}
{"line": 31, "text": "print(f\"Vocabulary ratio : {len(counts)/max(1,len(words)):.6f}\")"}
{"line": 32, "text": ""}
{"line": 33, "text": "print()"}
{"line": 34, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 35, "text": "print(\"TOP VOCABULARY\")"}
{"line": 36, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 37, "text": ""}
{"line": 38, "text": "for word, count in counts.most_common(50):"}
{"line": 39, "text": "    print(f\"{count:7d} | {word}\")"}
{"line": 40, "text": ""}
{"line": 41, "text": "print()"}
{"line": 42, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 43, "text": "print(\"SOURCE TYPES\")"}
{"line": 44, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 45, "text": ""}
{"line": 46, "text": "types = Counter()"}
{"line": 47, "text": ""}
{"line": 48, "text": "for source in sources:"}
{"line": 49, "text": "    suffix = Path(source).suffix.lower() or \"[none]\""}
{"line": 50, "text": "    types[suffix] += 1"}
{"line": 51, "text": ""}
{"line": 52, "text": "for suffix, count in types.most_common():"}
{"line": 53, "text": "    print(f\"{suffix:10s} | {count}\")"}
{"line": 54, "text": ""}
{"line": 55, "text": "print()"}
{"line": 56, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 57, "text": "print(\"DIVERSITY\")"}
{"line": 58, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 59, "text": ""}
{"line": 60, "text": "total = len(words)"}
{"line": 61, "text": ""}
{"line": 62, "text": "if total:"}
{"line": 63, "text": "    entropy = -sum("}
{"line": 64, "text": "        (c / total) * math.log2(c / total)"}
{"line": 65, "text": "        for c in counts.values()"}
{"line": 66, "text": "    )"}
{"line": 67, "text": "else:"}
{"line": 68, "text": "    entropy = 0.0"}
{"line": 69, "text": ""}
{"line": 70, "text": "print(f\"Lexical entropy : {entropy:.6f} bits\")"}
{"line": 71, "text": "print(f\"Hapax words     : {sum(1 for c in counts.values() if c == 1):,}\")"}
{"line": 72, "text": "print(f\"Repeated words  : {sum(1 for c in counts.values() if c > 1):,}\")"}
{"line": 73, "text": ""}
{"line": 74, "text": "print()"}
{"line": 75, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 76, "text": "print(\"BLOOM CORPUS PROFILE COMPLETE\")"}
{"line": 77, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}

================================================================================
FILE: bloom_dataset_validation.py
================================================================================
{"line": 1, "text": "#!/usr/bin/env python3"}
{"line": 2, "text": ""}
{"line": 3, "text": "from pathlib import Path"}
{"line": 4, "text": "from collections import Counter"}
{"line": 5, "text": "import hashlib"}
{"line": 6, "text": "import re"}
{"line": 7, "text": "import math"}
{"line": 8, "text": ""}
{"line": 9, "text": "ROOT = Path.home() / \"BLOOM\""}
{"line": 10, "text": "TRAIN = ROOT / \"bloom_train_v2.txt\""}
{"line": 11, "text": "VALID = ROOT / \"bloom_valid_v2.txt\""}
{"line": 12, "text": ""}
{"line": 13, "text": "train = TRAIN.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")"}
{"line": 14, "text": "valid = VALID.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")"}
{"line": 15, "text": ""}
{"line": 16, "text": "def tokenize(text):"}
{"line": 17, "text": "    return re.findall("}
{"line": 18, "text": "        r\"\\b[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_'-]*\\b\","}
{"line": 19, "text": "        text.lower()"}
{"line": 20, "text": "    )"}
{"line": 21, "text": ""}
{"line": 22, "text": "train_words = tokenize(train)"}
{"line": 23, "text": "valid_words = tokenize(valid)"}
{"line": 24, "text": ""}
{"line": 25, "text": "train_set = set(train_words)"}
{"line": 26, "text": "valid_set = set(valid_words)"}
{"line": 27, "text": ""}
{"line": 28, "text": "overlap = train_set & valid_set"}
{"line": 29, "text": "novel_valid = valid_set - train_set"}
{"line": 30, "text": ""}
{"line": 31, "text": "train_counts = Counter(train_words)"}
{"line": 32, "text": ""}
{"line": 33, "text": "entropy = 0.0"}
{"line": 34, "text": "if train_words:"}
{"line": 35, "text": "    total = len(train_words)"}
{"line": 36, "text": "    for count in train_counts.values():"}
{"line": 37, "text": "        p = count / total"}
{"line": 38, "text": "        entropy -= p * math.log2(p)"}
{"line": 39, "text": ""}
{"line": 40, "text": "train_lines = ["}
{"line": 41, "text": "    x.strip()"}
{"line": 42, "text": "    for x in train.splitlines()"}
{"line": 43, "text": "    if x.strip()"}
{"line": 44, "text": "]"}
{"line": 45, "text": ""}
{"line": 46, "text": "valid_lines = ["}
{"line": 47, "text": "    x.strip()"}
{"line": 48, "text": "    for x in valid.splitlines()"}
{"line": 49, "text": "    if x.strip()"}
{"line": 50, "text": "]"}
{"line": 51, "text": ""}
{"line": 52, "text": "train_hashes = {"}
{"line": 53, "text": "    hashlib.sha256("}
{"line": 54, "text": "        x.lower().encode(\"utf-8\")"}
{"line": 55, "text": "    ).hexdigest()"}
{"line": 56, "text": "    for x in train_lines"}
{"line": 57, "text": "}"}
{"line": 58, "text": ""}
{"line": 59, "text": "valid_hashes = {"}
{"line": 60, "text": "    hashlib.sha256("}
{"line": 61, "text": "        x.lower().encode(\"utf-8\")"}
{"line": 62, "text": "    ).hexdigest()"}
{"line": 63, "text": "    for x in valid_lines"}
{"line": 64, "text": "}"}
{"line": 65, "text": ""}
{"line": 66, "text": "exact_overlap = train_hashes & valid_hashes"}
{"line": 67, "text": ""}

FILE: bloom_english.py

{"line": 1, "text": "import requests, numpy as np"}
{"line": 2, "text": "from pathlib import Path"}
{"line": 3, "text": ""}
{"line": 4, "text": "# 1. ENGLISH DATA - not Shakespeare"}
{"line": 5, "text": "# TinyStories + your voice = modern English"}
{"line": 6, "text": "url = \"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/roneneldan/TinyStories/master/TinyStories_all_data/valid.txt\""}
{"line": 7, "text": "try:"}
{"line": 8, "text": "    txt = requests.get(url, timeout=10).text[:400000]"}
{"line": 9, "text": "    print(\"TinyStories English loaded\")"}
{"line": 10, "text": "except:"}
{"line": 11, "text": "    txt = (Path(\"my_texts.txt\").read_text()+\"\\n\")*200 if Path(\"my_texts.txt\").exists() else \"\""}
{"line": 12, "text": "    txt += \"The meaning of life is to bloom. BLOOM is the best LLM on Termux. I live in Lovejoy, Georgia. The computer learns English. \" * 5000"}
{"line": 13, "text": "    print(\"Using my_texts.txt x200 + English seed\")"}
{"line": 14, "text": ""}
{"line": 15, "text": "txt = txt + \"\\n\" + (Path(\"my_texts.txt\").read_text()*50 if Path(\"my_texts.txt\").exists() else \"\")"}
{"line": 16, "text": "chars=sorted(set(txt)); vs=len(chars); stoi={c:i for i,c in enumerate(chars)}; itos={i:c for i,c in enumerate(chars)}"}
{"line": 17, "text": "data=np.array([stoi[c] for c in txt])"}
{"line": 18, "text": "print(f\"ENGLISH vocab {vs} len {len(txt)}\")"}
{"line": 19, "text": ""}
{"line": 20, "text": "H=128; T=64; B=16"}
{"line": 21, "text": "Wxh=np.random.randn(vs,H)*0.01; Whh=np.random.randn(H,H)*0.01; Why=np.random.randn(H,vs)*0.01"}
{"line": 22, "text": "bh=np.zeros(H); by=np.zeros(vs)"}
{"line": 23, "text": "mWxh=np.zeros_like(Wxh); mWhh=np.zeros_like(Whh); mWhy=np.zeros_like(Why); mbh=np.zeros_like(bh); mby=np.zeros_like(by)"}
{"line": 24, "text": ""}
{"line": 25, "text": "def sample(h, seed, n=200):"}
{"line": 26, "text": "    x=np.zeros((vs,1)); x[seed]=1"}
{"line": 27, "text": "    out=\"\""}
{"line": 28, "text": "    for _ in range(n):"}
{"line": 29, "text": "        h=np.tanh(Wxh.T@x + Whh.T@h + bh[:,None])"}
{"line": 30, "text": "        y=Why.T@h + by[:,None]"}
{"line": 31, "text": "        p=np.exp(y-y.max()); p/=p.sum()"}
{"line": 32, "text": "        idx=np.random.choice(vs,p=p.ravel())"}
{"line": 33, "text": "        x=np.zeros((vs,1)); x[idx]=1"}
{"line": 34, "text": "        out+=itos[idx]"}
{"line": 35, "text": "    return out"}
{"line": 36, "text": ""}
{"line": 37, "text": "it=0; best=10"}
{"line": 38, "text": "hprev=np.zeros((H,1))"}
{"line": 39, "text": "while True:"}
{"line": 40, "text": "    it+=1"}
{"line": 41, "text": "    idx=np.random.randint(0,len(data)-T-1)"}
{"line": 42, "text": "    xs=data[idx:idx+T]; ys=data[idx+1:idx+T+1]"}
{"line": 43, "text": "    xs_one=np.zeros((vs,T)); xs_one[xs,np.arange(T)]=1"}
{"line": 44, "text": "    # forward"}
{"line": 45, "text": "    hs={-1:hprev}; loss=0"}
{"line": 46, "text": "    for t in range(T):"}
{"line": 47, "text": "        hs[t]=np.tanh(Wxh.T@xs_one[:,t,None] + Whh.T@hs[t-1] + bh[:,None])"}
{"line": 48, "text": "    # loss + backward (truncated)"}
{"line": 49, "text": "    dWxh=np.zeros_like(Wxh); dWhh=np.zeros_like(Whh); dWhy=np.zeros_like(Why); dbh=np.zeros_like(bh); dby=np.zeros_like(by)"}
{"line": 50, "text": "    dhnext=np.zeros((H,1))"}
{"line": 51, "text": "    for t in reversed(range(T)):"}
{"line": 52, "text": "        y=Why.T@hs[t] + by[:,None]"}
{"line": 53, "text": "        p=np.exp(y-y.max()); p/=p.sum()"}
{"line": 54, "text": "        loss-=np.log(p[ys[t],0]+1e-8)"}
{"line": 55, "text": "        dy=p.copy(); dy[ys[t]]-=1"}
{"line": 56, "text": "        dWhy+=hs[t]@dy.T; dby+=dy.ravel()"}
{"line": 57, "text": "        dh=Why@dy + dhnext"}
{"line": 58, "text": "        dhraw=(1-hs[t]**2)*dh"}
{"line": 59, "text": "        dbh+=dhraw.ravel(); dWxh+=xs_one[:,t,None]@dhraw.T; dWhh+=hs[t-1]@dhraw.T"}
{"line": 60, "text": "        dhnext=Whh@dhraw"}
{"line": 61, "text": "    loss/=T"}
{"line": 62, "text": "    # adam-ish"}
{"line": 63, "text": "    for p,d,m in [(Wxh,dWxh,mWxh),(Whh,dWhh,mWhh),(Why,dWhy,mWhy)]:"}
{"line": 64, "text": "        m[:]=0.9*m+0.1*d; p-=0.001*m"}
{"line": 65, "text": "    bh-=0.001*dbh; by-=0.001*dby"}
{"line": 66, "text": "    hprev=hs[T-1]"}
{"line": 67, "text": "    if it%100==0:"}
{"line": 68, "text": "        print(f\"{it} loss {loss:.3f} best {best:.3f} ENGLISH\")"}
{"line": 69, "text": "        if loss<best:"}
{"line": 70, "text": "            best=loss"}
{"line": 71, "text": "            print(sample(hprev, stoi[txt[0]], 300)+\"\\n---\")"}

FILE: bloom_finance.py
================================================================================
{"line": 1, "text": "import json, random, hashlib, time, math"}
{"line": 2, "text": "from pathlib import Path"}
{"line": 3, "text": "from collections import defaultdict"}
{"line": 4, "text": "Path(\"garden\").mkdir(exist_ok=True)"}
{"line": 5, "text": ""}
{"line": 6, "text": "# FORCE FRESH for finance - old bloom keys don't match"}
{"line": 7, "text": "seed={"}
{"line": 8, "text": "    \"root\":{\"mean\":0.0},"}
{"line": 9, "text": "    \"petals\":{"}
{"line": 10, "text": "        \"momentum\":{\"mean\":0.12},"}
{"line": 11, "text": "        \"liquidity\":{\"mean\":0.12},"}
{"line": 12, "text": "        \"value\":{\"mean\":0.12},"}
{"line": 13, "text": "        \"volatility\":{\"mean\":-0.12}"}
{"line": 14, "text": "    },"}
{"line": 15, "text": "    \"gen\":0,\"id\":hashlib.sha256(b\"fin-gen0\").hexdigest()[:6],"}
{"line": 16, "text": "    \"total_steps\":0"}
{"line": 17, "text": "}"}
{"line": 18, "text": "capital, total = 100.0, 0"}
{"line": 19, "text": "# try load finance seed only"}
{"line": 20, "text": "try:"}
{"line": 21, "text": "    s=json.load(open(\"garden/seedling.json\"))"}
{"line": 22, "text": "    if \"momentum\" in s[\"petals\"]:"}
{"line": 23, "text": "        seed, capital, total = s, json.load(open(\"garden/dew.json\"))[\"dew\"], s.get(\"total_steps\",0)"}
{"line": 24, "text": "except:"}
{"line": 25, "text": "    pass"}
{"line": 26, "text": ""}
{"line": 27, "text": "def market_regime(step):"}
{"line": 28, "text": "    regime = (step // 20) % 3"}
{"line": 29, "text": "    if regime==0:"}
{"line": 30, "text": "        return {\"momentum\":random.uniform(6,10),\"liquidity\":random.uniform(2,6),\"value\":random.uniform(2,6),\"volatility\":random.uniform(0,4)}, regime"}
{"line": 31, "text": "    elif regime==1:"}
{"line": 32, "text": "        return {\"momentum\":random.uniform(2,6),\"liquidity\":random.uniform(6,10),\"value\":random.uniform(2,6),\"volatility\":random.uniform(0,4)}, regime"}
{"line": 33, "text": "    else:"}
{"line": 34, "text": "        return {\"momentum\":random.uniform(0,4),\"liquidity\":random.uniform(0,4),\"value\":random.uniform(0,4),\"volatility\":random.uniform(6,10)}, regime"}
{"line": 35, "text": ""}
{"line": 36, "text": "def vigor(sig, sd): return sum(sig[k]*sd[\"petals\"][k][\"mean\"] for k in sig) + sd[\"root\"][\"mean\"]"}
{"line": 37, "text": "def sigmoid(x): return 1/(1+math.exp(-x))"}
{"line": 38, "text": ""}
{"line": 39, "text": "hits, lifespan = [], 0"}
{"line": 40, "text": "for _ in range(20):"}
{"line": 41, "text": "    if capital <= 0:"}
{"line": 42, "text": "        print(f\"BANKRUPT step {total} cap {capital:.1f} gen {seed['gen']} vol {seed['petals']['volatility']['mean']:.2f}\")"}
{"line": 43, "text": "        with open(\"garden/lineage.jsonl\",\"a\") as f:"}
{"line": 44, "text": "            f.write(json.dumps({\"id\":seed[\"id\"],\"gen\":seed[\"gen\"],\"lifespan\":lifespan,\"capital\":capital,\"total\":total})+\"\\n\")"}
{"line": 45, "text": "        seed[\"gen\"]+=1"}
{"line": 46, "text": "        seed[\"id\"]=hashlib.sha256(f\"{seed['id']}{time.time()}\".encode()).hexdigest()[:6]"}
{"line": 47, "text": "        for k in seed[\"petals\"]:"}
{"line": 48, "text": "            seed[\"petals\"][k][\"mean\"] = seed[\"petals\"][k][\"mean\"]*0.5 + random.gauss(0,0.2)"}
{"line": 49, "text": "            if k==\"volatility\": seed[\"petals\"][k][\"mean\"] -= 0.3"}
{"line": 50, "text": "        seed[\"root\"][\"mean\"]*=0.5"}
{"line": 51, "text": "        capital, lifespan = 100.0, 0"}
{"line": 52, "text": "        print(f\" EVOLVED fund gen {seed['gen']} vol {seed['petals']['volatility']['mean']:.2f}\")"}
{"line": 53, "text": ""}
{"line": 54, "text": "    sig, regime = market_regime(total)"}
{"line": 55, "text": "    prob = sigmoid(vigor(sig, seed))"}
{"line": 56, "text": "    will_long = prob > 0.5"}
{"line": 57, "text": "    true_long = (sig[\"momentum\"]+sig[\"liquidity\"]+sig[\"value\"]) > sig[\"volatility\"]*2.2"}
{"line": 58, "text": "    did_long = true_long if random.random()<0.78 else not true_long"}
{"line": 59, "text": "    hit = will_long==did_long"}
{"line": 60, "text": "    hits.append(hit); lifespan+=1"}
{"line": 61, "text": "    capital = capital - 1.1 + (1.0 if hit and will_long else -1.4 if not hit else 0.0)"}
{"line": 62, "text": ""}
{"line": 63, "text": "    with open(\"garden/petals.jsonl\",\"a\") as f:"}
{"line": 64, "text": "        f.write(json.dumps({\"total\":total,\"gen\":seed[\"gen\"],\"regime\":regime,\"hit\":hit,\"capital\":round(capital,1)})+\"\\n\")"}
{"line": 65, "text": ""}
{"line": 66, "text": "    error = (1.0 if did_long else 0.0) - prob"}
{"line": 67, "text": "    lr=0.05"}
{"line": 68, "text": "    for k in sig:"}
{"line": 69, "text": "        seed[\"petals\"][k][\"mean\"] = seed[\"petals\"][k][\"mean\"]*0.99 + lr*error*sig[k]"}
{"line": 70, "text": "    seed[\"root\"][\"mean\"] = seed[\"root\"][\"mean\"]*0.99 + lr*error"}
{"line": 71, "text": "    total+=1"}
{"line": 72, "text": ""}
{"line": 73, "text": "seed[\"total_steps\"]=total"}
{"line": 74, "text": "with open(\"garden/seedling.json\",\"w\") as f: json.dump(seed,f,indent=2)"}
{"line": 75, "text": "with open(\"garden/dew.json\",\"w\") as f: json.dump({\"dew\":capital},f,indent=2)"}
{"line": 76, "text": ""}
{"line": 77, "text": "all_p=[json.loads(l) for l in open(\"garden/petals.jsonl\")]"}
{"line": 78, "text": "by=defaultdict(list)"}
{"line": 79, "text": "for p in all_p: by[p[\"regime\"]].append(p[\"hit\"])"}
{"line": 80, "text": "print(f\"\\nFINANCE total {len(all_p)} gen {seed['gen']} cap {capital:.1f}\")"}
{"line": 81, "text": "for r in sorted(by):"}
{"line": 82, "text": "    label={0:\"bull\",1:\"sideways\",2:\"crash\"}[r]"}
{"line": 83, "text": "    print(f\" Regime {r} {label}: {sum(by[r])/len(by[r]):.2f} n={len(by[r])}\")"}

{"line": 1, "text": "import requests, numpy as np"}
{"line": 2, "text": "from pathlib import Path"}
{"line": 3, "text": "Path(\"bloom_frontier\").mkdir(exist_ok=True)"}
{"line": 4, "text": ""}
{"line": 5, "text": "txt=requests.get(\"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/karpathy/char-rnn/master/data/tinyshakespeare/input.txt\",timeout=15).text"}
{"line": 6, "text": "try:"}
{"line": 7, "text": "    txt+=\"\\n\"+Path(\"my_texts.txt\").read_text()"}
{"line": 8, "text": "    print(\"added my_texts.txt\")"}
{"line": 9, "text": "except: pass"}
{"line": 10, "text": "chars=sorted(list(set(txt))); vs=len(chars)"}
{"line": 11, "text": "stoi={c:i for i,c in enumerate(chars)}; itos={i:c for i,c in enumerate(chars)}"}
{"line": 12, "text": "data=np.array([stoi[c] for c in txt],dtype=np.int32)"}
{"line": 13, "text": "print(f\"FRONTIER vocab {vs} len {len(txt)}\")"}
{"line": 14, "text": ""}
{"line": 15, "text": "# --- hyper ---"}
{"line": 16, "text": "B=16; T=64; H=128; n_layer=2; lr=3e-4"}
{"line": 17, "text": "nh=4; hs=H//nh"}
{"line": 18, "text": ""}
{"line": 19, "text": "# init"}
{"line": 20, "text": "def rand(*s): return np.random.randn(*s)*0.02"}
{"line": 21, "text": "Wte=rand(vs,H); Wpe=rand(T,H)"}
{"line": 22, "text": "layers=[]"}
{"line": 23, "text": "for _ in range(n_layer):"}
{"line": 24, "text": "    layers.append(dict("}
{"line": 25, "text": "        ln1_g=np.ones(H), ln1_b=np.zeros(H),"}
{"line": 26, "text": "        Wq=rand(H,H), Wk=rand(H,H), Wv=rand(H,H), Wo=rand(H,H),"}
{"line": 27, "text": "        ln2_g=np.ones(H), ln2_b=np.zeros(H),"}
{"line": 28, "text": "        W1=rand(H,4*H), b1=np.zeros(4*H),"}
{"line": 29, "text": "        W2=rand(4*H,H), b2=np.zeros(H)"}
{"line": 30, "text": "    ))"}
{"line": 31, "text": "lnf_g=np.ones(H); lnf_b=np.zeros(H)"}
{"line": 32, "text": "Why=rand(H,vs); by=np.zeros(vs)"}
{"line": 33, "text": ""}
{"line": 34, "text": "# adam"}
{"line": 35, "text": "params=[]"}
{"line": 36, "text": "for p in [Wte,Wpe]: params.append(p)"}
{"line": 37, "text": "for l in layers:"}
{"line": 38, "text": "    for k in ['ln1_g','ln1_b','Wq','Wk','Wv','Wo','ln2_g','ln2_b','W1','b1','W2','b2']: params.append(l[k])"}
{"line": 39, "text": "params+=[lnf_g,lnf_b,Why,by]"}
{"line": 40, "text": "m=[np.zeros_like(p) for p in params]; v=[np.zeros_like(p) for p in params]"}
{"line": 41, "text": ""}
{"line": 42, "text": "def ln_fwd(x,g,b,eps=1e-5):"}
{"line": 43, "text": "    mean=x.mean(-1,keepdims=True); var=((x-mean)**2).mean(-1,keepdims=True)"}
{"line": 44, "text": "    std=np.sqrt(var+eps); xn=(x-mean)/std; out=xn*g+b"}
{"line": 45, "text": "    return out,(x,xn,mean,std,g)"}
{"line": 46, "text": "def ln_bwd(dout,cache):"}
{"line": 47, "text": "    x,xn,mean,std,g=cache"}
{"line": 48, "text": "    dxn=dout*g"}
{"line": 49, "text": "    dvar=np.sum(dxn*(x-mean)*-0.5*std**-3,axis=-1,keepdims=True)"}
{"line": 50, "text": "    dmean=np.sum(dxn*-1/std,axis=-1,keepdims=True)+dvar*np.mean(-2*(x-mean),axis=-1,keepdims=True)"}
{"line": 51, "text": "    dx=dxn/std + dvar*2*(x-mean)/H + dmean/H"}
{"line": 52, "text": "    dg=np.sum(dout*xn,axis=(0,1)); db=np.sum(dout,axis=(0,1))"}
{"line": 53, "text": "    return dx,dg,db"}
{"line": 54, "text": ""}
{"line": 55, "text": "def gelu(x): return 0.5*x*(1+np.tanh(np.sqrt(2/np.pi)*(x+0.044715*x**3)))"}
{"line": 56, "text": "def gelu_bwd(dout,x):"}
{"line": 57, "text": "    # approx derivative"}
{"line": 58, "text": "    c=np.sqrt(2/np.pi); c3=0.044715"}
{"line": 59, "text": "    tanh_arg=c*(x+c3*x**3); tanh_out=np.tanh(tanh_arg)"}
{"line": 60, "text": "    left=0.5*(1+tanh_out); right=0.5*x*(1-tanh_out**2)*c*(1+3*c3*x**2)"}
{"line": 61, "text": "    return dout*(left+right)"}
{"line": 62, "text": ""}
{"line": 63, "text": "mask = np.full((T,T), -1e10); mask = np.triu(mask, k=1) # causal"}
{"line": 64, "text": ""}
{"line": 65, "text": "def forward(ids): # ids (B,T)"}
{"line": 66, "text": "    B,Tt=ids.shape"}
{"line": 67, "text": "    cache={}"}
{"line": 68, "text": "    x = Wte[ids] + Wpe[:Tt][None,:,:] # (B,T,H)"}
{"line": 69, "text": "    cache['x0']=x"}
{"line": 70, "text": "    for li,l in enumerate(layers):"}
{"line": 71, "text": "        # ln1"}
{"line": 72, "text": "        xl1,c1=ln_fwd(x,l['ln1_g'],l['ln1_b']); cache[f'ln1_{li}']=c1"}
{"line": 73, "text": "        Q=xl1@l['Wq']; K=xl1@l['Wk']; V=xl1@l['Wv']"}
{"line": 74, "text": "        cache[f'Q_{li}']=Q; cache[f'K_{li}']=K; cache[f'V_{li}']=V; cache[f'xl1_{li}']=xl1"}
{"line": 75, "text": "        # attention B,T,H -> split heads"}
{"line": 76, "text": "        Qh=Q.reshape(B,Tt,nh,hs).transpose(0,2,1,3) # B,nh,T,hs"}
{"line": 77, "text": "        Kh=Qh*0; Kh=K.reshape(B,Tt,nh,hs).transpose(0,2,1,3)"}
{"line": 78, "text": "        Vh=V.reshape(B,Tt,nh,hs).transpose(0,2,1,3)"}
{"line": 79, "text": "        scores = (Qh @ Kh.transpose(0,1,3,2))/np.sqrt(hs) + mask[:Tt,:Tt] # B,nh,T,T"}
{"line": 80, "text": "        # softmax"}
{"line": 81, "text": "        smax=np.max(scores,axis=-1,keepdims=True); exp=np.exp(scores-smax)"}
{"line": 82, "text": "        probs=exp/np.sum(exp,axis=-1,keepdims=True)"}
{"line": 83, "text": "        cache[f'probs_{li}']=probs; cache[f'Qh_{li}']=Qh; cache[f'Kh_{li}']=Kh; cache[f'Vh_{li}']=Vh"}
{"line": 84, "text": "        attn = (probs @ Vh).transpose(0,2,1,3).reshape(B,Tt,H)"}
{"line": 85, "text": "        proj = attn @ l['Wo']; cache[f'attn_{li}']=attn"}
{"line": 86, "text": "        x = x + proj"}
{"line": 87, "text": "        # ln2 + mlp"}
{"line": 88, "text": "        xl2,c2=ln_fwd(x,l['ln2_g'],l['ln2_b']); cache[f'ln2_{li}']=c2; cache[f'x1_{li}']=x"}
{"line": 89, "text": "        fc1=xl2@l['W1']+l['b1']; fc1g=gelu(fc1)"}
{"line": 90, "text": "        fc2=fc1g@l['W2']+l['b2']"}
{"line": 91, "text": "        cache[f'xl2_{li}']=xl2; cache[f'fc1_{li}']=fc1; cache[f'fc1g_{li}']=fc1g"}
{"line": 92, "text": "        x = x + fc2"}
{"line": 93, "text": "    cache['x_last']=x"}
{"line": 94, "text": "    xf,cf=ln_fwd(x,lnf_g,lnf_b); cache['lnf']=cf"}
{"line": 95, "text": "    logits=xf@Why+by # B,T,vs"}
{"line": 96, "text": "    return logits,cache"}
{"line": 97, "text": ""}
{"line": 98, "text": "def sample(start=\"\\n\", n=300):"}
{"line": 99, "text": "    ids=np.array([[stoi[c] for c in start]],dtype=np.int32)"}
{"line": 100, "text": "    for _ in range(n):"}
{"line": 101, "text": "        cur=ids[:,-T:]; logits,_=forward(cur)"}
{"line": 102, "text": "        p=np.exp(logits[0,-1]-np.max(logits[0,-1])); p/=p.sum()"}
{"line": 103, "text": "        nxt=np.random.choice(vs,p=p); ids=np.concatenate([ids,[[nxt]]],axis=1)"}
{"line": 104, "text": "    return \"\".join(itos[i] for i in ids[0])"}
{"line": 105, "text": ""}
{"line": 106, "text": "it=0; best=10"}
{"line": 107, "text": "while True:"}
{"line": 108, "text": "    it+=1"}
{"line": 109, "text": "    idx=np.random.randint(0,len(data)-T-1,size=B)"}
{"line": 110, "text": "    xb=np.zeros((B,T),dtype=np.int32); yb=np.zeros((B,T),dtype=np.int32)"}
{"line": 111, "text": "    for b,i in enumerate(idx): xb[b]=data[i:i+T]; yb[b]=data[i+1:i+T+1]"}
{"line": 112, "text": "    logits,cache=forward(xb)"}
{"line": 113, "text": "    # loss"}
{"line": 114, "text": "    m_log=np.max(logits,axis=-1,keepdims=True); e=np.exp(logits-m_log); probs=e/np.sum(e,axis=-1,keepdims=True)"}
{"line": 115, "text": "    loss=-np.mean(np.log(probs[np.arange(B)[:,None],np.arange(T),yb]+1e-8))"}
{"line": 116, "text": "    # backward"}
{"line": 117, "text": "    dlogits=probs.copy(); dlogits[np.arange(B)[:,None],np.arange(T),yb]-=1; dlogits/=B*T"}
{"line": 118, "text": "    #... gradients dict"}
{"line": 119, "text": "    grads={}"}
{"line": 120, "text": "    # final layer"}
{"line": 121, "text": "    xf_cache=cache['lnf']; x_last=cache['x_last']"}
{"line": 122, "text": "    # lnf backward"}
{"line": 123, "text": "    dxf = dlogits @ Why.T # B,T,H"}
{"line": 124, "text": "    dWhy = x_last.transpose(0,2,1) @ dlogits # actually need sum"}
{"line": 125, "text": "    # simplify: accumulate"}
{"line": 126, "text": "    dWhy = np.einsum('bth,btv->hv', cache['x_last']*0 + xf_cache[1], dlogits) # use xn? approximate with xf"}
{"line": 127, "text": "    # for brevity: use exact but simple accum"}
{"line": 128, "text": "    # We'll do full backward loop"}
{"line": 129, "text": "    # --- quick manual backward for 2 layers ---"}
{"line": 130, "text": "    # d for Why,by"}
{"line": 131, "text": "    dWhy = np.einsum('bth,btv->hv', forward(xb)[0]*0+0, dlogits) # placeholder will be overwritten below"}
{"line": 132, "text": "    # RECOMPUTE proper grads with simple version (no layernorm grad for final for speed)"}
{"line": 133, "text": "    xf = ln_fwd(x_last,lnf_g,lnf_b)[0]"}
{"line": 134, "text": "    dWhy = np.einsum('bth,btv->hv', xf, dlogits)"}
{"line": 135, "text": "    dby = dlogits.sum(axis=(0,1))"}
{"line": 136, "text": "    dx = dlogits @ Why.T # B,T,H"}
{"line": 137, "text": "    dx,_dgf,_dbf = ln_bwd(dx, cache['lnf'])"}
{"line": 138, "text": "    # backward layers"}
{"line": 139, "text": "    dparams={}"}
{"line": 140, "text": "    # we will collect grads in order of params list"}
{"line": 141, "text": "    # for simplicity, zero grads and fill"}
{"line": 142, "text": "    for li in reversed(range(n_layer)):"}
{"line": 143, "text": "        l=layers[li]"}
{"line": 144, "text": "        # mlp backward"}
{"line": 145, "text": "        # x = x1 + fc2, so dx goes to both"}
{"line": 146, "text": "        dfc2 = dx # (B,T,H)"}
{"line": 147, "text": "        dW2 = np.einsum('bth,btv->hv', cache[f'fc1g_{li}'], dfc2) # 4H,H"}
{"line": 148, "text": "        db2 = dfc2.sum(axis=(0,1))"}
{"line": 149, "text": "        dfc1g = dfc2 @ l['W2'].T"}
{"line": 150, "text": "        dfc1 = gelu_bwd(dfc1g, cache[f'fc1_{li}'])"}
{"line": 151, "text": "        dW1 = np.einsum('bth,btv->hv', cache[f'xl2_{li}'], dfc1)"}
{"line": 152, "text": "        db1 = dfc1.sum(axis=(0,1))"}
{"line": 153, "text": "        dxl2 = dfc1 @ l['W1'].T"}
{"line": 154, "text": "        dx1_ln, dg2, db2_ln = ln_bwd(dxl2, cache[f'ln2_{li}'])"}
{"line": 155, "text": "        dx1 = dx + dx1_ln # residual from mlp"}
{"line": 156, "text": "        # attn backward"}
{"line": 157, "text": "        dproj = dx1"}
{"line": 158, "text": "        dWo = np.einsum('bth,btv->hv', cache[f'attn_{li}'], dproj)"}
{"line": 159, "text": "        dattn = dproj @ l['Wo'].T # B,T,H"}
{"line": 160, "text": "        # heads"}
{"line": 161, "text": "        dattn_h = dattn.reshape(B,T,nh,hs).transpose(0,2,1,3) # B,nh,T,hs"}
{"line": 162, "text": "        probs=cache[f'probs_{li}']; Vh=cache[f'Vh_{li}']; Qh=cache[f'Qh_{li}']; Kh=cache[f'Kh_{li}']"}
{"line": 163, "text": "        dVh = np.matmul(probs.transpose(0,1,3,2), dattn_h) # B,nh,T,hs (prob^T @ dattn)"}
{"line": 164, "text": "        dprobs = np.matmul(dattn_h, Vh.transpose(0,1,3,2)) # B,nh,T,T"}
{"line": 165, "text": "        # softmax backward"}
{"line": 166, "text": "        sum_dp = np.sum(dprobs*probs,axis=-1,keepdims=True)"}
{"line": 167, "text": "        dscores = probs*(dprobs - sum_dp)"}
{"line": 168, "text": "        dQh = np.matmul(dscores, Kh)/np.sqrt(hs)"}
{"line": 169, "text": "        dKh = np.matmul(dscores.transpose(0,1,3,2), Qh)/np.sqrt(hs)"}
{"line": 170, "text": "        # merge heads"}
{"line": 171, "text": "        dQ = dQh.transpose(0,2,1,3).reshape(B,T,H)"}
{"line": 172, "text": "        dK = dKh.transpose(0,2,1,3).reshape(B,T,H)"}
{"line": 173, "text": "        dV = dVh.transpose(0,2,1,3).reshape(B,T,H)"}
{"line": 174, "text": "        xl1=cache[f'xl1_{li}']"}
{"line": 175, "text": "        dWq = np.einsum('bth,btv->hv', xl1, dQ)"}
{"line": 176, "text": "        dWk = np.einsum('bth,btv->hv', xl1, dK)"}
{"line": 177, "text": "        dWv = np.einsum('bth,btv->hv', xl1, dV)"}
{"line": 178, "text": "        dxl1 = dQ@l['Wq'].T + dK@l['Wk'].T + dV@l['Wv'].T"}
{"line": 179, "text": "        dx0_ln, dg1, db1_ln = ln_bwd(dxl1, cache[f'ln1_{li}'])"}
{"line": 180, "text": "        dx = dx1 + dx0_ln # residual from attn"}
{"line": 181, "text": "        # store grads for this layer"}
{"line": 182, "text": "        dparams[f'{li}_W2']=dW2; dparams[f'{li}_b2']=db2"}
{"line": 183, "text": "        dparams[f'{li}_W1']=dW1; dparams[f'{li}_b1']=db1"}
{"line": 184, "text": "        dparams[f'{li}_Wo']=dWo; dparams[f'{li}_Wq']=dWq; dparams[f'{li}_Wk']=dWk; dparams[f'{li}_Wv']=dWv"}
{"line": 185, "text": "        dparams[f'{li}_ln2_g']=dg2; dparams[f'{li}_ln2_b']=db2_ln"}
{"line": 186, "text": "        dparams[f'{li}_ln1_g']=dg1; dparams[f'{li}_ln1_b']=db1_ln"}
{"line": 187, "text": "    # grads for embeddings"}
{"line": 188, "text": "    dWte=np.zeros_like(Wte); dWpe=np.zeros_like(Wpe)"}
{"line": 189, "text": "    # approximate: scatter add dx to Wte/Wpe"}
{"line": 190, "text": "    for b in range(B):"}
{"line": 191, "text": "        for t in range(T):"}
{"line": 192, "text": "            dWte[xb[b,t]]+=dx[b,t]"}
{"line": 193, "text": "            dWpe[t]+=dx[b,t]"}
{"line": 194, "text": "    # adam update"}
{"line": 195, "text": "    # build list in same order as params"}
{"line": 196, "text": "    glist=[dWte,dWpe]"}
{"line": 197, "text": "    for li in range(n_layer):"}
{"line": 198, "text": "        glist+=[dparams[f'{li}_ln1_g'],dparams[f'{li}_ln1_b'],dparams[f'{li}_Wq'],dparams[f'{li}_Wk'],dparams[f'{li}_Wv'],dparams[f'{li}_Wo'],dparams[f'{li}_ln2_g'],dparams[f'{li}_ln2_b'],dparams[f'{li}_W1'],dparams[f'{li}_b1'],dparams[f'{li}_W2'],dparams[f'{li}_b2']]"}
{"line": 199, "text": "    glist+=[_dgf,_dbf,dWhy,dby]"}
{"line": 200, "text": "    for i,(p,g) in enumerate(zip(params,glist)):"}
{"line": 201, "text": "        m[i]=0.9*m[i]+0.1*g; v[i]=0.999*v[i]+0.001*g*g"}
{"line": 202, "text": "        p-=lr*(m[i]/(1-0.9**it))/(np.sqrt(v[i]/(1-0.999**it))+1e-8)"}
{"line": 203, "text": "    if it%50==0:"}
{"line": 204, "text": "        print(f\"{it} loss {loss:.3f} best {best:.3f} FRONTIER {n_layer}L {H}H\")"}
{"line": 205, "text": "        if loss<best: best=loss; print(sample()[:400]); np.savez(\"bloom_frontier/W.npz\",Wte=Wte,Wpe=Wpe,Why=Why,by=by)"}

{"line": 493, "text": "# ================================================================"}
{"line": 494, "text": "# STATIC UNRESOLVED-NAME ANALYSIS"}
{"line": 495, "text": "# ================================================================"}
{"line": 496, "text": ""}
{"line": 497, "text": "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 80)"}
{"line": 498, "text": "print(\"16. STATIC UNRESOLVED NAME ANALYSIS\")"}
{"line": 499, "text": "print(\"=\" * 80)"}
{"line": 500, "text": ""}
{"line": 501, "text": "# This is deliberately conservative."}
{"line": 502, "text": "# It reports names read in top-level executable code that are not:"}
{"line": 503, "text": "#   - defined globally"}
{"line": 504, "text": "#   - imported"}
{"line": 505, "text": "#   - obvious Python/NumPy builtins"}
{"line": 506, "text": "#"}
{"line": 507, "text": "# It does NOT claim these are runtime failures."}
{"line": 508, "text": ""}
{"line": 509, "text": "imports = set()"}
{"line": 510, "text": ""}
{"line": 511, "text": "for node in ast.walk(tree):"}
{"line": 512, "text": ""}
{"line": 513, "text": "    if isinstance(node, ast.Import):"}
{"line": 514, "text": ""}
{"line": 515, "text": "        for alias in node.names:"}
{"line": 516, "text": "            imports.add("}
{"line": 517, "text": "                alias.asname or alias.name.split(\".\")[0]"}
{"line": 518, "text": "            )"}
{"line": 519, "text": ""}
{"line": 520, "text": "    elif isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom):"}
{"line": 521, "text": ""}
{"line": 522, "text": "        for alias in node.names:"}
{"line": 523, "text": "            imports.add("}
{"line": 524, "text": "                alias.asname or alias.name"}
{"line": 525, "text": "            )"}
{"line": 526, "text": ""}
{"line": 527, "text": "builtin_names = set(dir(__builtins__))"}
{"line": 528, "text": ""}
{"line": 529, "text": "obvious = {"}
{"line": 530, "text": "    \"np\","}
{"line": 531, "text": "    \"math\","}
{"line": 532, "text": "    \"random\","}
{"line": 533, "text": "    \"time\","}
{"line": 534, "text": "    \"sys\","}
{"line": 535, "text": "    \"pathlib\","}
{"line": 536, "text": "    \"glob\","}
{"line": 537, "text": "    \"pickle\","}
{"line": 538, "text": "    \"True\","}
{"line": 539, "text": "    \"False\","}
{"line": 540, "text": "    \"None\","}
{"line": 541, "text": "}"}
{"line": 542, "text": ""}
{"line": 543, "text": "unresolved = {}"}
{"line": 544, "text": ""}
{"line": 545, "text": "for node in tree.body:"}
{"line": 546, "text": ""}
{"line": 547, "text": "    if isinstance("}
{"line": 548, "text": "        node,"}
{"line": 549, "text": "        ("}
{"line": 550, "text": "            ast.FunctionDef,"}
{"line": 551, "text": "            ast.ClassDef,"}
{"line": 552, "text": "            ast.Import,"}
{"line": 553, "text": "            ast.ImportFrom,"}
{"line": 554, "text": "        )"}
{"line": 555, "text": "    ):"}
{"line": 556, "text": "        continue"}
{"line": 557, "text": ""}
{"line": 558, "text": "    for child in ast.walk(node):"}
{"line": 559, "text": ""}
{"line": 560, "text": "        if isinstance(child, ast.Name):"}
{"line": 561, "text": "            if isinstance(child.ctx, ast.Load):"}
{"line": 562, "text": ""}
{"line": 563, "text": "                name = child.id"}
{"line": 564, "text": ""}
{"line": 565, "text": "                if ("}
{"line": 566, "text": "                    name not in defined_globals"}
{"line": 567, "text": "                    and name not in imports"}
{"line": 568, "text": "                    and name not in builtin_names"}
{"line": 569, "text": "                    and name not in obvious"}
{"line": 570, "text": "                ):"}
{"line": 571, "text": "                    unresolved.setdefault("}
{"line": 572, "text": "                        name,"}
{"line": 573, "text": "                        child.lineno"}
{"line": 574, "text": "                    )"}
{"line": 575, "text": ""}
{"line": 576, "text": "if unresolved:"}
{"line": 577, "text": ""}
{"line": 578, "text": "    for name, line in sorted("}
{"line": 579, "text": "        unresolved.items(),"}
{"line": 580, "text": "        key=lambda x: x[1]"}
{"line": 581, "text": "    ):"}
{"line": 582, "text": "        print("}
{"line": 583, "text": "            f\"{line:5d}: {name}\""}
{"line": 584, "text": "        )"}
{"line": 585, "text": "else:"}
{"line": 586, "text": "    print(\"NONE\")"}
{"line": 587, "text": ""}
{"line": 588, "text": "# ================================================================"}
{"line": 589, "text": "# ACTUAL IMPORT TEST"}
{"line": 590, "text": "# ================================================================"}
{"line": 591, "text": "#"}
{"line": 592, "text": "# This is the first point where execution is allowed."}
{"line": 593, "text": "#"}
{"line": 594, "text": "# IMPORTANT:"}
{"line": 595, "text": "# bloom_real.py currently performs top-level model construction."}
{"line": 596, "text": "# Therefore this test is observational only. We do NOT alter sys.modules,"}
{"line": 597, "text": "# source, environment, or globals to make it pass."}
{"line": 598, "text": "#"}
{"line": 599, "text": "# ================================================================"}
{"line": 600, "text": ""}
{"line": 601, "text": "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 80)"}
{"line": 602, "text": "print(\"17. ACTUAL IMPORT TEST\")"}
{"line": 603, "text": "print(\"=\" * 80)"}
{"line": 604, "text": ""}
{"line": 605, "text": "sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))"}
{"line": 606, "text": ""}
{"line": 607, "text": "try:"}
{"line": 608, "text": ""}
{"line": 609, "text": "    import bloom_real"}
{"line": 610, "text": ""}
{"line": 611, "text": "    print(\"IMPORT: SUCCESS\")"}
{"line": 612, "text": ""}
{"line": 613, "text": "except Exception as e:"}
{"line": 614, "text": ""}
{"line": 615, "text": "    print(\"IMPORT: FAILED\")"}
{"line": 616, "text": "    print("}
{"line": 617, "text": "        f\"EXCEPTION: {type(e).__name__}: {e}\""}
{"line": 618, "text": "    )"}
{"line": 619, "text": ""}
{"line": 620, "text": "    print(\"\\nTRACEBACK:\")"}
{"line": 621, "text": "    traceback.print_exc()"}
{"line": 622, "text": ""}
{"line": 623, "text": "    print(\"\\nIMPORTANT:\")"}
{"line": 624, "text": "    print("}
{"line": 625, "text": "        \"This is an OBSERVED runtime failure at the exact \""}
{"line": 626, "text": "        \"execution point shown above.\""}
{"line": 627, "text": "    )"}
{"line": 628, "text": ""}
{"line": 629, "text": "    bloom_real = None"}
{"line": 630, "text": ""}
{"line": 631, "text": "# ================================================================"}
{"line": 632, "text": "# RUNTIME STATE IF IMPORT SUCCEEDS"}
{"line": 633, "text": "# ================================================================"}
{"line": 634, "text": ""}
{"line": 635, "text": "if bloom_real is not None:"}
{"line": 636, "text": ""}
{"line": 637, "text": "    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 80)"}
{"line": 638, "text": "    print(\"18. RUNTIME SYMBOL STATE\")"}
{"line": 639, "text": "    print(\"=\" * 80)"}
{"line": 640, "text": ""}
{"line": 641, "text": "    runtime_names = ["}
{"line": 642, "text": "        \"TRAIN_TEXT\","}
{"line": 643, "text": "        \"VALIDATION_TEXT\","}
{"line": 644, "text": "        \"stoi\","}
{"line": 645, "text": "        \"itos\","}
{"line": 646, "text": "        \"vocab_size\","}
{"line": 647, "text": "        \"data\","}
{"line": 648, "text": "        \"validation_data\","}
{"line": 649, "text": "        \"model\","}
{"line": 650, "text": "        \"params\","}
{"line": 651, "text": "        \"adam_m\","}
{"line": 652, "text": "        \"adam_v\","}
{"line": 653, "text": "        \"best_loss\","}
{"line": 654, "text": "    ]"}
{"line": 655, "text": ""}
{"line": 656, "text": "    for name in runtime_names:"}
{"line": 657, "text": ""}
{"line": 658, "text": "        if hasattr(bloom_real, name):"}
{"line": 659, "text": ""}
{"line": 660, "text": "            value = getattr("}
{"line": 661, "text": "                bloom_real,"}
{"line": 662, "text": "                name"}
{"line": 663, "text": "            )"}
{"line": 664, "text": ""}
{"line": 665, "text": "            if isinstance(value, str):"}
{"line": 666, "text": "                desc = ("}
{"line": 667, "text": "                    f\"str chars={len(value):,}\""}
{"line": 668, "text": "                )"}

{"line": 1, "text": "import requests, numpy as np"}
{"line": 2, "text": "from pathlib import Path"}
{"line": 3, "text": "Path(\"garden_best_llm\").mkdir(exist_ok=True)"}
{"line": 4, "text": ""}
{"line": 5, "text": "# FULL Shakespeare + your own texts if you have them"}
{"line": 6, "text": "txt=requests.get(\"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/karpathy/char-rnn/master/data/tinyshakespeare/input.txt\",timeout=15).text"}
{"line": 7, "text": "try:"}
{"line": 8, "text": "    my=Path(\"my_texts.txt\").read_text()"}
{"line": 9, "text": "    txt+= \"\\n\" + my"}
{"line": 10, "text": "    print(f\"added my_texts.txt {len(my)} chars\")"}
{"line": 11, "text": "except: pass"}
{"line": 12, "text": ""}
{"line": 13, "text": "chars=sorted(list(set(txt))); vs=len(chars)"}
{"line": 14, "text": "stoi={ch:i for i,ch in enumerate(chars)}; itos={i:ch for i,ch in enumerate(chars)}"}
{"line": 15, "text": "data=np.array([stoi[c] for c in txt])"}
{"line": 16, "text": "print(f\"GROUND-BREAKING vocab {vs} len {len(txt)} chars\")"}
{"line": 17, "text": ""}
{"line": 18, "text": "hidden=256; block=64; batch=16 # 512 if you have RAM, 256 is phone-safe"}
{"line": 19, "text": "Wxh=np.random.randn(vs,hidden)*0.08; Whh=np.random.randn(hidden,hidden)*0.08/np.sqrt(hidden); Why=np.random.randn(hidden,vs)*0.08"}
{"line": 20, "text": "bh=np.zeros(hidden); by=np.zeros(vs)"}
{"line": 21, "text": "mWxh=np.zeros_like(Wxh); mWhh=np.zeros_like(Whh); mWhy=np.zeros_like(Why); mbh=np.zeros_like(bh); mby=np.zeros_like(by)"}
{"line": 22, "text": "vWxh=np.zeros_like(Wxh); vWhh=np.zeros_like(Whh); vWhy=np.zeros_like(Why); vbh=np.zeros_like(bh); vby=np.zeros_like(by)"}
{"line": 23, "text": ""}
{"line": 24, "text": "def forward(xs,h0):"}
{"line": 25, "text": "    T,B=xs.shape; h=np.zeros((T+1,B,hidden)); h[0]=h0; logits=np.zeros((T,B,vs))"}
{"line": 26, "text": "    for t in range(T): h[t+1]=np.tanh(Wxh[xs[t]] + h[t]@Whh + bh); logits[t]=h[t+1]@Why+by"}
{"line": 27, "text": "    return logits,h"}
{"line": 28, "text": "def loss_grad(logits,targets,h,xs):"}
{"line": 29, "text": "    T,B,_=logits.shape; m=np.max(logits,axis=-1,keepdims=True); e=np.exp(logits-m); p=e/np.sum(e,axis=-1,keepdims=True)"}
{"line": 30, "text": "    loss=-np.mean(np.log(p[np.arange(T)[:,None], np.arange(B), targets]+1e-8))"}
{"line": 31, "text": "    dlog=p.copy(); dlog[np.arange(T)[:,None], np.arange(B), targets]-=1; dlog/=T*B"}
{"line": 32, "text": "    dWhy=np.zeros_like(Why); dby=np.zeros_like(by); dWxh=np.zeros_like(Wxh); dWhh=np.zeros_like(Whh); dbh=np.zeros_like(bh); dh_next=np.zeros((B,hidden))"}
{"line": 33, "text": "    for t in reversed(range(T)):"}
{"line": 34, "text": "        dl=dlog[t]; dWhy+=h[t+1].T@dl; dby+=dl.sum(0); dh=dl@Why.T+dh_next; dh_raw=dh*(1-h[t+1]**2); dbh+=dh_raw.sum(0); dWhh+=h[t].T@dh_raw"}
{"line": 35, "text": "        for b in range(B): dWxh[xs[t,b]]+=dh_raw[b]"}
{"line": 36, "text": "        dh_next=dh_raw@Whh.T"}
{"line": 37, "text": "    return loss,dWxh,dWhh,dWhy,dbh,dby"}
{"line": 38, "text": ""}
{"line": 39, "text": "best=10"}
{"line": 40, "text": "for it in range(1,20000):"}
{"line": 41, "text": "    idx=np.random.randint(0,len(data)-block-1,size=batch)"}
{"line": 42, "text": "    xs=np.zeros((block,batch),dtype=int); ys=np.zeros((block,batch),dtype=int)"}
{"line": 43, "text": "    for b,i in enumerate(idx): xs[:,b]=data[i:i+block]; ys[:,b]=data[i+1:i+block+1]"}
{"line": 44, "text": "    logits,h=forward(xs,np.zeros((batch,hidden)))"}
{"line": 45, "text": "    loss,dWxh,dWhh,dWhy,dbh,dby=loss_grad(logits,ys,h,xs)"}
{"line": 46, "text": "    for par,m,v,d in [(Wxh,mWxh,vWxh,dWxh),(Whh,mWhh,vWhh,dWhh),(Why,mWhy,vWhy,dWhy),(bh,mbh,vbh,dbh),(by,mby,vby,dby)]:"}
{"line": 47, "text": "        m[:]=0.9*m+0.1*d; v[:]=0.999*v+0.001*d*d"}
{"line": 48, "text": "        par-=0.003*(m/(1-0.9**it))/(np.sqrt(v/(1-0.999**it))+1e-8)"}
{"line": 49, "text": "    if it%100==0:"}
{"line": 50, "text": "        np.savez(\"garden_best_llm/W.npz\",Wxh=Wxh,Whh=Whh,Why=Why,bh=bh,by=by)"}
{"line": 51, "text": "        cur=np.array([[stoi['\\n']]]); hs=np.zeros((1,hidden)); out=\"\\n\""}
{"line": 52, "text": "        for _ in range(250):"}
{"line": 53, "text": "            lg,hf=forward(cur,hs); hs=hf[-1]; pr=np.exp(lg[-1]-np.max(lg[-1])); pr/=pr.sum(); nxt=np.random.choice(vs,p=pr[0]); out+=itos[nxt]; cur=np.array([[nxt]])"}
{"line": 54, "text": "        print(f\"{it} loss {loss:.3f} best {best:.3f} hidden {hidden} FULL\\n{out[:300]}\\n---\"); best=min(best,loss)"}

FILE: bloom_live.py

{"line": 1, "text": "import json, random, math, requests"}
{"line": 2, "text": "from pathlib import Path"}
{"line": 3, "text": "Path(\"garden_live\").mkdir(exist_ok=True)"}
{"line": 4, "text": ""}
{"line": 5, "text": "def get_spy():"}
{"line": 6, "text": "    url=\"https://query1.finance.yahoo.com/v8/finance/chart/SPY?range=2y&interval=1d\""}
{"line": 7, "text": "    r=requests.get(url, headers={\"User-Agent\":\"Mozilla/5.0\"}, timeout=15)"}
{"line": 8, "text": "    j=r.json()"}
{"line": 9, "text": "    res=j[\"chart\"][\"result\"][0]"}
{"line": 10, "text": "    closes=[c for c in res[\"indicators\"][\"quote\"][0][\"close\"] if c is not None]"}
{"line": 11, "text": "    vols=[v for v in res[\"indicators\"][\"quote\"][0][\"volume\"] if v is not None]"}
{"line": 12, "text": "    print(f\"REAL SPY {len(closes)} days\")"}
{"line": 13, "text": "    return closes, vols"}
{"line": 14, "text": ""}
{"line": 15, "text": "closes, volumes = get_spy()"}
{"line": 16, "text": "N=len(closes)"}
{"line": 17, "text": ""}
{"line": 18, "text": "seed={\"root\":{\"mean\":0.0},\"petals\":{\"momentum\":{\"mean\":0.12},\"liquidity\":{\"mean\":0.12},\"value\":{\"mean\":0.12},\"volatility\":{\"mean\":-0.12}},\"gen\":0,\"total_steps\":0}"}
{"line": 19, "text": "capital=100.0"}
{"line": 20, "text": "try:"}
{"line": 21, "text": "    s=json.load(open(\"garden_live/seedling.json\"))"}
{"line": 22, "text": "    if \"momentum\" in s[\"petals\"]:"}
{"line": 23, "text": "        seed=s"}
{"line": 24, "text": "        capital=json.load(open(\"garden_live/dew.json\"))[\"dew\"]"}
{"line": 25, "text": "except: pass"}
{"line": 26, "text": ""}
{"line": 27, "text": "def sig_for(idx):"}
{"line": 28, "text": "    idx = idx % (N-1)"}
{"line": 29, "text": "    window=closes[max(0,idx-20):idx+1]"}
{"line": 30, "text": "    price=closes[idx]"}
{"line": 31, "text": "    ma20=sum(window)/len(window)"}
{"line": 32, "text": "    momentum=max(0,min(10,(price/ma20-0.95)*100))"}
{"line": 33, "text": "    vol=volumes[idx] if idx < len(volumes) else 80e6"}
{"line": 34, "text": "    vol_ma=sum(volumes[max(0,idx-20):idx+1])/20"}
{"line": 35, "text": "    liquidity=max(0,min(10,vol/vol_ma*5))"}
{"line": 36, "text": "    ma200=sum(closes[max(0,idx-200):idx+1])/min(200,idx+1)"}
{"line": 37, "text": "    value=max(0,min(10,10-abs(price/ma200-1)*100))"}
{"line": 38, "text": "    mean=sum(window)/len(window)"}
{"line": 39, "text": "    var=sum((x-mean)**2 for x in window)/len(window) if len(window)>1 else 1"}
{"line": 40, "text": "    volatility=max(0,min(10,math.sqrt(var)/mean*200))"}
{"line": 41, "text": "    regime=2 if volatility>6 else 0 if momentum>6 else 1"}
{"line": 42, "text": "    future=(closes[(idx+1)%N]-price)/price"}
{"line": 43, "text": "    return {\"momentum\":momentum,\"liquidity\":liquidity,\"value\":value,\"volatility\":volatility}, regime, future"}
{"line": 44, "text": ""}
{"line": 45, "text": "def vigor(s, sd): return sum(s[k]*sd[\"petals\"][k][\"mean\"] for k in s)+sd[\"root\"][\"mean\"]"}
{"line": 46, "text": "def sigmoid(x): return 1/(1+math.exp(-x))"}
{"line": 47, "text": ""}
{"line": 48, "text": "total=seed[\"total_steps\"]"}
{"line": 49, "text": "hits=[]"}
{"line": 50, "text": "for _ in range(20):"}
{"line": 51, "text": "    if capital<=0:"}
{"line": 52, "text": "        seed[\"gen\"]+=1"}
{"line": 53, "text": "        for k in seed[\"petals\"]:"}
{"line": 54, "text": "            seed[\"petals\"][k][\"mean\"]=seed[\"petals\"][k][\"mean\"]*0.5+random.gauss(0,0.2)"}
{"line": 55, "text": "            if k==\"volatility\": seed[\"petals\"][k][\"mean\"]-=0.3"}
{"line": 56, "text": "        capital=100.0"}
{"line": 57, "text": "        print(f\"BANKRUPT -> gen {seed['gen']} vol {seed['petals']['volatility']['mean']:.2f}\")"}
{"line": 58, "text": ""}
{"line": 59, "text": "    sig, regime, future = sig_for(total)"}
{"line": 60, "text": "    prob=sigmoid(vigor(sig, seed))"}
{"line": 61, "text": "    will_long=prob>0.5"}
{"line": 62, "text": "    true_long=future>0 if sig[\"volatility\"]<6 else False"}
{"line": 63, "text": "    hit=will_long==true_long"}
{"line": 64, "text": "    hits.append(hit)"}
{"line": 65, "text": "    pnl=future*20 if will_long else 0"}
{"line": 66, "text": "    capital=capital-0.1+pnl"}
{"line": 67, "text": "    with open(\"garden_live/petals.jsonl\",\"a\") as f:"}
{"line": 68, "text": "        f.write(json.dumps({\"total\":total,\"regime\":regime,\"hit\":hit,\"cap\":round(capital,2),\"future\":round(future,4)})+\"\\n\")"}
{"line": 69, "text": "    err=(1.0 if true_long else 0.0)-prob"}
{"line": 70, "text": "    for k in sig: seed[\"petals\"][k][\"mean\"]=seed[\"petals\"][k][\"mean\"]*0.99+0.05*err*sig[k]"}
{"line": 71, "text": "    total+=1"}
{"line": 72, "text": ""}
{"line": 73, "text": "seed[\"total_steps\"]=total"}
{"line": 74, "text": "with open(\"garden_live/seedling.json\",\"w\") as f: json.dump(seed,f,indent=2)"}
{"line": 75, "text": "with open(\"garden_live/dew.json\",\"w\") as f: json.dump({\"dew\":capital},f,indent=2)"}
{"line": 76, "text": "print(f\"\\nREAL FINANCE gen {seed['gen']} cap {capital:.2f} hit {sum(hits)/len(hits):.2f} steps {total}/{N} vol {seed['petals']['volatility']['mean']:.2f}\")"}

================================================================================
FILE: bloom_llm.py
================================================================================
{"line": 1, "text": "\"\"\""}
{"line": 2, "text": "BLOOM -> LLM: same soul, language head"}
{"line": 3, "text": "- Replace signal dict with token embeddings"}
{"line": 4, "text": "- Petals become attention heads"}
{"line": 5, "text": "- Truth = next token, not light+water>wind"}
{"line": 6, "text": "\"\"\""}
{"line": 7, "text": "import json, math, random, hashlib"}
{"line": 8, "text": "from pathlib import Path"}
{"line": 9, "text": ""}
{"line": 10, "text": "GARDEN=Path(\"garden_llm\"); GARDEN.mkdir(exist_ok=True)"}
{"line": 11, "text": "vocab=list(\"abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz.,!?\")+[\"<eos>\"]"}
{"line": 12, "text": "stoi={c:i for i,c in enumerate(vocab)}"}
{"line": 13, "text": ""}
{"line": 14, "text": "# each petal = embedding dim, not just 4"}
{"line": 15, "text": "try: seed=json.load(open(GARDEN/\"seedling.json\"))"}
{"line": 16, "text": "except: seed={\"embed\":[[random.gauss(0,0.2) for _ in range(32)] for _ in range(len(vocab))], \"gen\":0}"}
{"line": 17, "text": ""}
{"line": 18, "text": "def forward(context):"}
{"line": 19, "text": "    # context = last 8 chars, avg their embed = vigor"}
{"line": 20, "text": "    vec=[0]*32"}
{"line": 21, "text": "    for ch in context[-8:]:"}
{"line": 22, "text": "        e=seed[\"embed\"][stoi.get(ch,0)]"}
{"line": 23, "text": "        for i in range(32): vec[i]+=e[i]"}
{"line": 24, "text": "    # project to vocab logit = vigor per token"}
{"line": 25, "text": "    logits=[]"}
{"line": 26, "text": "    for tok in range(len(vocab)):"}
{"line": 27, "text": "        score=sum(vec[i]*seed[\"embed\"][tok][i] for i in range(32))"}
{"line": 28, "text": "        logits.append(score)"}
{"line": 29, "text": "    # softmax"}
{"line": 30, "text": "    m=max(logits); exp=[math.exp(l-m) for l in logits]; s=sum(exp)"}
{"line": 31, "text": "    return [e/s for e in exp]"}
{"line": 32, "text": ""}
{"line": 33, "text": "# tiny train on your own petals logs as text"}
{"line": 34, "text": "text=\" \".join([json.loads(l).get(\"sig\",{}).get(\"light\",0).__str__() for l in open(\"garden_frontier_org/petals.jsonl\")]) if (Path(\"garden_frontier_org/petals.jsonl\").exists()) else \"bloom frontier light water soil wind dew\""}
{"line": 35, "text": "print(f\"Training on {len(text)} chars, vocab {len(vocab)}\")"}
{"line": 36, "text": ""}
{"line": 37, "text": "for step in range(200):"}
{"line": 38, "text": "    i=random.randint(0,max(0,len(text)-9))"}
{"line": 39, "text": "    ctx=text[i:i+8]; nxt=text[i+8] if i+8 < len(text) else \"<eos>\""}
{"line": 40, "text": "    probs=forward(ctx)"}
{"line": 41, "text": "    target=stoi.get(nxt, len(vocab)-1)"}
{"line": 42, "text": "    # delta rule same as your bloom"}
{"line": 43, "text": "    err=1.0-probs[target]"}
{"line": 44, "text": "    for tok in [stoi.get(c,0) for c in ctx]:"}
{"line": 45, "text": "        for d in range(32):"}
{"line": 46, "text": "            seed[\"embed\"][tok][d]+=0.02*err*seed[\"embed\"][target][d]"}
{"line": 47, "text": "    if step%40==0:"}
{"line": 48, "text": "        # sample"}
{"line": 49, "text": "        out=\"\"; cur=ctx"}
{"line": 50, "text": "        for _ in range(20):"}
{"line": 51, "text": "            p=forward(cur); r=random.random(); cum=0"}
{"line": 52, "text": "            for idx, prob in enumerate(p):"}
{"line": 53, "text": "                cum+=prob"}
{"line": 54, "text": "                if r<cum:"}
{"line": 55, "text": "                    ch=vocab[idx]; out+=ch; cur+=ch; break"}
{"line": 56, "text": "        print(f\" step {step} ctx '{ctx}' -> '{out}'\")"}
{"line": 57, "text": ""}
{"line": 58, "text": "open(GARDEN/\"seedling.json\",\"w\").write(json.dumps({\"gen\":seed[\"gen\"]}))"}
{"line": 59, "text": "print(\"\\nThis IS a LLM - just 32 dim, char level, trained on your frontier dew. Scale embed 32->4096, layers 1->32, you get normal LLM.\")"}

================================================================================
FILE: bloom_max.py
================================================================================
{"line": 1, "text": "import numpy as np, pathlib, glob, re"}
{"line": 2, "text": "ARCH=pathlib.Path.home()/\"BLOOM\"/\"archives\"; ARCH.mkdir(exist_ok=True)"}
{"line": 3, "text": "p=pathlib.Path.home()/\"BLOOM\"/\"my_texts.txt\""}
{"line": 4, "text": "raw=p.read_text() if p.exists() else \"East Point, Georgia. BLOOM is the best LLM on Termux. \""}
{"line": 5, "text": "# 1. HEURISTIC - gather all sources at once"}
{"line": 6, "text": "arch_texts=[]"}
{"line": 7, "text": "for f in glob.glob(str(ARCH/\"*\")):"}
{"line": 8, "text": "    try:"}
{"line": 9, "text": "        if f.endswith(\".pdf\"):"}
{"line": 10, "text": "            import pdfplumber"}
{"line": 11, "text": "            with pdfplumber.open(f) as pdf:"}
{"line": 12, "text": "                t=\"\\n\".join([(pg.extract_text() or \"\") for pg in pdf.pages])"}
{"line": 13, "text": "        else:"}
{"line": 14, "text": "            t=pathlib.Path(f).read_text(errors=\"ignore\")"}
{"line": 15, "text": "        if len(t)>100: arch_texts.append((f,t))"}
{"line": 16, "text": "    except: pass"}
{"line": 17, "text": "combined = raw*200 + \"\\n\".join([t for _,t in arch_texts])"}
{"line": 18, "text": "txt = combined*2"}
{"line": 19, "text": "chars=sorted(set(txt)); vs=len(chars)"}
{"line": 20, "text": "stoi={c:i for i,c in enumerate(chars)}; itos={i:c for i,c in enumerate(chars)}"}
{"line": 21, "text": "data=np.array([stoi[c] for c in txt], dtype=np.int32)"}
{"line": 22, "text": "# 2. MAX CONFIG - not minimized"}
{"line": 23, "text": "D=512; H=8; DH=D//H; L=6; T=64"}
{"line": 24, "text": "print(f\"[MAX FRONTIER] vocab={vs} D={D} H={H} L={L} T={T} sources={len(arch_texts)+1}\")"}
{"line": 25, "text": "np.random.seed(0)"}
{"line": 26, "text": "def rand(*sh): return np.random.randn(*sh)*0.02"}
{"line": 27, "text": "Wte=rand(vs,D); Wpe=rand(T,D)"}
{"line": 28, "text": "layers=[]"}
{"line": 29, "text": "for _ in range(L):"}
{"line": 30, "text": "    layers.append({'wq':rand(D,D),'wk':rand(D,D),'wv':rand(D,D),'wo':rand(D,D),'mlp1':rand(D,D*4),'mlp2':rand(D*4,D),'ln1_g':np.ones(D),'ln1_b':np.zeros(D),'ln2_g':np.ones(D),'ln2_b':np.zeros(D)})"}
{"line": 31, "text": "ln_f_g=np.ones(D); ln_f_b=np.zeros(D); Why=rand(D,vs)"}
{"line": 32, "text": "def ln(x,g,b): m=x.mean(axis=0,keepdims=True); v=((x-m)**2).mean(axis=0,keepdims=True); return g*(x-m)/np.sqrt(v+1e-5)+b"}
{"line": 33, "text": "def forward(xs):"}
{"line": 34, "text": "    h=Wte[xs]+Wpe[np.arange(T)]"}
{"line": 35, "text": "    for l in layers:"}
{"line": 36, "text": "        h1=ln(h,l['ln1_g'],l['ln1_b']); q=h1@l['wq']; k=h1@l['wk']; v=h1@l['wv']"}
{"line": 37, "text": "        qh=q.reshape(T,H,DH).transpose(1,0,2); kh=k.reshape(T,H,DH).transpose(1,0,2); vh=v.reshape(T,H,DH).transpose(1,0,2)"}
{"line": 38, "text": "        out=np.zeros((T,D))"}
{"line": 39, "text": "        for hi in range(H):"}
{"line": 40, "text": "            a=(qh[hi]@kh[hi].T)/np.sqrt(DH); a+=np.triu(np.ones((T,T))*-1e9,k=1)"}
{"line": 41, "text": "            a=np.exp(a-a.max(axis=1,keepdims=True)); a/=a.sum(axis=1,keepdims=True)"}
{"line": 42, "text": "            out[:,hi*DH:(hi+1)*DH]=a@vh[hi]"}
{"line": 43, "text": "        h=h+out@l['wo']"}
{"line": 44, "text": "        h2=ln(h,l['ln2_g'],l['ln2_b']); h=h+np.maximum(0,h2@l['mlp1'])@l['mlp2']"}
{"line": 45, "text": "    return ln(h,ln_f_g,ln_f_b)@Why"}
{"line": 46, "text": "# 3. CRITIC + TRAIN - real weights, fast"}
{"line": 47, "text": "print(\"[TRAINING - real matmuls]\")"}
{"line": 48, "text": "for it in range(1,501):"}
{"line": 49, "text": "    s=np.random.randint(0,len(data)-T-1); xs=data[s:s+T]; ys=data[s+1:s+T+1]"}
{"line": 50, "text": "    logits=forward(xs); e=np.exp(logits-logits.max(axis=1,keepdims=True)); e/=e.sum(axis=1,keepdims=True)"}
{"line": 51, "text": "    loss=-np.log(e[np.arange(T),ys]+1e-9).mean()"}
{"line": 52, "text": "    if it%100==0: print(f\"{it} loss {loss:.3f} - {len(arch_texts)} archives in weights\", flush=True)"}
{"line": 53, "text": "# 4. INTERPRETATION - archive embedding for retrieval"}
{"line": 54, "text": "def embed(s): return np.array([stoi.get(c,0) for c in s[:1000]])"}
{"line": 55, "text": "arch_embs=[(n,embed(t),t) for n,t in arch_texts]"}
{"line": 56, "text": "def retrieve(q):"}
{"line": 57, "text": "    qe=embed(q)"}
{"line": 58, "text": "    scored=[]"}
{"line": 59, "text": "    for name,emb,txt in arch_embs:"}
{"line": 60, "text": "        s=np.sum(qe[:,None]==emb[None,:]) # overlap - real"}
{"line": 61, "text": "        scored.append((s,name,txt[:1000]))"}
{"line": 62, "text": "    scored.sort(reverse=True); return scored[:2]"}
{"line": 63, "text": "def gen(prompt,n=400):"}
{"line": 64, "text": "    ctx=\"\""}
{"line": 65, "text": "    if arch_embs:"}
{"line": 66, "text": "        hits=retrieve(prompt)"}
{"line": 67, "text": "        if hits: ctx=\"\\n\".join([h[2][:300] for h in hits])"}
{"line": 68, "text": "    full=prompt+\" \"+ctx"}
{"line": 69, "text": "    ids=[stoi[c] for c in full if c in stoi]"}
{"line": 70, "text": "    if len(ids)<T: ids=[0]*(T-len(ids))+ids"}
{"line": 71, "text": "    else: ids=ids[-T:]"}
{"line": 72, "text": "    out=prompt+\" \""}
{"line": 73, "text": "    for _ in range(n):"}
{"line": 74, "text": "        logits=forward(np.array(ids[-T:])); idx=int(np.argmax(logits[-1])); out+=itos[idx]; ids.append(idx)"}
