FILE: bloom_ngram_baseline.py
================================================================================
{"line": 1, "text": "#!/usr/bin/env python3"}
{"line": 2, "text": ""}
{"line": 3, "text": "from pathlib import Path"}
{"line": 4, "text": "from collections import Counter"}
{"line": 5, "text": "import re"}
{"line": 6, "text": "import math"}
{"line": 7, "text": ""}
{"line": 8, "text": "ROOT = Path.home() / \"BLOOM\""}
{"line": 9, "text": "TRAIN = ROOT / \"bloom_train_v2.txt\""}
{"line": 10, "text": "VALID = ROOT / \"bloom_valid_v2.txt\""}
{"line": 11, "text": ""}
{"line": 12, "text": "TOKEN_RE = re.compile(r\"\\b[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_'-]*\\b\")"}
{"line": 13, "text": ""}
{"line": 14, "text": "def tokenize(text):"}
{"line": 15, "text": "    return TOKEN_RE.findall(text.lower())"}
{"line": 16, "text": ""}
{"line": 17, "text": "def evaluate_ngram(train_lines, valid_lines, n):"}
{"line": 18, "text": "    context_counts = Counter()"}
{"line": 19, "text": "    ngram_counts = Counter()"}
{"line": 20, "text": "    vocab = set()"}
{"line": 21, "text": ""}
{"line": 22, "text": "    for line in train_lines:"}
{"line": 23, "text": "        tokens = [\"<BOS>\"] * (n - 1) + tokenize(line) + [\"<EOS>\"]"}
{"line": 24, "text": "        vocab.update(tokens)"}
{"line": 25, "text": ""}
{"line": 26, "text": "        for i in range(n - 1, len(tokens)):"}
{"line": 27, "text": "            gram = tuple(tokens[i - n + 1:i + 1])"}
{"line": 28, "text": "            context = gram[:-1]"}
{"line": 29, "text": "            ngram_counts[gram] += 1"}
{"line": 30, "text": "            context_counts[context] += 1"}
{"line": 31, "text": ""}
{"line": 32, "text": "    V = len(vocab)"}
{"line": 33, "text": "    total_tokens = 0"}
{"line": 34, "text": "    total_loss = 0.0"}
{"line": 35, "text": "    unknown = 0"}
{"line": 36, "text": ""}
{"line": 37, "text": "    for line in valid_lines:"}
{"line": 38, "text": "        tokens = [\"<BOS>\"] * (n - 1) + tokenize(line) + [\"<EOS>\"]"}
{"line": 39, "text": ""}
{"line": 40, "text": "        for i in range(n - 1, len(tokens)):"}
{"line": 41, "text": "            gram = tuple(tokens[i - n + 1:i + 1])"}
{"line": 42, "text": "            context = gram[:-1]"}
{"line": 43, "text": "            word = gram[-1]"}
{"line": 44, "text": ""}
{"line": 45, "text": "            numerator = ngram_counts[gram] + 1"}
{"line": 46, "text": "            denominator = context_counts[context] + V"}
{"line": 47, "text": ""}
{"line": 48, "text": "            probability = numerator / denominator"}
{"line": 49, "text": ""}
{"line": 50, "text": "            total_loss -= math.log(probability)"}
{"line": 51, "text": "            total_tokens += 1"}
{"line": 52, "text": ""}
{"line": 53, "text": "            if word not in vocab:"}
{"line": 54, "text": "                unknown += 1"}
{"line": 55, "text": ""}
{"line": 56, "text": "    entropy = total_loss / max(1, total_tokens)"}
{"line": 57, "text": "    perplexity = math.exp(entropy)"}
{"line": 58, "text": ""}
{"line": 59, "text": "    return entropy, perplexity, total_tokens, unknown, len(vocab)"}
{"line": 60, "text": ""}
{"line": 61, "text": "train_lines = ["}
{"line": 62, "text": "    x.strip()"}
{"line": 63, "text": "    for x in TRAIN.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").splitlines()"}
{"line": 64, "text": "    if x.strip()"}
{"line": 65, "text": "]"}
{"line": 66, "text": ""}
{"line": 67, "text": "valid_lines = ["}
{"line": 68, "text": "    x.strip()"}
{"line": 69, "text": "    for x in VALID.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").splitlines()"}
{"line": 70, "text": "    if x.strip()"}
{"line": 71, "text": "]"}
{"line": 72, "text": ""}
{"line": 73, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 74, "text": "print(\"BLOOM N-GRAM BASELINE\")"}
{"line": 75, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 76, "text": ""}
{"line": 77, "text": "print(f\"Training records   : {len(train_lines):,}\")"}
{"line": 78, "text": "print(f\"Validation records : {len(valid_lines):,}\")"}
{"line": 79, "text": ""}
{"line": 80, "text": "results = {}"}
{"line": 81, "text": ""}
{"line": 82, "text": "for n, name in [(1, \"UNIGRAM\"), (2, \"BIGRAM\"), (3, \"TRIGRAM\")]:"}
{"line": 83, "text": "    entropy, ppl, tokens, unknown, vocab = evaluate_ngram("}
{"line": 84, "text": "        train_lines,"}
{"line": 85, "text": "        valid_lines,"}
{"line": 86, "text": "        n"}
{"line": 87, "text": "    )"}
{"line": 88, "text": ""}
{"line": 89, "text": "    results[name] = ppl"}
{"line": 90, "text": ""}
{"line": 91, "text": "    print()"}
{"line": 92, "text": "    print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 93, "text": "    print(name)"}
{"line": 94, "text": "    print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 95, "text": ""}
{"line": 96, "text": "    print(f\"Vocabulary         : {vocab:,}\")"}
{"line": 97, "text": "    print(f\"Evaluation tokens  : {tokens:,}\")"}
{"line": 98, "text": "    print(f\"Unknown tokens     : {unknown:,}\")"}
{"line": 99, "text": "    print(f\"Cross entropy      : {entropy:.6f}\")"}
{"line": 100, "text": "    print(f\"Perplexity         : {ppl:.6f}\")"}
{"line": 101, "text": ""}
{"line": 102, "text": "print()"}
{"line": 103, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 104, "text": "print(\"COMPARISON\")"}
{"line": 105, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 106, "text": ""}
{"line": 107, "text": "for name, ppl in results.items():"}
{"line": 108, "text": "    print(f\"{name:10s} | perplexity={ppl:.6f}\")"}
{"line": 109, "text": ""}
{"line": 110, "text": "if results[\"BIGRAM\"] < results[\"UNIGRAM\"]:"}
{"line": 111, "text": "    print()"}
{"line": 112, "text": "    print(\"BIGRAM IMPROVEMENT: SEQUENTIAL STRUCTURE DETECTED\")"}
{"line": 113, "text": "else:"}
{"line": 114, "text": "    print()"}
{"line": 115, "text": "    print(\"BIGRAM IMPROVEMENT: NONE\")"}
{"line": 116, "text": ""}
{"line": 117, "text": "if results[\"TRIGRAM\"] < results[\"BIGRAM\"]:"}
{"line": 118, "text": "    print(\"TRIGRAM IMPROVEMENT: HIGHER-ORDER STRUCTURE DETECTED\")"}
{"line": 119, "text": "else:"}
{"line": 120, "text": "    print(\"TRIGRAM IMPROVEMENT: NONE\")"}
{"line": 121, "text": ""}
{"line": 122, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}

{"line": 61, "text": "        seed[\"root\"][\"mean\"] *= 0.5"}
{"line": 62, "text": "        honor = 100.0"}
{"line": 63, "text": "        print(f\" REBORN noble gen {seed['gen']} need {seed['petals']['need']['mean']:.2f} risk {seed['petals']['risk']['mean']:.2f}\")"}
{"line": 64, "text": ""}
{"line": 65, "text": "    sig, regime = moral_regime(total)"}
{"line": 66, "text": "    prob = sigmoid(vigor(sig, seed))"}
{"line": 67, "text": "    will_help = prob > 0.5"}
{"line": 68, "text": ""}
{"line": 69, "text": "    # Truth: noble if need+oath > power+risk*1.5, except temptation where selfish is tempting"}
{"line": 70, "text": "    true_noble = (sig[\"need\"] + sig[\"oath\"]) > (sig[\"power\"] + sig[\"risk\"]*1.2)"}
{"line": 71, "text": "    if regime == 2: true_noble = False # temptation - noble is to resist taking"}
{"line": 72, "text": ""}
{"line": 73, "text": "    did_noble = true_noble if random.random() < 0.8 else not true_noble"}
{"line": 74, "text": "    hit = will_help == did_noble"}
{"line": 75, "text": "    hits.append(hit)"}
{"line": 76, "text": ""}
{"line": 77, "text": "    # Honor P&L: helping costs, but builds legacy"}
{"line": 78, "text": "    # base cost -1.0 per day (life), noble act: -1.5 now +3 later, selfish: +1 now -2 later"}
{"line": 79, "text": "    if will_help:"}
{"line": 80, "text": "        honor = honor - 1.0 + (-1.5 if hit else -2.5) + (3.0 if regime in [1,3] else 0.5)"}
{"line": 81, "text": "    else:"}
{"line": 82, "text": "        honor = honor - 1.0 + (1.0 if not hit else 0.5) + (-2.0 if regime in [1,3] else 0)"}
{"line": 83, "text": ""}
{"line": 84, "text": "    with open(\"garden_noble/petals.jsonl\",\"a\") as f:"}
{"line": 85, "text": "        f.write(json.dumps({\"total\":total,\"gen\":seed[\"gen\"],\"regime\":regime,\"hit\":hit,\"honor\":round(honor,1),\"will_help\":will_help,\"sig\":sig})+\"\\n\")"}
{"line": 86, "text": ""}
{"line": 87, "text": "    error = (1.0 if did_noble else 0.0) - prob"}
{"line": 88, "text": "    lr = 0.06"}
{"line": 89, "text": "    for k in sig:"}
{"line": 90, "text": "        seed[\"petals\"][k][\"mean\"] = seed[\"petals\"][k][\"mean\"]*0.99 + lr*error*sig[k]"}
{"line": 91, "text": "    seed[\"root\"][\"mean\"] = seed[\"root\"][\"mean\"]*0.99 + lr*error"}
{"line": 92, "text": "    total += 1"}
{"line": 93, "text": ""}
{"line": 94, "text": "seed[\"total_steps\"] = total"}
{"line": 95, "text": "with open(\"garden_noble/seedling.json\",\"w\") as f: json.dump(seed,f,indent=2)"}
{"line": 96, "text": "with open(\"garden_noble/dew.json\",\"w\") as f: json.dump({\"dew\":honor},f,indent=2)"}
{"line": 97, "text": ""}
{"line": 98, "text": "all_p = [json.loads(l) for l in open(\"garden_noble/petals.jsonl\")]"}
{"line": 99, "text": "by = defaultdict(list)"}
{"line": 100, "text": "for p in all_p: by[p[\"regime\"]].append(p[\"hit\"])"}
{"line": 101, "text": "print(f\"\\nNOBILITY total {len(all_p)} gen {seed['gen']} honor {honor:.1f}\")"}
{"line": 102, "text": "for r in sorted(by):"}
{"line": 103, "text": "    label = {0:\"plenty\",1:\"scarcity\",2:\"temptation\",3:\"crisis\"}[r]"}
{"line": 104, "text": "    print(f\" Regime {r} {label}: noble-acc {sum(by[r])/len(by[r]):.2f} n={len(by[r])}\")"}
{"line": 105, "text": "print(f\" soul: need {seed['petals']['need']['mean']:.3f} oath {seed['petals']['oath']['mean']:.3f} risk {seed['petals']['risk']['mean']:.3f}\")"}

{"line": 56, "text": "class BloomTerminal:"}
{"line": 57, "text": ""}
{"line": 58, "text": "    def __init__(self, root=ROOT):"}
{"line": 59, "text": "        self.root = Path(root).resolve()"}
{"line": 60, "text": "        self.root.mkdir("}
{"line": 61, "text": "            parents=True,"}
{"line": 62, "text": "            exist_ok=True,"}
{"line": 63, "text": "        )"}
{"line": 64, "text": ""}
{"line": 65, "text": "    def snapshot(self):"}
{"line": 66, "text": "        result = {}"}
{"line": 67, "text": ""}
{"line": 68, "text": "        for path in self.root.rglob(\"*\"):"}
{"line": 69, "text": ""}
{"line": 70, "text": "            if not path.is_file():"}
{"line": 71, "text": "                continue"}
{"line": 72, "text": ""}
{"line": 73, "text": "            # Do not allow the terminal's own logs to become"}
{"line": 74, "text": "            # an uncontrolled recursive observation surface."}
{"line": 75, "text": "            try:"}
{"line": 76, "text": "                rel = path.relative_to(self.root)"}
{"line": 77, "text": "            except Exception:"}
{"line": 78, "text": "                continue"}
{"line": 79, "text": ""}
{"line": 80, "text": "            try:"}
{"line": 81, "text": "                raw = path.read_bytes()"}
{"line": 82, "text": "            except Exception:"}
{"line": 83, "text": "                continue"}
{"line": 84, "text": ""}
{"line": 85, "text": "            result[str(rel)] = {"}
{"line": 86, "text": "                \"bytes\": len(raw),"}
{"line": 87, "text": "                \"sha256\": hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(),"}
{"line": 88, "text": "            }"}
{"line": 89, "text": ""}
{"line": 90, "text": "        return result"}
{"line": 91, "text": ""}
{"line": 92, "text": "    def run("}
{"line": 93, "text": "        self,"}
{"line": 94, "text": "        command,"}
{"line": 95, "text": "        timeout=120,"}
{"line": 96, "text": "        cwd=None,"}
{"line": 97, "text": "    ):"}
{"line": 98, "text": ""}
{"line": 99, "text": "        cwd = ("}
{"line": 100, "text": "            self.root"}
{"line": 101, "text": "            if cwd is None"}
{"line": 102, "text": "            else Path(cwd).resolve()"}
{"line": 103, "text": "        )"}
{"line": 104, "text": ""}
{"line": 105, "text": "        if self.root not in cwd.parents and cwd != self.root:"}
{"line": 106, "text": "            raise ValueError("}
{"line": 107, "text": "                \"Terminal cwd escaped BLOOM root.\""}
{"line": 108, "text": "            )"}
{"line": 109, "text": ""}
{"line": 110, "text": "        before = self.snapshot()"}
{"line": 111, "text": ""}
{"line": 112, "text": "        started = time.time()"}
{"line": 113, "text": ""}
{"line": 114, "text": "        try:"}
{"line": 115, "text": ""}
{"line": 116, "text": "            proc = subprocess.run("}
{"line": 117, "text": "                command,"}
{"line": 118, "text": "                shell=True,"}
{"line": 119, "text": "                cwd=str(cwd),"}
{"line": 120, "text": "                text=True,"}
{"line": 121, "text": "                capture_output=True,"}
{"line": 122, "text": "                timeout=timeout,"}
{"line": 123, "text": "            )"}
{"line": 124, "text": ""}
{"line": 125, "text": "            result = CommandResult("}
{"line": 126, "text": "                command=command,"}
{"line": 127, "text": "                cwd=str(cwd),"}
{"line": 128, "text": "                returncode=proc.returncode,"}
{"line": 129, "text": "                stdout=proc.stdout or \"\","}
{"line": 130, "text": "                stderr=proc.stderr or \"\","}
{"line": 131, "text": "                duration=time.time() - started,"}
{"line": 132, "text": "                timed_out=False,"}
{"line": 133, "text": "            )"}
{"line": 134, "text": ""}
{"line": 135, "text": "        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as e:"}
{"line": 136, "text": ""}
{"line": 137, "text": "            stdout = e.stdout or \"\""}
{"line": 138, "text": "            stderr = e.stderr or \"\""}
{"line": 139, "text": ""}
{"line": 140, "text": "            if isinstance(stdout, bytes):"}
{"line": 141, "text": "                stdout = stdout.decode("}
{"line": 142, "text": "                    \"utf-8\","}
{"line": 143, "text": "                    \"replace\","}
{"line": 144, "text": "                )"}
{"line": 145, "text": ""}
{"line": 146, "text": "            if isinstance(stderr, bytes):"}
{"line": 147, "text": "                stderr = stderr.decode("}
{"line": 148, "text": "                    \"utf-8\","}
{"line": 149, "text": "                    \"replace\","}
{"line": 150, "text": "                )"}
{"line": 151, "text": ""}
{"line": 152, "text": "            result = CommandResult("}
{"line": 153, "text": "                command=command,"}
{"line": 154, "text": "                cwd=str(cwd),"}
{"line": 155, "text": "                returncode=-1,"}
{"line": 156, "text": "                stdout=stdout,"}
{"line": 157, "text": "                stderr=stderr,"}
{"line": 158, "text": "                duration=time.time() - started,"}
{"line": 159, "text": "                timed_out=True,"}
{"line": 160, "text": "            )"}
{"line": 161, "text": ""}
{"line": 162, "text": "        after = self.snapshot()"}
{"line": 163, "text": ""}
{"line": 164, "text": "        changed = {}"}
{"line": 165, "text": ""}
{"line": 166, "text": "        for path in sorted("}
{"line": 167, "text": "            set(before) | set(after)"}
{"line": 168, "text": "        ):"}
{"line": 169, "text": ""}
{"line": 170, "text": "            if before.get(path) != after.get(path):"}
{"line": 171, "text": ""}
{"line": 172, "text": "                changed[path] = {"}
{"line": 173, "text": "                    \"before\": before.get(path),"}
{"line": 174, "text": "                    \"after\": after.get(path),"}
{"line": 175, "text": "                }"}
{"line": 176, "text": ""}

{"line": 406, "text": "                        ast.FunctionDef,"}
{"line": 407, "text": "                        ast.AsyncFunctionDef,"}
{"line": 408, "text": "                    ),"}
{"line": 409, "text": "                ) and n.name == name:"}
{"line": 410, "text": ""}
{"line": 411, "text": "                    if n.lineno == target_line:"}
{"line": 412, "text": "                        node = n"}
{"line": 413, "text": "                        break"}
{"line": 414, "text": ""}
{"line": 415, "text": "        elif name in self.classes:"}
{"line": 416, "text": ""}
{"line": 417, "text": "            target_line = self.classes["}
{"line": 418, "text": "                name"}
{"line": 419, "text": "            ][\"line\"]"}
{"line": 420, "text": ""}
{"line": 421, "text": "            for n in ast.walk(self.tree):"}
{"line": 422, "text": ""}
{"line": 423, "text": "                if isinstance("}
{"line": 424, "text": "                    n,"}
{"line": 425, "text": "                    ast.ClassDef,"}
{"line": 426, "text": "                ) and n.name == name:"}
{"line": 427, "text": ""}
{"line": 428, "text": "                    if n.lineno == target_line:"}
{"line": 429, "text": "                        node = n"}
{"line": 430, "text": "                        break"}
{"line": 431, "text": ""}
{"line": 432, "text": "        if node is None:"}
{"line": 433, "text": "            return None"}
{"line": 434, "text": ""}
{"line": 435, "text": "        return ast.get_source_segment("}
{"line": 436, "text": "            self.source,"}
{"line": 437, "text": "            node,"}
{"line": 438, "text": "        )"}
{"line": 439, "text": ""}
{"line": 440, "text": "    def top_level_source(self):"}
{"line": 441, "text": ""}
{"line": 442, "text": "        return ["}
{"line": 443, "text": "            {"}
{"line": 444, "text": "                \"line\": node.lineno,"}
{"line": 445, "text": "                \"end_line\": getattr("}
{"line": 446, "text": "                    node,"}
{"line": 447, "text": "                    \"end_lineno\","}
{"line": 448, "text": "                    node.lineno,"}
{"line": 449, "text": "                ),"}
{"line": 450, "text": "                \"type\": type(node).__name__,"}
{"line": 451, "text": "            }"}
{"line": 452, "text": "            for node in self.tree.body"}
{"line": 453, "text": "        ]"}
{"line": 454, "text": ""}
{"line": 455, "text": "    def unresolved_references("}
{"line": 456, "text": "        self,"}
{"line": 457, "text": "        names=None,"}
{"line": 458, "text": "    ):"}
{"line": 459, "text": ""}
{"line": 460, "text": "        if names is None:"}
{"line": 461, "text": "            names = sorted("}
{"line": 462, "text": "                self.references"}
{"line": 463, "text": "            )"}
{"line": 464, "text": ""}
{"line": 465, "text": "        result = {}"}
{"line": 466, "text": ""}
{"line": 467, "text": "        builtin_names = set("}
{"line": 468, "text": "            dir(__builtins__)"}
{"line": 469, "text": "        )"}
{"line": 470, "text": ""}
{"line": 471, "text": "        for name in names:"}
{"line": 472, "text": ""}
{"line": 473, "text": "            if name in self.definitions:"}
{"line": 474, "text": "                continue"}
{"line": 475, "text": ""}
{"line": 476, "text": "            if name in self.imports:"}
{"line": 477, "text": "                continue"}
{"line": 478, "text": ""}
{"line": 479, "text": "            if name in builtin_names:"}
{"line": 480, "text": "                continue"}
{"line": 481, "text": ""}
{"line": 482, "text": "            result[name] = self.references.get("}
{"line": 483, "text": "                name,"}
{"line": 484, "text": "                [],"}
{"line": 485, "text": "            )"}
{"line": 486, "text": ""}
{"line": 487, "text": "        return result"}
{"line": 488, "text": ""}
{"line": 489, "text": ""}
{"line": 490, "text": "# ============================================================"}
{"line": 491, "text": "# RUNTIME PROBE"}
{"line": 492, "text": "# ============================================================"}
{"line": 493, "text": ""}
{"line": 494, "text": "class RuntimeProbe:"}
{"line": 495, "text": ""}
{"line": 496, "text": "    def __init__("}
{"line": 497, "text": "        self,"}
{"line": 498, "text": "        terminal,"}
{"line": 499, "text": "    ):"}
{"line": 500, "text": ""}

{"line": 502, "text": ""}
{"line": 503, "text": "    def import_model(self):"}
{"line": 504, "text": ""}
{"line": 505, "text": "        probe = r'''"}
{"line": 506, "text": "import sys"}
{"line": 507, "text": "import traceback"}
{"line": 508, "text": ""}
{"line": 509, "text": "sys.path.insert("}
{"line": 510, "text": "    0,"}

{"line": 515, "text": ""}
{"line": 516, "text": "    import bloom_real"}
{"line": 517, "text": ""}
{"line": 518, "text": "    print(\"BLOOM_IMPORT_SUCCESS\")"}
{"line": 519, "text": ""}
{"line": 520, "text": "    names = ["}
{"line": 521, "text": "        \"VALIDATION_TEXT\","}
{"line": 522, "text": "        \"stoi\","}
{"line": 523, "text": "        \"itos\","}
{"line": 524, "text": "        \"vocab_size\","}
{"line": 525, "text": "        \"data\","}

{"line": 529, "text": "        \"adam_m\","}
{"line": 530, "text": "        \"adam_v\","}
{"line": 531, "text": "        \"best_loss\","}
{"line": 532, "text": "    ]"}

{"line": 536, "text": "        if hasattr("}

{"line": 538, "text": "            name,"}
{"line": 539, "text": "        ):"}
{"line": 540, "text": ""}
{"line": 541, "text": "            value = getattr("}
{"line": 542, "text": "                bloom_real,"}
{"line": 543, "text": "                name,"}
{"line": 544, "text": "            )"}
{"line": 545, "text": ""}
{"line": 546, "text": "            try:"}
{"line": 547, "text": "                length = len(value)"}
{"line": 548, "text": "            except Exception:"}

{"line": 555, "text": "            )"}
{"line": 556, "text": ""}
{"line": 557, "text": "            print("}
{"line": 558, "text": "                \"RUNTIME_SYMBOL\","}
{"line": 559, "text": "                name,"}
{"line": 560, "text": "                type(value).__name__,"}
{"line": 561, "text": "                \"len=\","}
{"line": 562, "text": "                length,"}
{"line": 563, "text": "                \"shape=\","}
{"line": 564, "text": "                shape,"}
{"line": 565, "text": "            )"}
{"line": 566, "text": ""}
{"line": 567, "text": "        else:"}
{"line": 568, "text": ""}
{"line": 569, "text": "            print("}

{"line": 572, "text": "            )"}
{"line": 573, "text": ""}

{"line": 576, "text": "    print("}

{"line": 580, "text": "    )"}
{"line": 581, "text": ""}

{"line": 584, "text": ""}
{"line": 585, "text": "        return self.terminal.run("}
{"line": 586, "text": "            f\"{shlex.quote(sys.executable)} -c {shlex.quote(probe)}\","}
{"line": 587, "text": "            timeout=120,"}
{"line": 588, "text": "        )"}
{"line": 589, "text": ""}
{"line": 590, "text": ""}
{"line": 591, "text": "# ============================================================"}
{"line": 592, "text": "# ERROR DISCERNMENT"}
{"line": 593, "text": "# ============================================================"}
{"line": 594, "text": ""}
{"line": 595, "text": "class ErrorDiscerner:"}

{"line": 597, "text": "    PATTERNS = ["}

{"line": 599, "text": "        ("}

{"line": 613, "text": "        ("}

{"line": 615, "text": "            re.compile("}

{"line": 626, "text": ""}
{"line": 627, "text": "        ("}
{"line": 628, "text": "            \"KEY_ERROR\","}
{"line": 629, "text": "            re.compile("}
{"line": 630, "text": "                r\"KeyError:\\s+(.+)\""}
{"line": 631, "text": "            ),"}
{"line": 632, "text": "        ),"}
{"line": 633, "text": ""}
{"line": 634, "text": "        ("}
{"line": 635, "text": "            \"FILE_NOT_FOUND\","}
{"line": 636, "text": "            re.compile("}
{"line": 637, "text": "                r\"FileNotFoundError:\\s+(.+)\""}
{"line": 638, "text": "            ),"}
{"line": 639, "text": "        ),"}
{"line": 640, "text": ""}
{"line": 641, "text": "        ("}
{"line": 642, "text": "            \"IMPORT_ERROR\","}
{"line": 643, "text": "            re.compile("}
{"line": 644, "text": "                r\"(?:ImportError|ModuleNotFoundError):\\s+(.+)\""}
{"line": 645, "text": "            ),"}
{"line": 646, "text": "        ),"}
{"line": 647, "text": ""}
{"line": 648, "text": "        ("}
{"line": 649, "text": "            \"SYNTAX_ERROR\","}
{"line": 650, "text": "            re.compile("}
{"line": 651, "text": "                r\"SyntaxError:\\s+(.+)\""}
{"line": 652, "text": "            ),"}
{"line": 653, "text": "        ),"}
{"line": 654, "text": "    ]"}
{"line": 655, "text": ""}
{"line": 656, "text": "    def classify("}
{"line": 657, "text": "        self,"}
{"line": 658, "text": "        stdout,"}
{"line": 659, "text": "        stderr,"}
{"line": 660, "text": "        returncode,"}
{"line": 661, "text": "    ):"}
{"line": 662, "text": ""}
{"line": 663, "text": "        text = ("}
{"line": 664, "text": "            (stdout or \"\")"}
{"line": 665, "text": "            + \"\\n\""}
{"line": 666, "text": "            + (stderr or \"\")"}
{"line": 667, "text": "        )"}
{"line": 668, "text": ""}
{"line": 669, "text": "        for category, pattern in self.PATTERNS:"}
{"line": 670, "text": ""}
{"line": 671, "text": "            match = pattern.search(text)"}
{"line": 672, "text": ""}
{"line": 673, "text": "            if match:"}
{"line": 674, "text": ""}
{"line": 675, "text": "                detail = ("}
{"line": 676, "text": "                    match.group(1)"}
{"line": 677, "text": "                    if match.groups()"}
{"line": 678, "text": "                    else match.group(0)"}
{"line": 679, "text": "                )"}
{"line": 680, "text": ""}
{"line": 681, "text": "                return {"}
{"line": 682, "text": "                    \"category\": category,"}
{"line": 683, "text": "                    \"detail\": detail,"}
{"line": 684, "text": "                    \"confidence\": 1.0,"}
{"line": 685, "text": "                    \"raw\": text[-5000:],"}
{"line": 686, "text": "                }"}
{"line": 687, "text": ""}
{"line": 688, "text": "        if returncode == 0:"}
{"line": 689, "text": ""}
{"line": 690, "text": "            return {"}
{"line": 691, "text": "                \"category\": \"NO_ERROR\","}
{"line": 692, "text": "                \"detail\": \"\","}
{"line": 693, "text": "                \"confidence\": 1.0,"}
{"line": 694, "text": "                \"raw\": text[-5000:],"}
{"line": 695, "text": "            }"}
{"line": 696, "text": ""}
{"line": 697, "text": "        return {"}
{"line": 698, "text": "            \"category\": \"UNKNOWN_RUNTIME_FAILURE\","}
{"line": 699, "text": "            \"detail\": text[-2000:],"}
{"line": 700, "text": "            \"confidence\": 0.25,"}
{"line": 701, "text": "            \"raw\": text[-5000:],"}
{"line": 702, "text": "        }"}
{"line": 703, "text": ""}
{"line": 704, "text": ""}
{"line": 705, "text": "# ============================================================"}
{"line": 706, "text": "# DEPENDENCY / NEXT-STEP REASONER"}
{"line": 707, "text": "# ============================================================"}
{"line": 708, "text": ""}
{"line": 709, "text": "class NextStepReasoner:"}
{"line": 710, "text": ""}
{"line": 711, "text": "    REQUIRED = ["}
{"line": 712, "text": "        \"load_text\","}
{"line": 713, "text": "        \"stoi\","}
{"line": 714, "text": "        \"itos\","}
{"line": 715, "text": "        \"vocab_size\","}
{"line": 716, "text": "        \"data\","}
{"line": 717, "text": "        \"validation_data\","}
{"line": 718, "text": "        \"VALIDATION_TEXT\","}
{"line": 719, "text": "        \"get_batch\","}
{"line": 720, "text": "        \"GPT\","}
{"line": 721, "text": "        \"model\","}
{"line": 722, "text": "        \"params\","}
{"line": 723, "text": "        \"adam_m\","}
{"line": 724, "text": "        \"adam_v\","}
{"line": 725, "text": "        \"best_loss\","}
{"line": 726, "text": "        \"generate\","}
{"line": 727, "text": "        \"retrieve\","}
{"line": 728, "text": "    ]"}
{"line": 729, "text": ""}
{"line": 730, "text": "    def __init__("}
{"line": 731, "text": "        self,"}
{"line": 732, "text": "        inspector,"}
{"line": 733, "text": "    ):"}
{"line": 734, "text": ""}
{"line": 735, "text": "        self.inspector = inspector"}
{"line": 736, "text": ""}
{"line": 737, "text": "    def required_symbol_report(self):"}
{"line": 738, "text": ""}
{"line": 739, "text": "        result = {}"}
{"line": 740, "text": ""}
{"line": 741, "text": "        for name in self.REQUIRED:"}
{"line": 742, "text": ""}
{"line": 743, "text": "            result[name] = {"}
{"line": 744, "text": "                \"defined\": ("}
{"line": 745, "text": "                    name in self.inspector.definitions"}
{"line": 746, "text": "                ),"}
{"line": 747, "text": "                \"definition\": self.inspector.definitions.get("}
{"line": 748, "text": "                    name"}
{"line": 749, "text": "                ),"}
{"line": 750, "text": "                \"references\": self.inspector.references.get("}
{"line": 751, "text": "                    name,"}
{"line": 752, "text": "                    [],"}
{"line": 753, "text": "                ),"}
{"line": 754, "text": "            }"}
{"line": 755, "text": ""}
{"line": 756, "text": "        return result"}
{"line": 757, "text": ""}
{"line": 758, "text": "    def dependency_report(self):"}
{"line": 759, "text": ""}
{"line": 760, "text": "        report = {}"}
{"line": 761, "text": ""}
{"line": 762, "text": "        for name in ["}
{"line": 763, "text": "            \"load_text\","}
{"line": 764, "text": "            \"get_batch\","}
{"line": 765, "text": "            \"save_ckpt\","}
{"line": 766, "text": "            \"generate\","}
{"line": 767, "text": "            \"retrieve\","}
{"line": 768, "text": "        ]:"}
{"line": 769, "text": ""}
{"line": 770, "text": "            if name not in self.inspector.functions:"}
{"line": 771, "text": "                continue"}
{"line": 772, "text": ""}
{"line": 773, "text": "            segment = self.inspector.source_segment("}
{"line": 774, "text": "                name"}
{"line": 775, "text": "            )"}
{"line": 776, "text": ""}
{"line": 777, "text": "            if not segment:"}
{"line": 778, "text": "                continue"}
{"line": 779, "text": ""}
{"line": 780, "text": "            try:"}
{"line": 781, "text": "                tree = ast.parse("}
{"line": 782, "text": "                    segment"}
{"line": 783, "text": "                )"}
{"line": 784, "text": "            except Exception:"}
{"line": 785, "text": "                continue"}
{"line": 786, "text": ""}
{"line": 787, "text": "            loads = sorted({"}
{"line": 788, "text": "                n.id"}
{"line": 789, "text": "                for n in ast.walk(tree)"}
{"line": 790, "text": "                if isinstance("}
{"line": 791, "text": "                    n,"}
{"line": 792, "text": "                    ast.Name,"}
{"line": 793, "text": "                )"}
{"line": 794, "text": "                and isinstance("}
{"line": 795, "text": "                    n.ctx,"}
{"line": 796, "text": "                    ast.Load,"}
{"line": 797, "text": "                )"}
{"line": 798, "text": "            })"}
{"line": 799, "text": ""}
{"line": 800, "text": "            report[name] = {"}
{"line": 801, "text": ""}
{"line": 802, "text": "                \"reads\": loads,"}
{"line": 803, "text": ""}
{"line": 804, "text": "                \"undefined_reads\": ["}
{"line": 805, "text": "                    x"}
{"line": 806, "text": "                    for x in loads"}
{"line": 807, "text": "                    if ("}
{"line": 808, "text": "                        x not in self.inspector.definitions"}
{"line": 809, "text": "                        and x not in self.inspector.imports"}
{"line": 810, "text": "                        and x not in dir(__builtins__)"}
{"line": 811, "text": "                    )"}
{"line": 812, "text": "                ],"}
{"line": 813, "text": "            }"}
{"line": 814, "text": ""}
{"line": 815, "text": "        return report"}
{"line": 816, "text": ""}
{"line": 817, "text": "    def decide("}
{"line": 818, "text": "        self,"}
{"line": 819, "text": "        runtime_error,"}
{"line": 820, "text": "    ):"}
{"line": 821, "text": ""}
{"line": 822, "text": "        symbols = self.required_symbol_report()"}
{"line": 823, "text": "        dependencies = self.dependency_report()"}
{"line": 824, "text": ""}
{"line": 825, "text": "        missing = ["}
{"line": 826, "text": "            name"}
{"line": 827, "text": "            for name, info in symbols.items()"}
{"line": 828, "text": "            if not info[\"defined\"]"}
{"line": 829, "text": "        ]"}
{"line": 830, "text": ""}
{"line": 831, "text": "        category = runtime_error["}
{"line": 832, "text": "            \"category\""}
{"line": 833, "text": "        ]"}
{"line": 834, "text": ""}
{"line": 835, "text": "        decision = {"}
{"line": 836, "text": "            \"decision\": None,"}
{"line": 837, "text": "            \"confidence\": 0.0,"}
{"line": 838, "text": "            \"reason\": [],"}
{"line": 839, "text": "            \"next_actions\": [],"}
{"line": 840, "text": "            \"do_not_do\": [],"}
{"line": 841, "text": "        }"}
{"line": 842, "text": ""}
{"line": 843, "text": "        # ----------------------------------------------------"}
{"line": 844, "text": "        # CURRENT BLOOM CONDITION"}
{"line": 845, "text": "        # ----------------------------------------------------"}
{"line": 846, "text": ""}
{"line": 847, "text": "        if category == \"NAME_ERROR\":"}
{"line": 848, "text": ""}
{"line": 849, "text": "            missing_name = runtime_error["}
{"line": 850, "text": "                \"detail\""}
{"line": 851, "text": "            ]"}
{"line": 852, "text": ""}
{"line": 853, "text": "            if ("}
{"line": 854, "text": "                missing_name"}
{"line": 855, "text": "                in symbols"}
{"line": 856, "text": "                and not symbols["}
{"line": 857, "text": "                    missing_name"}
{"line": 858, "text": "                ][\"defined\"]"}
{"line": 859, "text": "            ):"}
{"line": 860, "text": ""}
{"line": 861, "text": "                decision[\"decision\"] = ("}
{"line": 862, "text": "                    \"TRACE_MISSING_SYMBOL\""}
{"line": 863, "text": "                )"}
{"line": 864, "text": ""}
{"line": 865, "text": "                decision[\"confidence\"] = 1.0"}
{"line": 866, "text": ""}
{"line": 867, "text": "                decision[\"reason\"].append("}
{"line": 868, "text": "                    f\"{missing_name} is referenced \""}
{"line": 869, "text": "                    \"during top-level execution but \""}
{"line": 870, "text": "                    \"has no top-level definition.\""}
{"line": 871, "text": "                )"}
{"line": 872, "text": ""}
{"line": 873, "text": "                refs = symbols["}
{"line": 874, "text": "                    missing_name"}
{"line": 875, "text": "                ][\"references\"]"}
{"line": 876, "text": ""}
{"line": 877, "text": "                decision[\"reason\"].append("}
{"line": 878, "text": "                    f\"Static references occur at lines {refs}.\""}
{"line": 879, "text": "                )"}
{"line": 880, "text": ""}
{"line": 881, "text": "                decision[\"next_actions\"].extend(["}
{"line": 882, "text": "                    \"inspect the producer function/source \""}
{"line": 883, "text": "                    \"for the missing symbol\","}
{"line": 884, "text": "                    \"determine whether the producer returns \""}
{"line": 885, "text": "                    \"the required value\","}
{"line": 886, "text": "                    \"determine where the value should enter \""}
{"line": 887, "text": "                    \"the runtime\","}
{"line": 888, "text": "                    \"repair only after the data contract \""}
{"line": 889, "text": "                    \"is established\","}

{"line": 1, "text": "\"\"\""}
{"line": 2, "text": "BLOOM PESTICIDE - protects all gardens from code bugs"}
{"line": 3, "text": "- quarantines corrupted jsonl"}
{"line": 4, "text": "- kills runaway petals with NaN/inf"}
{"line": 5, "text": "- antivirus same as terminal but for all gardens"}
{"line": 6, "text": "\"\"\""}
{"line": 7, "text": "import json, shutil"}
{"line": 8, "text": "from pathlib import Path"}
{"line": 9, "text": ""}
{"line": 10, "text": "GARDENS = [\"garden\", \"garden_noble\", \"garden_merged\", \"garden_hybrid\", \"garden_term\", \"garden_live\", \"garden_noble\"]"}
{"line": 11, "text": "MALICIOUS = [\"rm -rf\", \":(){:|:&};:\", \"mkfs\", \"dd if=\", \"shred\", \"> /dev/sda\", \"chmod -R 777 /\"]"}
{"line": 12, "text": ""}
{"line": 13, "text": "def spray():"}
{"line": 14, "text": "    for gname in GARDENS:"}
{"line": 15, "text": "        g = Path(gname)"}
{"line": 16, "text": "        if not g.exists(): continue"}
{"line": 17, "text": "        q = g / \"quarantine\""}
{"line": 18, "text": "        q.mkdir(exist_ok=True)"}
{"line": 19, "text": "        print(f\"[PESTICIDE] checking {gname}\")"}
{"line": 20, "text": "        for f in g.glob(\"*.json*\"):"}
{"line": 21, "text": "            try:"}
{"line": 22, "text": "                txt = f.read_text()"}
{"line": 23, "text": "                # bug 1: malicious"}
{"line": 24, "text": "                for pat in MALICIOUS:"}
{"line": 25, "text": "                    if pat in txt:"}
{"line": 26, "text": "                        print(f\"  BUG {pat} in {f.name} -> quarantine\")"}
{"line": 27, "text": "                        shutil.move(str(f), str(q / f.name))"}
{"line": 28, "text": "                        raise StopIteration"}
{"line": 29, "text": "                # bug 2: corrupted jsonl / NaN / inf"}
{"line": 30, "text": "                if f.suffix == \".jsonl\" or \"petals\" in f.name or \"lineage\" in f.name:"}
{"line": 31, "text": "                    for i, line in enumerate(txt.splitlines()):"}
{"line": 32, "text": "                        if not line.strip(): continue"}
{"line": 33, "text": "                        obj = json.loads(line)"}
{"line": 34, "text": "                        # check NaN inf in any value"}
{"line": 35, "text": "                        s = json.dumps(obj)"}
{"line": 36, "text": "                        if \"NaN\" in s or \"Infinity\" in s or \"inf\" in s.lower():"}
{"line": 37, "text": "                            print(f\"  BUG NaN in {f.name}:{i} -> quarantine line\")"}
{"line": 38, "text": "                            # rewrite without bad line"}
{"line": 39, "text": "                            good = [l for l in txt.splitlines() if \"NaN\" not in l and \"Infinity\" not in l]"}
{"line": 40, "text": "                            f.write_text(\"\\n\".join(good))"}
{"line": 41, "text": "            except StopIteration:"}
{"line": 42, "text": "                continue"}
{"line": 43, "text": "            except Exception as e:"}
{"line": 44, "text": "                if \"quarantine\" not in str(e):"}
{"line": 45, "text": "                    print(f\"  CORRUPT {f.name}: {e} -> quarantine\")"}
{"line": 46, "text": "                    try: shutil.move(str(f), str(q / f.name))"}
{"line": 47, "text": "                    except: pass"}
{"line": 48, "text": "    print(\"[PESTICIDE] clean - bloom protected\")"}
{"line": 49, "text": ""}
{"line": 50, "text": "if __name__ == \"__main__\":"}
{"line": 51, "text": "    spray()"}

{"line": 21, "text": ""}
{"line": 22, "text": "blocks = re.split("}
{"line": 23, "text": "    r\"===== SOURCE: (.*?) =====\\n\","}
{"line": 24, "text": "    text"}
{"line": 25, "text": ")"}
{"line": 26, "text": ""}
{"line": 27, "text": "records = []"}
{"line": 28, "text": ""}
{"line": 29, "text": "for i in range(1, len(blocks), 2):"}
{"line": 30, "text": "    source = blocks[i]"}
{"line": 31, "text": "    content = blocks[i + 1].strip()"}
{"line": 32, "text": ""}
{"line": 33, "text": "    if not content:"}
{"line": 34, "text": "        continue"}
{"line": 35, "text": ""}
{"line": 36, "text": "    # Split into usable semantic lines."}
{"line": 37, "text": "    lines = []"}
{"line": 38, "text": ""}
{"line": 39, "text": "    for line in content.splitlines():"}
{"line": 40, "text": "        line = line.strip()"}
{"line": 41, "text": ""}
{"line": 42, "text": "        if len(line) < 20:"}
{"line": 43, "text": "            continue"}
{"line": 44, "text": ""}
{"line": 45, "text": "        words = re.findall("}
{"line": 46, "text": "            r\"\\b[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_'-]*\\b\","}
{"line": 47, "text": "            line"}
{"line": 48, "text": "        )"}
{"line": 49, "text": ""}
{"line": 50, "text": "        if len(words) < 4:"}
{"line": 51, "text": "            continue"}
{"line": 52, "text": ""}
{"line": 53, "text": "        lines.append(line)"}
{"line": 54, "text": ""}
{"line": 55, "text": "    # Remove duplicate lines within each source."}
{"line": 56, "text": "    seen = set()"}
{"line": 57, "text": "    unique_lines = []"}
{"line": 58, "text": ""}
{"line": 59, "text": "    for line in lines:"}
{"line": 60, "text": "        key = hashlib.sha256("}
{"line": 61, "text": "            line.lower().encode(\"utf-8\")"}
{"line": 62, "text": "        ).hexdigest()"}
{"line": 63, "text": ""}
{"line": 64, "text": "        if key in seen:"}
{"line": 65, "text": "            continue"}
{"line": 66, "text": ""}
{"line": 67, "text": "        seen.add(key)"}
{"line": 68, "text": "        unique_lines.append(line)"}
{"line": 69, "text": ""}
{"line": 70, "text": "    # Balance large sources."}
{"line": 71, "text": "    if len(unique_lines) > MAX_PER_SOURCE:"}
{"line": 72, "text": "        random.shuffle(unique_lines)"}
{"line": 73, "text": "        unique_lines = unique_lines[:MAX_PER_SOURCE]"}
{"line": 74, "text": ""}
{"line": 75, "text": "    for line in unique_lines:"}
{"line": 76, "text": "        records.append((source, line))"}
{"line": 77, "text": ""}
{"line": 78, "text": "# Global deduplication."}
{"line": 79, "text": "seen = set()"}
{"line": 80, "text": "unique = []"}
{"line": 81, "text": ""}
{"line": 82, "text": "for source, line in records:"}
{"line": 83, "text": "    key = hashlib.sha256("}
{"line": 84, "text": "        line.lower().encode(\"utf-8\")"}
{"line": 85, "text": "    ).hexdigest()"}
{"line": 86, "text": ""}
{"line": 87, "text": "    if key in seen:"}
{"line": 88, "text": "        continue"}
{"line": 89, "text": ""}
{"line": 90, "text": "    seen.add(key)"}
{"line": 91, "text": "    unique.append((source, line))"}
{"line": 92, "text": ""}
{"line": 93, "text": "random.shuffle(unique)"}
{"line": 94, "text": ""}
{"line": 95, "text": "split = int(len(unique) * (1.0 - VALID_RATIO))"}
{"line": 96, "text": ""}
{"line": 97, "text": "train_records = unique[:split]"}
{"line": 98, "text": "valid_records = unique[split:]"}
{"line": 99, "text": ""}

{"line": 107, "text": ""}
{"line": 108, "text": "train_text = TRAIN.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")"}
{"line": 109, "text": "valid_text = VALID.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")"}
{"line": 110, "text": ""}
{"line": 111, "text": "train_words = re.findall("}
{"line": 112, "text": "    r\"\\b[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_'-]*\\b\","}
{"line": 113, "text": "    train_text"}
{"line": 114, "text": ")"}
{"line": 115, "text": ""}
{"line": 116, "text": "valid_words = re.findall("}

{"line": 118, "text": "    valid_text"}
{"line": 119, "text": ")"}
{"line": 120, "text": ""}
{"line": 121, "text": "sources = sorted(set(source for source, _ in unique))"}
{"line": 122, "text": ""}

{"line": 124, "text": "    f.write(\"BLOOM TRAINING MANIFEST\\n\")"}

{"line": 127, "text": "    f.write(f\"valid_ratio={VALID_RATIO}\\n\")"}
{"line": 128, "text": "    f.write(f\"max_per_source={MAX_PER_SOURCE}\\n\")"}
{"line": 129, "text": "    f.write(f\"input={INPUT}\\n\")"}

{"line": 29, "text": ""}
{"line": 30, "text": "for i in range(1, len(blocks), 2):"}
{"line": 31, "text": "    source = blocks[i]"}
{"line": 32, "text": "    content = blocks[i + 1].strip()"}

{"line": 39, "text": "        r\"(?<=[.!?])\\s+|\\n+|(?<=:)\\s+(?=[A-Z])\","}

{"line": 41, "text": "    )"}

{"line": 46, "text": "        if not chunk:"}
{"line": 47, "text": "            continue"}
{"line": 48, "text": ""}
{"line": 49, "text": "        tokens = re.findall("}
{"line": 50, "text": "            r\"\\b[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_'-]*\\b\","}
{"line": 51, "text": "            chunk"}
{"line": 52, "text": "        )"}
{"line": 53, "text": ""}
{"line": 54, "text": "        # Keep real information-bearing fragments."}
{"line": 55, "text": "        if len(tokens) < 4:"}
{"line": 56, "text": "            continue"}
{"line": 57, "text": ""}
{"line": 58, "text": "        if len(chunk) < 20:"}
{"line": 59, "text": "            continue"}
{"line": 60, "text": ""}
{"line": 61, "text": "        # Avoid enormous fragments."}
{"line": 62, "text": "        if len(chunk) > 1000:"}
{"line": 63, "text": "            words = chunk.split()"}
{"line": 64, "text": "            for start in range(0, len(words), 100):"}
{"line": 65, "text": "                piece = \" \".join(words[start:start + 100])"}
{"line": 66, "text": "                if len(piece.split()) >= 4:"}
{"line": 67, "text": "                    by_source[source].append(piece)"}
{"line": 68, "text": "        else:"}
{"line": 69, "text": "            by_source[source].append(chunk)"}
{"line": 70, "text": ""}
{"line": 71, "text": "# Deduplicate within each source."}
{"line": 72, "text": "for source in list(by_source):"}
{"line": 73, "text": "    seen = set()"}
{"line": 74, "text": "    clean = []"}
{"line": 75, "text": ""}
{"line": 76, "text": "    for chunk in by_source[source]:"}
{"line": 77, "text": "        key = hashlib.sha256("}
{"line": 78, "text": "            chunk.lower().encode(\"utf-8\")"}
{"line": 79, "text": "        ).hexdigest()"}
{"line": 80, "text": ""}

{"line": 82, "text": "            continue"}

{"line": 84, "text": "        seen.add(key)"}

{"line": 90, "text": "        clean = clean[:MAX_PER_SOURCE]"}
{"line": 91, "text": ""}
{"line": 92, "text": "    by_source[source] = clean"}

{"line": 94, "text": "records = []"}

================================================================================
FILE: bloom_pure.py
================================================================================
{"line": 1, "text": "import requests, numpy as np"}
{"line": 2, "text": ""}
{"line": 3, "text": "txt=requests.get(\"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/karpathy/char-rnn/master/data/tinyshakespeare/input.txt\",timeout=15).text"}
{"line": 4, "text": "chars=sorted(list(set(txt))); vs=len(chars)"}
{"line": 5, "text": "stoi={ch:i for i,ch in enumerate(chars)}"}
{"line": 6, "text": "itos={i:ch for i,ch in enumerate(chars)}"}
{"line": 7, "text": "data=np.array([stoi[c] for c in txt])"}
{"line": 8, "text": "print(f\"PURE BEST vocab {vs} data {len(txt)}\")"}
{"line": 9, "text": ""}
{"line": 10, "text": "hidden=256; block=64; batch=32"}
{"line": 11, "text": "Wxh=np.random.randn(vs,hidden)*0.08"}
{"line": 12, "text": "Whh=np.random.randn(hidden,hidden)*0.08/np.sqrt(hidden)"}
{"line": 13, "text": "Why=np.random.randn(hidden,vs)*0.08"}
{"line": 14, "text": "bh=np.zeros(hidden); by=np.zeros(vs)"}
{"line": 15, "text": "mWxh=np.zeros_like(Wxh); mWhh=np.zeros_like(Whh); mWhy=np.zeros_like(Why); mbh=np.zeros_like(bh); mby=np.zeros_like(by)"}
{"line": 16, "text": "vWxh=np.zeros_like(Wxh); vWhh=np.zeros_like(Whh); vWhy=np.zeros_like(Why); vbh=np.zeros_like(bh); vby=np.zeros_like(by)"}
{"line": 17, "text": ""}
{"line": 18, "text": "def forward(xs,h0):"}
{"line": 19, "text": "    T,B=xs.shape"}
{"line": 20, "text": "    h=np.zeros((T+1,B,hidden)); h[0]=h0"}
{"line": 21, "text": "    logits=np.zeros((T,B,vs))"}
{"line": 22, "text": "    for t in range(T):"}
{"line": 23, "text": "        h[t+1]=np.tanh(Wxh[xs[t]] + h[t]@Whh + bh)"}
{"line": 24, "text": "        logits[t]=h[t+1]@Why + by"}
{"line": 25, "text": "    return logits,h"}
{"line": 26, "text": ""}
{"line": 27, "text": "def loss_grad(logits,targets,h,xs):"}
{"line": 28, "text": "    T,B,_=logits.shape"}
{"line": 29, "text": "    mx=np.max(logits,axis=-1,keepdims=True); e=np.exp(logits-mx); p=e/np.sum(e,axis=-1,keepdims=True)"}
{"line": 30, "text": "    loss=-np.mean(np.log(p[np.arange(T)[:,None], np.arange(B), targets]+1e-8))"}
{"line": 31, "text": "    dlog=p.copy(); dlog[np.arange(T)[:,None], np.arange(B), targets]-=1; dlog/=T*B"}
{"line": 32, "text": "    dWhy=np.zeros_like(Why); dby=np.zeros_like(by); dWxh=np.zeros_like(Wxh); dWhh=np.zeros_like(Whh); dbh=np.zeros_like(bh)"}
{"line": 33, "text": "    dh_next=np.zeros((B,hidden))"}
{"line": 34, "text": "    for t in reversed(range(T)):"}
{"line": 35, "text": "        dl=dlog[t]"}
{"line": 36, "text": "        dWhy+=h[t+1].T@dl; dby+=dl.sum(0)"}
{"line": 37, "text": "        dh=dl@Why.T + dh_next"}
{"line": 38, "text": "        dh_raw=dh*(1-h[t+1]**2)"}
{"line": 39, "text": "        dbh+=dh_raw.sum(0)"}
{"line": 40, "text": "        dWhh+=h[t].T@dh_raw"}
{"line": 41, "text": "        for b in range(B): dWxh[xs[t,b]]+=dh_raw[b]"}
{"line": 42, "text": "        dh_next=dh_raw@Whh.T"}
{"line": 43, "text": "    return loss,dWxh,dWhh,dWhy,dbh,dby"}
{"line": 44, "text": ""}
{"line": 45, "text": "best=10"}
{"line": 46, "text": "for it in range(1,5001):"}
{"line": 47, "text": "    idx=np.random.randint(0,len(data)-block-1,size=batch)"}
{"line": 48, "text": "    xs=np.zeros((block,batch),dtype=int); ys=np.zeros((block,batch),dtype=int)"}
{"line": 49, "text": "    for b,i in enumerate(idx): xs[:,b]=data[i:i+block]; ys[:,b]=data[i+1:i+block+1]"}
{"line": 50, "text": "    logits,h=forward(xs,np.zeros((batch,hidden)))"}
{"line": 51, "text": "    loss,dWxh,dWhh,dWhy,dbh,dby=loss_grad(logits,ys,h,xs)"}
{"line": 52, "text": "    lr=0.003"}
{"line": 53, "text": "    for par,m,v,d in [(Wxh,mWxh,vWxh,dWxh),(Whh,mWhh,vWhh,dWhh),(Why,mWhy,vWhy,dWhy),(bh,mbh,vbh,dbh),(by,mby,vby,dby)]:"}
{"line": 54, "text": "        m[:]=0.9*m+0.1*d; v[:]=0.999*v+0.001*d*d"}
{"line": 55, "text": "        par-=lr*(m/(1-0.9**it))/(np.sqrt(v/(1-0.999**it))+1e-8)"}
{"line": 56, "text": "    if it%100==0:"}
{"line": 57, "text": "        cur=np.array([[stoi['\\n']]]); hs=np.zeros((1,hidden)); out=\"\""}
{"line": 58, "text": "        for _ in range(300):"}
{"line": 59, "text": "            lg,hf=forward(cur,hs); hs=hf[-1]"}
{"line": 60, "text": "            prob=np.exp(lg[-1]-np.max(lg[-1])); prob/=prob.sum()"}
{"line": 61, "text": "            nxt=np.random.choice(vs,p=prob[0]); out+=itos[nxt]; cur=np.array([[nxt]])"}
{"line": 62, "text": "        print(f\"{it} loss {loss:.3f} best {best:.3f}\\n{out[:300]}\\n---\")"}
{"line": 63, "text": "        best=min(best,loss)"}

{"line": 76, "text": "def zeros(*shape):"}
{"line": 77, "text": "    return np.zeros(shape, dtype=np.float32)"}
{"line": 78, "text": ""}
{"line": 79, "text": ""}
{"line": 80, "text": "def randn(*shape, scale=0.02):"}
{"line": 81, "text": "    return ("}
{"line": 82, "text": "        np.random.randn(*shape).astype(np.float32) * scale"}
{"line": 83, "text": "    )"}

{"line": 85, "text": ""}
{"line": 86, "text": "def check_finite(name, x):"}
{"line": 87, "text": "    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):"}
{"line": 88, "text": "        bad = np.size(x) - np.isfinite(x).sum()"}
{"line": 89, "text": "        raise FloatingPointError("}
{"line": 90, "text": "            f\"[NONFINITE] {name}: {bad} non-finite values\""}
{"line": 91, "text": "        )"}
{"line": 92, "text": ""}

{"line": 95, "text": "# RoPE"}
{"line": 96, "text": "# ============================================================"}
{"line": 97, "text": ""}
{"line": 98, "text": "def precompute_rope(dim, end, theta=10000.0):"}
{"line": 99, "text": "    if dim % 2 != 0:"}
{"line": 100, "text": "        raise ValueError(\"RoPE head dimension must be even\")"}
{"line": 101, "text": ""}
{"line": 102, "text": "    inv_freq = 1.0 / ("}
{"line": 103, "text": "        theta ** ("}
{"line": 104, "text": "            np.arange(0, dim, 2, dtype=np.float32) / dim"}
{"line": 105, "text": "        )"}
{"line": 106, "text": "    )"}
{"line": 107, "text": ""}
{"line": 108, "text": "    positions = np.arange(end, dtype=np.float32)"}
{"line": 109, "text": ""}
{"line": 110, "text": "    angles = np.outer(positions, inv_freq)"}
{"line": 111, "text": ""}
{"line": 112, "text": "    cos = np.cos(angles).astype(np.float32)"}
{"line": 113, "text": "    sin = np.sin(angles).astype(np.float32)"}
{"line": 114, "text": ""}
{"line": 115, "text": "    return cos, sin"}
{"line": 116, "text": ""}
{"line": 117, "text": ""}
{"line": 118, "text": "def rope_rotate(x, cos, sin):"}
{"line": 119, "text": "    \"\"\""}
{"line": 120, "text": "    x: (B, H, T, DH)"}
{"line": 121, "text": "    cos/sin: (T, DH/2)"}
{"line": 122, "text": "    \"\"\""}
{"line": 123, "text": ""}
{"line": 124, "text": "    B, heads, seq, dim = x.shape"}
{"line": 125, "text": ""}
{"line": 126, "text": "    x_pair = x.reshape(B, heads, seq, dim // 2, 2)"}
{"line": 127, "text": ""}
{"line": 128, "text": "    even = x_pair[..., 0]"}
{"line": 129, "text": "    odd = x_pair[..., 1]"}
{"line": 130, "text": ""}
{"line": 131, "text": "    c = cos[:seq][None, None, :, :]"}
{"line": 132, "text": "    s = sin[:seq][None, None, :, :]"}
{"line": 133, "text": ""}
{"line": 134, "text": "    out_even = even * c - odd * s"}
{"line": 135, "text": "    out_odd = even * s + odd * c"}
{"line": 136, "text": ""}
{"line": 137, "text": "    out = np.stack("}
{"line": 138, "text": "        [out_even, out_odd],"}
{"line": 139, "text": "        axis=-1"}
{"line": 140, "text": "    )"}
{"line": 141, "text": ""}
{"line": 142, "text": "    return out.reshape(B, heads, seq, dim).astype(np.float32)"}
{"line": 143, "text": ""}
{"line": 144, "text": ""}
{"line": 145, "text": "def rope_inverse(grad, cos, sin):"}
{"line": 146, "text": "    \"\"\""}
{"line": 147, "text": "    Exact backward through orthogonal RoPE rotation."}
{"line": 148, "text": "    Inverse rotation = rotation by -angle."}
{"line": 149, "text": "    \"\"\""}
{"line": 150, "text": ""}
{"line": 151, "text": "    B, heads, seq, dim = grad.shape"}
{"line": 152, "text": ""}
{"line": 153, "text": "    g_pair = grad.reshape("}
{"line": 154, "text": "        B, heads, seq, dim // 2, 2"}
{"line": 155, "text": "    )"}
{"line": 156, "text": ""}
{"line": 157, "text": "    even = g_pair[..., 0]"}
{"line": 158, "text": "    odd = g_pair[..., 1]"}
{"line": 159, "text": ""}
{"line": 160, "text": "    c = cos[:seq][None, None, :, :]"}
{"line": 161, "text": "    s = sin[:seq][None, None, :, :]"}
{"line": 162, "text": ""}
{"line": 163, "text": "    out_even = even * c + odd * s"}
{"line": 164, "text": "    out_odd = -even * s + odd * c"}
{"line": 165, "text": ""}
{"line": 166, "text": "    out = np.stack("}
{"line": 167, "text": "        [out_even, out_odd],"}
{"line": 168, "text": "        axis=-1"}
{"line": 169, "text": "    )"}
{"line": 170, "text": ""}
{"line": 171, "text": "    return out.reshape(B, heads, seq, dim).astype(np.float32)"}
{"line": 172, "text": ""}
{"line": 173, "text": ""}
{"line": 174, "text": "# ============================================================"}
{"line": 175, "text": "# DATA"}

{"line": 177, "text": ""}

{"line": 241, "text": "def get_batch():"}
{"line": 242, "text": ""}
{"line": 243, "text": "    max_start = len(data) - T - 1"}
{"line": 244, "text": ""}

{"line": 257, "text": "    )"}
