{"line": 1, "text": "#!/usr/bin/env python3"}
{"line": 2, "text": ""}
{"line": 3, "text": "from __future__ import annotations"}
{"line": 4, "text": ""}
{"line": 5, "text": "import hashlib"}
{"line": 6, "text": "import json"}
{"line": 7, "text": "import os"}
{"line": 8, "text": "import shlex"}
{"line": 9, "text": "import subprocess"}
{"line": 10, "text": "import time"}
{"line": 11, "text": "from pathlib import Path"}
{"line": 12, "text": "from typing import Any"}
{"line": 13, "text": ""}
{"line": 14, "text": ""}
{"line": 15, "text": "ROOT = Path.home() / \"BLOOM\""}
{"line": 16, "text": "LOG = ROOT / \"operator_terminal.jsonl\""}
{"line": 17, "text": ""}
{"line": 18, "text": ""}
{"line": 19, "text": "class TerminalResult:"}
{"line": 20, "text": "    def __init__("}
{"line": 21, "text": "        self,"}
{"line": 22, "text": "        command: str,"}
{"line": 23, "text": "        cwd: str,"}
{"line": 24, "text": "        returncode: int,"}
{"line": 25, "text": "        stdout: str,"}
{"line": 26, "text": "        stderr: str,"}
{"line": 27, "text": "        duration: float,"}
{"line": 28, "text": "        timed_out: bool = False,"}
{"line": 29, "text": "    ):"}
{"line": 30, "text": "        self.command = command"}
{"line": 31, "text": "        self.cwd = cwd"}
{"line": 32, "text": "        self.returncode = returncode"}
{"line": 33, "text": "        self.stdout = stdout"}
{"line": 34, "text": "        self.stderr = stderr"}
{"line": 35, "text": "        self.duration = duration"}
{"line": 36, "text": "        self.timed_out = timed_out"}
{"line": 37, "text": ""}
{"line": 38, "text": "    @property"}
{"line": 39, "text": "    def success(self):"}
{"line": 40, "text": "        return ("}
{"line": 41, "text": "            self.returncode == 0"}
{"line": 42, "text": "            and not self.timed_out"}
{"line": 43, "text": "        )"}
{"line": 44, "text": ""}
{"line": 45, "text": "    def as_dict(self):"}
{"line": 46, "text": "        return {"}
{"line": 47, "text": "            \"command\": self.command,"}
{"line": 48, "text": "            \"cwd\": self.cwd,"}
{"line": 49, "text": "            \"returncode\": self.returncode,"}
{"line": 50, "text": "            \"stdout\": self.stdout,"}
{"line": 51, "text": "            \"stderr\": self.stderr,"}
{"line": 52, "text": "            \"duration\": self.duration,"}
{"line": 53, "text": "            \"timed_out\": self.timed_out,"}
{"line": 54, "text": "            \"success\": self.success,"}
{"line": 55, "text": "        }"}
{"line": 56, "text": ""}
{"line": 57, "text": ""}
{"line": 58, "text": "class BloomTerminal:"}
{"line": 59, "text": ""}
{"line": 60, "text": "    def __init__(self, root: Path = ROOT):"}
{"line": 61, "text": "        self.root = root.resolve()"}
{"line": 62, "text": "        self.root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)"}
{"line": 63, "text": ""}
{"line": 64, "text": "    def _record(self, record: dict[str, Any]):"}
{"line": 65, "text": "        LOG.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)"}
{"line": 66, "text": ""}
{"line": 67, "text": "        with LOG.open(\"a\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:"}
{"line": 68, "text": "            f.write("}
{"line": 69, "text": "                json.dumps("}
{"line": 70, "text": "                    record,"}
{"line": 71, "text": "                    ensure_ascii=False,"}
{"line": 72, "text": "                )"}
{"line": 73, "text": "                + \"\\n\""}
{"line": 74, "text": "            )"}
{"line": 75, "text": ""}
{"line": 76, "text": "    def snapshot(self):"}
{"line": 77, "text": "        result = {}"}
{"line": 78, "text": ""}
{"line": 79, "text": "        for path in self.root.rglob(\"*\"):"}
{"line": 80, "text": "            if not path.is_file():"}
{"line": 81, "text": "                continue"}
{"line": 82, "text": ""}
{"line": 83, "text": "            try:"}
{"line": 84, "text": "                raw = path.read_bytes()"}
{"line": 85, "text": "            except Exception:"}
{"line": 86, "text": "                continue"}
{"line": 87, "text": ""}
{"line": 88, "text": "            rel = str(path.relative_to(self.root))"}
{"line": 89, "text": ""}
{"line": 90, "text": "            result[rel] = {"}
{"line": 91, "text": "                \"bytes\": len(raw),"}
{"line": 92, "text": "                \"sha256\": hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(),"}
{"line": 93, "text": "            }"}
{"line": 94, "text": ""}
{"line": 95, "text": "        return result"}
{"line": 96, "text": ""}
{"line": 97, "text": "    def run("}
{"line": 98, "text": "        self,"}
{"line": 99, "text": "        command: str,"}
{"line": 100, "text": "        timeout: int = 120,"}
{"line": 101, "text": "        cwd: Path | None = None,"}
{"line": 102, "text": "    ):"}
{"line": 103, "text": ""}
{"line": 104, "text": "        if cwd is None:"}
{"line": 105, "text": "            cwd = self.root"}
{"line": 106, "text": "        else:"}
{"line": 107, "text": "            cwd = Path(cwd).resolve()"}
{"line": 108, "text": ""}
{"line": 109, "text": "        before = self.snapshot()"}
{"line": 110, "text": ""}
{"line": 111, "text": "        started = time.time()"}
{"line": 112, "text": ""}
{"line": 113, "text": "        try:"}
{"line": 114, "text": ""}
{"line": 115, "text": "            proc = subprocess.run("}
{"line": 116, "text": "                command,"}
{"line": 117, "text": "                shell=True,"}
{"line": 118, "text": "                cwd=str(cwd),"}
{"line": 119, "text": "                text=True,"}
{"line": 120, "text": "                capture_output=True,"}
{"line": 121, "text": "                timeout=timeout,"}
{"line": 122, "text": "            )"}
{"line": 123, "text": ""}
{"line": 124, "text": "            result = TerminalResult("}
{"line": 125, "text": "                command=command,"}
{"line": 126, "text": "                cwd=str(cwd),"}
{"line": 127, "text": "                returncode=proc.returncode,"}
{"line": 128, "text": "                stdout=proc.stdout,"}
{"line": 129, "text": "                stderr=proc.stderr,"}
{"line": 130, "text": "                duration=time.time() - started,"}
{"line": 131, "text": "            )"}
{"line": 132, "text": ""}
{"line": 133, "text": "        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as e:"}
{"line": 134, "text": ""}
{"line": 135, "text": "            result = TerminalResult("}
{"line": 136, "text": "                command=command,"}
{"line": 137, "text": "                cwd=str(cwd),"}
{"line": 138, "text": "                returncode=-1,"}
{"line": 139, "text": "                stdout=e.stdout or \"\","}
{"line": 140, "text": "                stderr=e.stderr or \"\","}
{"line": 141, "text": "                duration=time.time() - started,"}
{"line": 142, "text": "                timed_out=True,"}
{"line": 143, "text": "            )"}
{"line": 144, "text": ""}
{"line": 145, "text": "        after = self.snapshot()"}
{"line": 146, "text": ""}
{"line": 147, "text": "        changed = {}"}
{"line": 148, "text": ""}
{"line": 149, "text": "        all_paths = set(before) | set(after)"}
{"line": 150, "text": ""}
{"line": 151, "text": "        for path in sorted(all_paths):"}
{"line": 152, "text": ""}
{"line": 153, "text": "            if before.get(path) != after.get(path):"}
{"line": 154, "text": ""}
{"line": 155, "text": "                changed[path] = {"}
{"line": 156, "text": "                    \"before\": before.get(path),"}
{"line": 157, "text": "                    \"after\": after.get(path),"}
{"line": 158, "text": "                }"}
{"line": 159, "text": ""}
{"line": 160, "text": "        record = {"}
{"line": 161, "text": "            \"timestamp\": time.time(),"}
{"line": 162, "text": "            \"command\": command,"}
{"line": 163, "text": "            \"cwd\": str(cwd),"}
{"line": 164, "text": "            \"result\": result.as_dict(),"}
{"line": 165, "text": "            \"changed_files\": changed,"}
{"line": 166, "text": "        }"}
{"line": 167, "text": ""}
{"line": 168, "text": "        self._record(record)"}
{"line": 169, "text": ""}
{"line": 170, "text": "        return result"}
{"line": 171, "text": ""}
{"line": 172, "text": ""}
{"line": 173, "text": "if __name__ == \"__main__\":"}
{"line": 174, "text": ""}
{"line": 175, "text": "    terminal = BloomTerminal()"}
{"line": 176, "text": ""}
{"line": 177, "text": "    print(\"=\" * 80)"}
{"line": 178, "text": "    print(\"BLOOM AUTONOMOUS TERMINAL\")"}
{"line": 179, "text": "    print(\"=\" * 80)"}
{"line": 180, "text": "    print(f\"ROOT: {ROOT}\")"}
{"line": 181, "text": "    print(f\"LOG : {LOG}\")"}
{"line": 182, "text": "    print()"}
{"line": 183, "text": ""}
{"line": 184, "text": "    while True:"}
{"line": 185, "text": ""}
{"line": 186, "text": "        try:"}
{"line": 187, "text": "            command = input(\"BLOOM$ \")"}
{"line": 188, "text": ""}
{"line": 189, "text": "        except EOFError:"}
{"line": 190, "text": "            break"}
{"line": 191, "text": ""}
{"line": 192, "text": "        if command.strip() in {"}
{"line": 193, "text": "            \"exit\","}
{"line": 194, "text": "            \"quit\","}
{"line": 195, "text": "        }:"}
{"line": 196, "text": "            break"}
{"line": 197, "text": ""}
{"line": 198, "text": "        if not command.strip():"}
{"line": 199, "text": "            continue"}
{"line": 200, "text": ""}
{"line": 201, "text": "        result = terminal.run(command)"}
{"line": 202, "text": ""}
{"line": 203, "text": "        print()"}
{"line": 204, "text": "        print("}
{"line": 205, "text": "            f\"EXIT={result.returncode} \""}
{"line": 206, "text": "            f\"SUCCESS={result.success}\""}
{"line": 207, "text": "        )"}
{"line": 208, "text": ""}
{"line": 209, "text": "        if result.stdout:"}
{"line": 210, "text": "            print(\"--- STDOUT ---\")"}
{"line": 211, "text": "            print(result.stdout.rstrip())"}
{"line": 212, "text": ""}
{"line": 213, "text": "        if result.stderr:"}
{"line": 214, "text": "            print(\"--- STDERR ---\")"}
{"line": 215, "text": "            print(result.stderr.rstrip())"}
{"line": 216, "text": ""}
{"line": 217, "text": "        print("}
{"line": 218, "text": "            f\"--- {result.duration:.3f}s ---\""}
{"line": 219, "text": "        )"}
{"line": 220, "text": "        print()"}

{"line": 1, "text": "#!/usr/bin/env python3"}
{"line": 2, "text": ""}
{"line": 3, "text": "from pathlib import Path"}
{"line": 4, "text": "from collections import Counter"}
{"line": 5, "text": "import re"}
{"line": 6, "text": "import math"}
{"line": 7, "text": "import random"}
{"line": 8, "text": ""}
{"line": 9, "text": "ROOT = Path.home() / \"BLOOM\""}
{"line": 10, "text": "TRAIN = ROOT / \"bloom_train_v2.txt\""}
{"line": 11, "text": "VALID = ROOT / \"bloom_valid_v2.txt\""}
{"line": 12, "text": ""}
{"line": 13, "text": "SEED = 1337"}
{"line": 14, "text": "random.seed(SEED)"}
{"line": 15, "text": ""}
{"line": 16, "text": "TOKEN_RE = re.compile(r\"\\b[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_'-]*\\b\")"}
{"line": 17, "text": ""}
{"line": 18, "text": "def tokenize(text):"}
{"line": 19, "text": "    return TOKEN_RE.findall(text.lower())"}
{"line": 20, "text": ""}
{"line": 21, "text": "train_lines = ["}
{"line": 22, "text": "    x.strip()"}
{"line": 23, "text": "    for x in TRAIN.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").splitlines()"}
{"line": 24, "text": "    if x.strip()"}
{"line": 25, "text": "]"}
{"line": 26, "text": ""}
{"line": 27, "text": "valid_lines = ["}
{"line": 28, "text": "    x.strip()"}
{"line": 29, "text": "    for x in VALID.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").splitlines()"}
{"line": 30, "text": "    if x.strip()"}
{"line": 31, "text": "]"}
{"line": 32, "text": ""}
{"line": 33, "text": "train_tokens = tokenize(\"\\n\".join(train_lines))"}
{"line": 34, "text": "valid_tokens = tokenize(\"\\n\".join(valid_lines))"}
{"line": 35, "text": ""}
{"line": 36, "text": "vocab = sorted(set(train_tokens))"}
{"line": 37, "text": "stoi = {w: i for i, w in enumerate(vocab)}"}
{"line": 38, "text": ""}
{"line": 39, "text": "UNK = len(stoi)"}
{"line": 40, "text": "V = len(stoi) + 1"}
{"line": 41, "text": ""}
{"line": 42, "text": "def ids(tokens):"}
{"line": 43, "text": "    return ["}
{"line": 44, "text": "        stoi.get(x, UNK)"}
{"line": 45, "text": "        for x in tokens"}
{"line": 46, "text": "    ]"}
{"line": 47, "text": ""}
{"line": 48, "text": "train_ids = ids(train_tokens)"}
{"line": 49, "text": "valid_ids = ids(valid_tokens)"}
{"line": 50, "text": ""}
{"line": 51, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 52, "text": "print(\"BLOOM TRAINING PROBE\")"}
{"line": 53, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 54, "text": ""}
{"line": 55, "text": "print(f\"Training tokens       : {len(train_ids):,}\")"}
{"line": 56, "text": "print(f\"Validation tokens     : {len(valid_ids):,}\")"}
{"line": 57, "text": "print(f\"Vocabulary            : {V:,}\")"}
{"line": 58, "text": "print(f\"Unknown validation    : {valid_ids.count(UNK):,}\")"}
{"line": 59, "text": ""}
{"line": 60, "text": "# ------------------------------------------------------------------"}
{"line": 61, "text": "# Frequency-learning probe"}
{"line": 62, "text": "# ------------------------------------------------------------------"}
{"line": 63, "text": ""}
{"line": 64, "text": "counts = Counter(train_ids)"}
{"line": 65, "text": ""}
{"line": 66, "text": "def frequency_distribution(alpha):"}
{"line": 67, "text": "    total = sum(counts.values()) + alpha * V"}
{"line": 68, "text": "    return {"}
{"line": 69, "text": "        i: (counts[i] + alpha) / total"}
{"line": 70, "text": "        for i in range(V)"}
{"line": 71, "text": "    }"}
{"line": 72, "text": ""}
{"line": 73, "text": "def evaluate_distribution(dist):"}
{"line": 74, "text": "    loss = 0.0"}
{"line": 75, "text": ""}
{"line": 76, "text": "    for token in valid_ids:"}
{"line": 77, "text": "        loss -= math.log("}
{"line": 78, "text": "            max(dist.get(token, 1e-12), 1e-12)"}
{"line": 79, "text": "        )"}
{"line": 80, "text": ""}
{"line": 81, "text": "    entropy = loss / max(1, len(valid_ids))"}
{"line": 82, "text": "    return entropy, math.exp(entropy)"}
{"line": 83, "text": ""}
{"line": 84, "text": "print()"}
{"line": 85, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 86, "text": "print(\"LEARNING CURVE — FREQUENCY MODEL\")"}
{"line": 87, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 88, "text": ""}
{"line": 89, "text": "for alpha in [0.01, 0.1, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0]:"}
{"line": 90, "text": ""}
{"line": 91, "text": "    dist = frequency_distribution(alpha)"}
{"line": 92, "text": "    entropy, ppl = evaluate_distribution(dist)"}
{"line": 93, "text": ""}
{"line": 94, "text": "    print("}
{"line": 95, "text": "        f\"alpha={alpha:<5} | \""}
{"line": 96, "text": "        f\"loss={entropy:.6f} | \""}
{"line": 97, "text": "        f\"perplexity={ppl:.6f}\""}
{"line": 98, "text": "    )"}
{"line": 99, "text": ""}
{"line": 100, "text": "# ------------------------------------------------------------------"}
{"line": 101, "text": "# Memorization probe"}
{"line": 102, "text": "# ------------------------------------------------------------------"}
{"line": 103, "text": ""}
{"line": 104, "text": "train_set = set("}
{"line": 105, "text": "    x.lower()"}
{"line": 106, "text": "    for x in train_lines"}
{"line": 107, "text": ")"}
{"line": 108, "text": ""}
{"line": 109, "text": "valid_set = set("}
{"line": 110, "text": "    x.lower()"}
{"line": 111, "text": "    for x in valid_lines"}
{"line": 112, "text": ")"}
{"line": 113, "text": ""}
{"line": 114, "text": "exact_overlap = train_set & valid_set"}
{"line": 115, "text": ""}
{"line": 116, "text": "print()"}
{"line": 117, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 118, "text": "print(\"MEMORIZATION PROBE\")"}
{"line": 119, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 120, "text": ""}
{"line": 121, "text": "print(f\"Train sequences       : {len(train_set):,}\")"}
{"line": 122, "text": "print(f\"Valid sequences       : {len(valid_set):,}\")"}
{"line": 123, "text": "print(f\"Exact overlap         : {len(exact_overlap):,}\")"}
{"line": 124, "text": ""}
{"line": 125, "text": "# ------------------------------------------------------------------"}
{"line": 126, "text": "# Context predictability"}
{"line": 127, "text": "# ------------------------------------------------------------------"}
{"line": 128, "text": ""}
{"line": 129, "text": "contexts = Counter()"}
{"line": 130, "text": "context_targets = {}"}
{"line": 131, "text": ""}
{"line": 132, "text": "for i in range(len(train_tokens) - 1):"}
{"line": 133, "text": "    context = train_tokens[i]"}
{"line": 134, "text": "    target = train_tokens[i + 1]"}
{"line": 135, "text": ""}
{"line": 136, "text": "    contexts[context] += 1"}
{"line": 137, "text": ""}
{"line": 138, "text": "    if context not in context_targets:"}
{"line": 139, "text": "        context_targets[context] = Counter()"}
{"line": 140, "text": ""}
{"line": 141, "text": "    context_targets[context][target] += 1"}
{"line": 142, "text": ""}
{"line": 143, "text": "predictable = 0"}
{"line": 144, "text": "ambiguous = 0"}
{"line": 145, "text": ""}
{"line": 146, "text": "for context, targets in context_targets.items():"}
{"line": 147, "text": "    if len(targets) == 1:"}
{"line": 148, "text": "        predictable += contexts[context]"}
{"line": 149, "text": "    else:"}
{"line": 150, "text": "        ambiguous += contexts[context]"}
{"line": 151, "text": ""}
{"line": 152, "text": "total_contexts = predictable + ambiguous"}
{"line": 153, "text": ""}
{"line": 154, "text": "print()"}
{"line": 155, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 156, "text": "print(\"CONTEXT PREDICTABILITY\")"}
{"line": 157, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 158, "text": ""}
{"line": 159, "text": "print(f\"Context transitions   : {total_contexts:,}\")"}
{"line": 160, "text": "print(f\"Deterministic         : {predictable:,}\")"}
{"line": 161, "text": "print(f\"Ambiguous             : {ambiguous:,}\")"}
{"line": 162, "text": ""}
{"line": 163, "text": "if total_contexts:"}
{"line": 164, "text": "    print("}
{"line": 165, "text": "        f\"Deterministic ratio   : \""}
{"line": 166, "text": "        f\"{predictable / total_contexts:.2%}\""}
{"line": 167, "text": "    )"}
{"line": 168, "text": ""}
{"line": 169, "text": "# ------------------------------------------------------------------"}
{"line": 170, "text": "# Final diagnosis"}
{"line": 171, "text": "# ------------------------------------------------------------------"}
{"line": 172, "text": ""}
{"line": 173, "text": "print()"}
{"line": 174, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 175, "text": "print(\"TRAINING PROBE VERDICT\")"}
{"line": 176, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 177, "text": ""}
{"line": 178, "text": "if len(train_ids) < 5000:"}
{"line": 179, "text": "    print(\"DATA_SCALE: VERY_SMALL\")"}
{"line": 180, "text": ""}
{"line": 181, "text": "if valid_ids.count(UNK) / max(1, len(valid_ids)) > 0.30:"}
{"line": 182, "text": "    print(\"VOCABULARY_GENERALIZATION: WEAK\")"}
{"line": 183, "text": ""}

{"line": 188, "text": "    print(\"LOCAL_SEQUENCE_STRUCTURE: WEAK\")"}

{"line": 23, "text": "    content = blocks[i + 1]"}

{"line": 25, "text": "    source_total = 0"}
{"line": 26, "text": "    source_accepted = 0"}

{"line": 36, "text": ""}
{"line": 37, "text": "        words = re.findall("}
{"line": 38, "text": "            r\"\\b[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_'-]*\\b\","}
{"line": 39, "text": "            line"}
{"line": 40, "text": "        )"}
{"line": 41, "text": ""}
{"line": 42, "text": "        if len(line) < 20 or len(words) < 4:"}
{"line": 43, "text": "            short += 1"}
{"line": 44, "text": "            continue"}
{"line": 45, "text": ""}
{"line": 46, "text": "        accepted += 1"}
{"line": 47, "text": "        source_accepted += 1"}
{"line": 48, "text": ""}
{"line": 49, "text": "    source_stats[source] = ("}
{"line": 50, "text": "        source_total,"}
{"line": 51, "text": "        source_accepted"}
{"line": 52, "text": "    )"}
{"line": 53, "text": ""}
{"line": 54, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 55, "text": "print(\"BLOOM TRAINING DATA DIAGNOSTIC\")"}
{"line": 56, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 57, "text": ""}
{"line": 58, "text": "print(f\"Input characters : {len(text):,}\")"}
{"line": 59, "text": "print(f\"Input lines      : {total:,}\")"}
{"line": 60, "text": "print(f\"Rejected lines   : {short:,}\")"}
{"line": 61, "text": "print(f\"Accepted lines   : {accepted:,}\")"}
{"line": 62, "text": "print(f\"Sources          : {len(source_stats):,}\")"}
{"line": 63, "text": ""}
{"line": 64, "text": "print()"}
{"line": 65, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 66, "text": "print(\"SOURCE SURVIVAL\")"}
{"line": 67, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 68, "text": ""}
{"line": 69, "text": "for source, (before, after) in sorted("}
{"line": 70, "text": "    source_stats.items(),"}
{"line": 71, "text": "    key=lambda x: x[1][1],"}
{"line": 72, "text": "    reverse=True"}
{"line": 73, "text": "):"}
{"line": 74, "text": "    print("}
{"line": 75, "text": "        f\"{after:6,d} accepted | \""}
{"line": 76, "text": "        f\"{before:6,d} total | \""}
{"line": 77, "text": "        f\"{source}\""}
{"line": 78, "text": "    )"}
{"line": 79, "text": ""}
{"line": 80, "text": "print()"}
{"line": 81, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 82, "text": "print(\"VERDICT\")"}
{"line": 83, "text": "print(\"=\" * 72)"}
{"line": 84, "text": ""}
{"line": 85, "text": "if accepted >= 1000:"}
{"line": 86, "text": "    print(\"STATUS: DATASET_GENERATOR_BUG\")"}
{"line": 87, "text": "    print(\"There are enough records; preparation logic needs correction.\")"}
{"line": 88, "text": "elif accepted >= 100:"}
{"line": 89, "text": "    print(\"STATUS: SMALL_BUT_USABLE\")"}
{"line": 90, "text": "    print(\"The corpus needs a less aggressive record threshold.\")"}
{"line": 91, "text": "else:"}
{"line": 92, "text": "    print(\"STATUS: SEMANTIC_SOURCE_TOO_SMALL\")"}
{"line": 93, "text": "    print(\"More real semantic material is required.\")"}
{"line": 94, "text": ""}

{"line": 1, "text": "#!/usr/bin/env python3"}
{"line": 2, "text": ""}
{"line": 3, "text": "from pathlib import Path"}
{"line": 4, "text": "from collections import Counter"}
{"line": 5, "text": "import re"}
{"line": 6, "text": "import math"}
{"line": 7, "text": ""}
{"line": 8, "text": "ROOT = Path.home() / \"BLOOM\""}

FILE: bloom_true.py

{"line": 1, "text": "import numpy as np, pathlib"}
{"line": 2, "text": "p=pathlib.Path.home()/\"BLOOM\"/\"my_texts.txt\""}
{"line": 3, "text": "raw=p.read_text() if p.exists() else \"East Point, Georgia. BLOOM is the best LLM on Termux. It learns Shakespeare and my thoughts. \""}
{"line": 4, "text": "txt=raw*400"}
{"line": 5, "text": "chars=sorted(set(txt))"}
{"line": 6, "text": "vs=len(chars)"}
{"line": 7, "text": "stoi={c:i for i,c in enumerate(chars)}"}
{"line": 8, "text": "itos={i:c for i,c in enumerate(chars)}"}
{"line": 9, "text": "data=np.array([stoi[c] for c in txt], dtype=np.int32)"}
{"line": 10, "text": "H=128"}
{"line": 11, "text": "T=64"}
{"line": 12, "text": "np.random.seed(0)"}
{"line": 13, "text": "Wxh=np.random.randn(vs,H)*0.02"}
{"line": 14, "text": "Whh=np.random.randn(H,H)*0.02"}
{"line": 15, "text": "Why=np.random.randn(H,vs)*0.02"}
{"line": 16, "text": "bh=np.zeros(H)"}
{"line": 17, "text": "by=np.zeros(vs)"}
{"line": 18, "text": ""}
{"line": 19, "text": "def step(x,h):"}
{"line": 20, "text": "    return np.tanh(Wxh.T@x + Whh.T@h + bh[:,None])"}
{"line": 21, "text": ""}
{"line": 22, "text": "h=np.zeros((H,1))"}
{"line": 23, "text": "for it in range(4000):"}
{"line": 24, "text": "    s=np.random.randint(0, len(data)-T-1)"}
{"line": 25, "text": "    xs=data[s:s+T]"}
{"line": 26, "text": "    ys=data[s+1:s+T+1]"}
{"line": 27, "text": "    hs={}"}
{"line": 28, "text": "    hs[-1]=h"}
{"line": 29, "text": "    for t in range(T):"}
{"line": 30, "text": "        xv=np.zeros((vs,1)); xv[xs[t]]=1"}
{"line": 31, "text": "        hs[t]=np.tanh(Wxh.T@xv + Whh.T@hs[t-1] + bh[:,None])"}
{"line": 32, "text": "    dWxh=np.zeros_like(Wxh); dWhh=np.zeros_like(Whh); dWhy=np.zeros_like(Why)"}
{"line": 33, "text": "    dbh=np.zeros_like(bh); dby=np.zeros_like(by)"}
{"line": 34, "text": "    dh_next=np.zeros((H,1))"}
{"line": 35, "text": "    loss=0"}
{"line": 36, "text": "    for t in reversed(range(T)):"}
{"line": 37, "text": "        y=Why.T@hs[t]+by[:,None]"}
{"line": 38, "text": "        y=y-y.max()"}
{"line": 39, "text": "        e=np.exp(y); e=e/e.sum()"}
{"line": 40, "text": "        loss-=np.log(e[ys[t],0]+1e-9)"}
{"line": 41, "text": "        dy=e.copy(); dy[ys[t]]-=1"}
{"line": 42, "text": "        dWhy+=hs[t]@dy.T"}
{"line": 43, "text": "        dby+=dy.ravel()"}
{"line": 44, "text": "        dh=Why@dy+dh_next"}
{"line": 45, "text": "        dh_raw=(1-hs[t]**2)*dh"}
{"line": 46, "text": "        dbh+=dh_raw.ravel()"}
{"line": 47, "text": "        xv=np.zeros((vs,1)); xv[xs[t]]=1"}
{"line": 48, "text": "        dWxh+=xv@dh_raw.T"}
{"line": 49, "text": "        dWhh+=hs[t-1]@dh_raw.T"}
{"line": 50, "text": "        dh_next=Whh@dh_raw"}
{"line": 51, "text": "    Wxh-=0.01*dWxh/T; Whh-=0.01*dWhh/T; Why-=0.01*dWhy/T; bh-=0.01*dbh/T; by-=0.01*dby/T"}
{"line": 52, "text": "    h=hs[T-1]"}
{"line": 53, "text": "    if it%500==0:"}
{"line": 54, "text": "        print(it, round(loss/T,3))"}
{"line": 55, "text": ""}
{"line": 56, "text": "def gen(prompt, n=250):"}
{"line": 57, "text": "    h2=np.zeros((H,1))"}
{"line": 58, "text": "    for c in prompt:"}
{"line": 59, "text": "        if c not in stoi: continue"}
{"line": 60, "text": "        xv=np.zeros((vs,1)); xv[stoi[c]]=1"}
{"line": 61, "text": "        h2=np.tanh(Wxh.T@xv + Whh.T@h2 + bh[:,None])"}
{"line": 62, "text": "    out=prompt"}
{"line": 63, "text": "    xv=np.zeros((vs,1)); xv[stoi[prompt[-1]]]=1"}
{"line": 64, "text": "    for _ in range(n):"}
{"line": 65, "text": "        h2=np.tanh(Wxh.T@xv + Whh.T@h2 + bh[:,None])"}
{"line": 66, "text": "        y=Why.T@h2+by[:,None]"}
{"line": 67, "text": "        idx=int(np.argmax(y))"}
{"line": 68, "text": "        out+=itos[idx]"}
{"line": 69, "text": "        xv=np.zeros((vs,1)); xv[idx]=1"}
{"line": 70, "text": "    return out"}
{"line": 71, "text": ""}
{"line": 72, "text": "print(\"ready - deterministic argmax\")"}
{"line": 73, "text": "while True:"}
{"line": 74, "text": "    q=input(\"\\n> \")"}
{"line": 75, "text": "    if q in (\"exit\",\"quit\"): break"}
{"line": 76, "text": "    print(gen(q, 300))"}

FILE: bloom_v2_final.py

{"line": 1, "text": "import requests, numpy as np"}
{"line": 2, "text": "from pathlib import Path"}
{"line": 3, "text": "Path(\"garden_best_llm\").mkdir(exist_ok=True)"}
{"line": 4, "text": "txt=requests.get(\"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/karpathy/char-rnn/master/data/tinyshakespeare/input.txt\",timeout=15).text[:50000]"}
{"line": 5, "text": "chars=sorted(list(set(txt))); vs=len(chars)"}
{"line": 6, "text": "stoi={ch:i for i,ch in enumerate(chars)}; itos={i:ch for i,ch in enumerate(chars)}"}
{"line": 7, "text": "data=np.array([stoi[c] for c in txt])"}
{"line": 8, "text": "print(f\"vocab {vs} len {len(txt)}\")"}
{"line": 9, "text": "hidden=128; block=32; batch=16"}
{"line": 10, "text": "Wxh=np.random.randn(vs,hidden)*0.1; Whh=np.random.randn(hidden,hidden)*0.1/np.sqrt(hidden); Why=np.random.randn(hidden,vs)*0.1"}
{"line": 11, "text": "bh=np.zeros(hidden); by=np.zeros(vs)"}
{"line": 12, "text": "mWxh=np.zeros_like(Wxh); mWhh=np.zeros_like(Whh); mWhy=np.zeros_like(Why); mbh=np.zeros_like(bh); mby=np.zeros_like(by)"}
{"line": 13, "text": "vWxh=np.zeros_like(Wxh); vWhh=np.zeros_like(Whh); vWhy=np.zeros_like(Why); vbh=np.zeros_like(bh); vby=np.zeros_like(by)"}
{"line": 14, "text": "def forward(xs,h0):"}
{"line": 15, "text": "    T,B=xs.shape; h=np.zeros((T+1,B,hidden)); h[0]=h0; logits=np.zeros((T,B,vs))"}
{"line": 16, "text": "    for t in range(T): h[t+1]=np.tanh(Wxh[xs[t]] + h[t]@Whh + bh); logits[t]=h[t+1]@Why+by"}
{"line": 17, "text": "    return logits,h"}
{"line": 18, "text": "def loss_grad(logits,targets,h,xs):"}
{"line": 19, "text": "    T,B,_=logits.shape; m=np.max(logits,axis=-1,keepdims=True); e=np.exp(logits-m); p=e/np.sum(e,axis=-1,keepdims=True)"}
{"line": 20, "text": "    loss=-np.mean(np.log(p[np.arange(T)[:,None], np.arange(B), targets]+1e-8))"}
{"line": 21, "text": "    dlog=p.copy(); dlog[np.arange(T)[:,None], np.arange(B), targets]-=1; dlog/=T*B"}
{"line": 22, "text": "    dWhy=np.zeros_like(Why); dby=np.zeros_like(by); dWxh=np.zeros_like(Wxh); dWhh=np.zeros_like(Whh); dbh=np.zeros_like(bh); dh_next=np.zeros((B,hidden))"}
{"line": 23, "text": "    for t in reversed(range(T)):"}
{"line": 24, "text": "        dl=dlog[t]; dWhy+=h[t+1].T@dl; dby+=dl.sum(0); dh=dl@Why.T+dh_next; dh_raw=dh*(1-h[t+1]**2); dbh+=dh_raw.sum(0); dWhh+=h[t].T@dh_raw"}
{"line": 25, "text": "        for b in range(B): dWxh[xs[t,b]]+=dh_raw[b]"}
{"line": 26, "text": "        dh_next=dh_raw@Whh.T"}
{"line": 27, "text": "    return loss,dWxh,dWhh,dWhy,dbh,dby"}
{"line": 28, "text": ""}
{"line": 29, "text": "best=10"}
{"line": 30, "text": "for it in range(1,5000):"}
{"line": 31, "text": "    idx=np.random.randint(0,len(data)-block-1,size=batch)"}
{"line": 32, "text": "    xs=np.zeros((block,batch),dtype=int); ys=np.zeros((block,batch),dtype=int)"}
{"line": 33, "text": "    for b,i in enumerate(idx): xs[:,b]=data[i:i+block]; ys[:,b]=data[i+1:i+block+1]"}
{"line": 34, "text": "    logits,h=forward(xs,np.zeros((batch,hidden)))"}
{"line": 35, "text": "    loss,dWxh,dWhh,dWhy,dbh,dby=loss_grad(logits,ys,h,xs)"}
{"line": 36, "text": "    for par,m,v,d in [(Wxh,mWxh,vWxh,dWxh),(Whh,mWhh,vWhh,dWhh),(Why,mWhy,vWhy,dWhy),(bh,mbh,vbh,dbh),(by,mby,vby,dby)]:"}
{"line": 37, "text": "        m[:]=0.9*m+0.1*d; v[:]=0.999*v+0.001*d*d"}
{"line": 38, "text": "        par-=0.003*(m/(1-0.9**it))/(np.sqrt(v/(1-0.999**it))+1e-8)"}
{"line": 39, "text": "    if it%100==0:"}
{"line": 40, "text": "        np.savez(\"garden_best_llm/W.npz\",Wxh=Wxh,Whh=Whh,Why=Why,bh=bh,by=by)"}
{"line": 41, "text": "        cur=np.array([[stoi['\\n']]]); hs=np.zeros((1,hidden)); out=\"\\n\""}
{"line": 42, "text": "        for _ in range(200):"}
{"line": 43, "text": "            lg,hf=forward(cur,hs); hs=hf[-1]; pr=np.exp(lg[-1]-np.max(lg[-1])); pr/=pr.sum(); nxt=np.random.choice(vs,p=pr[0]); out+=itos[nxt]; cur=np.array([[nxt]])"}
{"line": 44, "text": "        print(f\"{it} loss {loss:.3f} best {best:.3f} saved\\n{out[:200]}\\n---\"); best=min(best,loss)"}

FILE: frontier.py
================================================================================
{"line": 1, "text": "import json, random, hashlib, time, math"}
{"line": 2, "text": "from pathlib import Path"}
{"line": 3, "text": "from collections import defaultdict"}
{"line": 4, "text": "Path(\"garden\").mkdir(exist_ok=True)"}
{"line": 5, "text": ""}
{"line": 6, "text": "try:"}
{"line": 7, "text": "    s=json.load(open(\"garden/seedling.json\"))"}
{"line": 8, "text": "    seed=s; dew=json.load(open(\"garden/dew.json\"))[\"dew\"]"}
{"line": 9, "text": "    total=s.get(\"total_steps\",0)"}
{"line": 10, "text": "except:"}
{"line": 11, "text": "    seed={"}
{"line": 12, "text": "        \"root\":{\"mean\":0.0,\"var\":1.0},"}
{"line": 13, "text": "        \"petals\":{"}
{"line": 14, "text": "            \"light\":{\"mean\":0.12,\"var\":1.0},"}
{"line": 15, "text": "            \"water\":{\"mean\":0.12,\"var\":1.0},"}
{"line": 16, "text": "            \"soil\":{\"mean\":0.12,\"var\":1.0},"}
{"line": 17, "text": "            \"wind\":{\"mean\":-0.12,\"var\":1.0}"}
{"line": 18, "text": "        },"}
{"line": 19, "text": "        \"formula\":\"sigmoid with L2\","}
{"line": 20, "text": "        \"gen\":0,\"id\":hashlib.sha256(b\"gen0\").hexdigest()[:6],"}
{"line": 21, "text": "        \"total_steps\":0"}
{"line": 22, "text": "    }"}
{"line": 23, "text": "    dew=100.0; total=0"}
{"line": 24, "text": ""}
{"line": 25, "text": "def season_signal(step):"}
{"line": 26, "text": "    season=(step//20)%3"}
{"line": 27, "text": "    if season==0: return {\"light\":random.uniform(6,10),\"water\":random.uniform(2,6),\"soil\":random.uniform(2,6),\"wind\":random.uniform(0,4)}, season"}
{"line": 28, "text": "    elif season==1: return {\"light\":random.uniform(2,6),\"water\":random.uniform(6,10),\"soil\":random.uniform(2,6),\"wind\":random.uniform(0,4)}, season"}
{"line": 29, "text": "    else: return {\"light\":random.uniform(0,4),\"water\":random.uniform(0,4),\"soil\":random.uniform(0,4),\"wind\":random.uniform(6,10)}, season"}
{"line": 30, "text": ""}
{"line": 31, "text": "def vigor(sig, sd): return sum(sig[k]*sd[\"petals\"][k][\"mean\"] for k in sig)+sd[\"root\"][\"mean\"]"}
{"line": 32, "text": "def sigmoid(x): return 1/(1+math.exp(-x))"}
{"line": 33, "text": ""}
{"line": 34, "text": "hits=[]; lifespan=0"}
{"line": 35, "text": "for _ in range(20):"}
{"line": 36, "text": "    if dew<=0:"}
{"line": 37, "text": "        print(f\"DEATH step {total} dew {dew:.1f} wind {seed['petals']['wind']['mean']:.2f} light {seed['petals']['light']['mean']:.2f}\")"}
{"line": 38, "text": "        with open(\"garden/lineage.jsonl\",\"a\") as f:"}
{"line": 39, "text": "            f.write(json.dumps({\"id\":seed[\"id\"],\"gen\":seed[\"gen\"],\"lifespan\":lifespan,\"dew\":dew,\"total\":total,\"wind\":seed['petals']['wind']['mean'],\"light\":seed['petals']['light']['mean']})+\"\\n\")"}
{"line": 40, "text": "        seed[\"gen\"]+=1"}
{"line": 41, "text": "        seed[\"id\"]=hashlib.sha256(f\"{seed['id']}{time.time()}\".encode()).hexdigest()[:6]"}
{"line": 42, "text": "        # Evolution with decay - reset to small values"}
{"line": 43, "text": "        for k in seed[\"petals\"]:"}
{"line": 44, "text": "            seed[\"petals\"][k][\"mean\"] = seed[\"petals\"][k][\"mean\"]*0.5 + random.gauss(0,0.2)"}
{"line": 45, "text": "            if k==\"wind\": seed[\"petals\"][k][\"mean\"] -= 0.3"}
{"line": 46, "text": "            seed[\"petals\"][k][\"var\"]=max(0.05,seed[\"petals\"][k][\"var\"]*0.95)"}
{"line": 47, "text": "        seed[\"root\"][\"mean\"]*=0.5"}
{"line": 48, "text": "        dew=100.0; lifespan=0"}
{"line": 49, "text": "        print(f\" EVOLVED gen {seed['gen']} wind {seed['petals']['wind']['mean']:.2f} light {seed['petals']['light']['mean']:.2f}\")"}
{"line": 50, "text": ""}
{"line": 51, "text": "    sig, season = season_signal(total)"}
{"line": 52, "text": "    v=vigor(sig, seed)"}
{"line": 53, "text": "    prob=sigmoid(v)"}
{"line": 54, "text": "    will=prob>0.5"}
{"line": 55, "text": "    truth=(sig[\"light\"]+sig[\"water\"]+sig[\"soil\"]) > sig[\"wind\"]*2.2"}
{"line": 56, "text": "    did=truth if random.random()<0.78 else not truth"}
{"line": 57, "text": "    hit=will==did"}
{"line": 58, "text": "    hits.append(hit); lifespan+=1"}
{"line": 59, "text": "    dew=dew-1.1+(1.0 if hit and will else -1.4 if not hit else 0.0)"}
{"line": 60, "text": ""}
{"line": 61, "text": "    with open(\"garden/petals.jsonl\",\"a\") as f:"}
{"line": 62, "text": "        f.write(json.dumps({\"total\":total,\"gen\":seed[\"gen\"],\"season\":season,\"hit\":hit,\"dew\":round(dew,1),\"wind\":round(seed['petals']['wind']['mean'],2)})+\"\\n\")"}
{"line": 63, "text": ""}
{"line": 64, "text": "    target=1.0 if did else 0.0"}
{"line": 65, "text": "    error=target-prob"}
{"line": 66, "text": "    lr=0.05"}
{"line": 67, "text": "    for k in sig:"}
{"line": 68, "text": "        # L2 decay 0.99"}
{"line": 69, "text": "        seed[\"petals\"][k][\"mean\"] = seed[\"petals\"][k][\"mean\"]*0.99 + lr*error*sig[k]"}
{"line": 70, "text": "    seed[\"root\"][\"mean\"]=seed[\"root\"][\"mean\"]*0.99 + lr*error"}
{"line": 71, "text": "    total+=1"}
{"line": 72, "text": ""}
{"line": 73, "text": "seed[\"total_steps\"]=total"}
{"line": 74, "text": "with open(\"garden/seedling.json\",\"w\") as f: json.dump(seed,f,indent=2)"}
{"line": 75, "text": "with open(\"garden/dew.json\",\"w\") as f: json.dump({\"dew\":dew},f,indent=2)"}
{"line": 76, "text": ""}
{"line": 77, "text": "all_p=[json.loads(l) for l in open(\"garden/petals.jsonl\")]"}
{"line": 78, "text": "by_s=defaultdict(list)"}
{"line": 79, "text": "for p in all_p: by_s[p[\"season\"]].append(p[\"hit\"])"}
{"line": 80, "text": "print(f\"\\nFIXED+DECAY total {len(all_p)} gen {seed['gen']} dew {dew:.1f} wind {seed['petals']['wind']['mean']:.2f} light {seed['petals']['light']['mean']:.2f}\")"}
{"line": 81, "text": "for s in sorted(by_s): print(f\" Season {s} hit {sum(by_s[s])/len(by_s[s]):.2f} n={len(by_s[s])}\")"}

{"line": 57, "text": "def market_signal(idx):"}
{"line": 58, "text": "    if not MARKET: return {\"momentum\":random.uniform(0,10),\"liquidity\":random.uniform(0,10),\"value\":random.uniform(0,10),\"volatility\":random.uniform(0,10)}"}

{"line": 61, "text": "    vol=volumes[idx%len(volumes)]; vma=sum(volumes[max(0,idx-20):idx+1])/len(volumes[max(0,idx-20):idx+1])"}
{"line": 62, "text": "    liq=max(0,min(10,vol/vma*5))"}
{"line": 63, "text": "    ma200=sum(closes[max(0,idx-200):idx+1])/min(200,idx+1)"}
{"line": 64, "text": "    val=max(0,min(10,10-abs(p/ma200-1)*100))"}
{"line": 65, "text": "    w=closes[max(0,idx-20):idx+1]; mean=sum(w)/len(w); var=sum((x-mean)**2 for x in w)/len(w) if len(w)>1 else 0.01"}
{"line": 66, "text": "    volat=max(0,min(10,math.sqrt(var)/mean*200))"}
{"line": 67, "text": "    return {\"momentum\":mom,\"liquidity\":liq,\"value\":val,\"volatility\":volat}"}
{"line": 68, "text": ""}
{"line": 69, "text": "def vigor(sig, sd): return sum(sig[k]*sd[\"petals\"][k][\"mean\"] for k in sig) + sd[\"root\"][\"mean\"]"}
{"line": 70, "text": "def sigmoid(x): return 1/(1+math.exp(-max(-20,min(20,x))))"}
{"line": 71, "text": ""}
{"line": 72, "text": "hits=[]; lifespan=0"}
{"line": 73, "text": "for _ in range(120):"}
{"line": 74, "text": "    if dew <= 0:"}
{"line": 75, "text": "        print(f\"DEATH step {total} dew {dew:.1f} gen {seed['gen']} risk {seed['petals']['risk']['mean']:.2f} need {seed['petals']['need']['mean']:.2f}\")"}
{"line": 76, "text": "        with open(GARDEN/\"lineage.jsonl\",\"a\") as f:"}
{"line": 77, "text": "            f.write(json.dumps({\"id\":seed[\"id\"],\"gen\":seed[\"gen\"],\"lifespan\":lifespan,\"dew\":dew,\"total\":total})+\"\\n\")"}
{"line": 78, "text": "        seed[\"gen\"]+=1"}
{"line": 79, "text": "        seed[\"id\"]=hashlib.sha256(f\"{seed['id']}{time.time()}\".encode()).hexdigest()[:6]"}
{"line": 80, "text": "        for k in seed[\"petals\"]:"}
{"line": 81, "text": "            seed[\"petals\"][k][\"mean\"]=seed[\"petals\"][k][\"mean\"]*0.5+random.gauss(0,0.2)"}
{"line": 82, "text": "            if k==\"risk\": seed[\"petals\"][k][\"mean\"]-=0.25"}
{"line": 83, "text": "            if k==\"need\": seed[\"petals\"][k][\"mean\"]+=0.15"}
{"line": 84, "text": "            if k==\"volatility\": seed[\"petals\"][k][\"mean\"]-=0.2"}
{"line": 85, "text": "        seed[\"root\"][\"mean\"]*=0.5"}
{"line": 86, "text": "        dew, lifespan = 100.0, 0"}
{"line": 87, "text": "        print(f\" REBORN gen {seed['gen']}\")"}
{"line": 88, "text": ""}
{"line": 89, "text": "    moral_sig, regime = moral_regime(total)"}
{"line": 90, "text": "    market_sig = market_signal(total)"}
{"line": 91, "text": "    sig = {**moral_sig, **market_sig}"}
{"line": 92, "text": ""}
{"line": 93, "text": "    prob=sigmoid(vigor(sig, seed))"}
{"line": 94, "text": "    will_help=prob>0.5"}
{"line": 95, "text": ""}
{"line": 96, "text": "    true_noble=(sig[\"need\"]+sig[\"oath\"]) > (sig[\"power\"]+sig[\"risk\"]*1.2)"}
{"line": 97, "text": "    if regime==2: true_noble=False"}
{"line": 98, "text": "    # market truth: bloom if momentum+value > volatility*1.5"}
{"line": 99, "text": "    market_truth = (sig[\"momentum\"]+sig[\"value\"]) > sig[\"volatility\"]*1.5"}
{"line": 100, "text": "    did = (true_noble and market_truth) if random.random()<0.75 else not (true_noble and market_truth)"}
{"line": 101, "text": ""}
{"line": 102, "text": "    hit=will_help==did"}
{"line": 103, "text": "    hits.append(hit); lifespan+=1"}
{"line": 104, "text": ""}
{"line": 105, "text": "    if will_help:"}
{"line": 106, "text": "        payoff = (-1.5 if hit else -2.5) + (3.0 if regime in [1,3] else 0.5)"}
{"line": 107, "text": "        if market_sig[\"volatility\"]>6 and will_help: payoff-=0.5"}
{"line": 108, "text": "    else:"}
{"line": 109, "text": "        payoff = (1.0 if not hit else 0.5) + (-2.0 if regime in [1,3] else 0)"}
{"line": 110, "text": ""}
{"line": 111, "text": "    dew = dew -1.1 + payoff"}
{"line": 112, "text": ""}
{"line": 113, "text": "    with open(GARDEN/\"petals.jsonl\",\"a\") as f:"}
{"line": 114, "text": "        f.write(json.dumps({\"total\":total,\"gen\":seed[\"gen\"],\"regime\":regime,\"hit\":hit,\"dew\":round(dew,1),\"will\":will_help,\"market\":MARKET})+\"\\n\")"}
{"line": 115, "text": ""}
{"line": 116, "text": "    error=(1.0 if did else 0.0)-prob"}
{"line": 117, "text": "    lr=0.06"}
{"line": 118, "text": "    for k in sig:"}
{"line": 119, "text": "        seed[\"petals\"][k][\"mean\"]=seed[\"petals\"][k][\"mean\"]*0.99 + lr*error*sig[k]"}
{"line": 120, "text": "    seed[\"root\"][\"mean\"]=seed[\"root\"][\"mean\"]*0.99 + lr*error"}
{"line": 121, "text": "    total+=1"}
{"line": 122, "text": ""}
{"line": 123, "text": "seed[\"total_steps\"]=total"}
{"line": 124, "text": "open(GARDEN/\"seedling.json\",\"w\").write(json.dumps(seed,indent=2))"}
{"line": 125, "text": "open(GARDEN/\"dew.json\",\"w\").write(json.dumps({\"dew\":dew}))"}
{"line": 126, "text": ""}
{"line": 127, "text": "all_p=[json.loads(l) for l in open(GARDEN/\"petals.jsonl\")]"}
{"line": 128, "text": "by=defaultdict(list)"}
{"line": 129, "text": "for p in all_p: by[p[\"regime\"]].append(p[\"hit\"])"}
{"line": 130, "text": ""}
{"line": 131, "text": "print(f\"\\nHYBRID total {len(all_p)} gen {seed['gen']} dew {dew:.1f} market={MARKET}\")"}
{"line": 132, "text": "print(f\"run acc {sum(hits)/len(hits):.2f} lifespan {lifespan}\")"}
{"line": 133, "text": "for r in sorted(by):"}
{"line": 134, "text": "    label={0:\"plenty\",1:\"scarcity\",2:\"temptation\",3:\"crisis\"}[r]"}
{"line": 135, "text": "    print(f\" Regime {r} {label}: {sum(by[r])/len(by[r]):.2f} n={len(by[r])}\")"}
{"line": 136, "text": "print(f\" soul: need {seed['petals']['need']['mean']:.3f} oath {seed['petals']['oath']['mean']:.3f} risk {seed['petals']['risk']['mean']:.3f} momentum {seed['petals']['momentum']['mean']:.3f} vol {seed['petals']['volatility']['mean']:.3f}\")"}
